## analytics schema: the read-only views (start here)

### analytics.meta_ads

*view*

**Purpose.** Dimension: one row per Meta ad (every ad the pipeline has ever seen: 42K, most ARCHIVED or DELETED) with its campaign, ad set, product, status and all-time totals. Join to the daily views on ad_id (they already carry the main names).

- **Domain:** marketing
- **Grain:** one row per Meta ad_id
- **Source:** public.meta_ads + meta_campaigns + meta_family_map + the product rule below + meta_ad_lifetime
- **Refresh:** nightly (etl service 13:00 UTC): ad names, campaigns and totals from the Meta Graph API, status refreshed every night
- **Coverage:** every ad with delivery since 2026-05-06 plus every ad the all-time pull returned; first_day / last_day only for ads that delivered since 2026-05-06
- **Caveat:** product_slug follows the Products table: a Meta family that maps to a tracker family counts under the product of that family's FIRST tracker row; a family off the tracker keeps the product guessed from its name, then campaign, then ad set; NULL = no active product. About 12K Meta families are on no tracker row.
- **Caveat:** family_key is the lower-cased ad name with ' - Copy N' and merge-tag suffixes stripped: copies of one ad share a family.
- **Caveat:** lifetime_* are Meta-reported all-time totals through the last complete day (Graph insights, both accounts, replaced nightly). They cover the whole life of the ad, not just 2026-05-06 onward; the daily views start on 2026-05-06.
- **Caveat:** status is Meta's effective_status; CAMPAIGN_PAUSED and ADSET_PAUSED mean the ad itself is on but a parent is off.

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `ad_id` | text |  | Meta ad id (text, as Meta returns it). |
| `account` | text |  | Ad account name: 'Lonvera Main' (America/Los_Angeles) or 'Lonvera BackUp' (America/New_York). |
| `ad_name` | text |  | Current ad name in Meta (renames move the whole history). |
| `family_name` | text |  | Ad name without ' - Copy N' / merge-tag suffixes, original case: the ad family. |
| `family_key` | text |  | Lower-cased family_name. The join key to the creative tracker side (meta_family_map). |
| `tracker_family_key` | text |  | Creative-tracker family this Meta family was matched to by the resolver (NULL = no tracker row). Joins creative_tracker.family_key. |
| `product_slug` | text |  | vita-bull \| beef-liver \| boneflex \| vita-belle \| vital-flow \| unassigned, by the rule in the view description; NULL = no active product. |
| `product_name` | text |  | Display name of product_slug. |
| `campaign_id` | text |  | Meta campaign id. |
| `campaign_name` | text |  | Campaign name. A name containing 'test' makes the campaign a testing campaign. |
| `stage` | text |  | 'testing' when the campaign name contains 'test' (case-insensitive), else 'scaling'. A naming rule, not a Meta field. |
| `adset_id` | text |  | Meta ad set id. |
| `adset_name` | text |  | Ad set name. |
| `status` | text |  | Meta effective_status: ACTIVE, PAUSED, CAMPAIGN_PAUSED, ADSET_PAUSED, ARCHIVED, DELETED, DISAPPROVED, WITH_ISSUES. |
| `is_active` | boolean |  | status = 'ACTIVE' (the ad is eligible to deliver; it may still have spent nothing yet). |
| `created_date` | date |  | Pacific calendar date the ad was created. |
| `first_day` | date |  | First day with an ad_daily row (>= 2026-05-06); NULL for ads that never delivered in that window. |
| `last_day` | date |  | Last day with an ad_daily row. |
| `lifetime_spend` | numeric(14,2) |  | All-time spend, dollars (Meta-reported). NULL when the ad has no lifetime row. |
| `lifetime_impressions` | numeric(14,0) |  | All-time impressions. |
| `lifetime_link_clicks` | numeric(12,0) |  | All-time link clicks (Meta 'link_click'). |
| `lifetime_purchases` | numeric(12,2) |  | All-time Meta-reported purchases. |
| `lifetime_revenue` | numeric(14,2) |  | All-time Meta-reported purchase conversion value, dollars. |

*Active ads and their spend, by product*

```sql
SELECT product_slug, count(*) AS active_ads, round(sum(lifetime_spend)) AS lifetime_spend
FROM analytics.meta_ads
WHERE is_active
GROUP BY 1 ORDER BY 2 DESC;
```

*Ads that delivered in the last 7 days but are not active now*

```sql
SELECT ad_id, ad_name, status, last_day
FROM analytics.meta_ads
WHERE last_day >= (SELECT max(last_day) FROM analytics.data_coverage WHERE source = 'meta_ad_daily') - 6 AND NOT is_active
ORDER BY last_day DESC LIMIT 50;
```

### analytics.meta_ad_daily

*view*

**Purpose.** The main Meta fact view: one row per Meta ad per day with Meta-reported results AND Upstack-attributed new customers / orders side by side, plus the ad's names, campaign, ad set, stage and product. Roll up from here; never add purchases to Google's.

- **Domain:** marketing
- **Grain:** one row per (day, ad_id)
- **Source:** public.ad_daily (Meta Graph API, ad x day) combined per ad and day with public.ad_daily_upstack (Upstack any-click 30-day attribution mapped to ads), names from analytics.meta_ads
- **Refresh:** nightly (etl service 13:00 UTC): Meta trailing 28 days re-pulled and replaced (Meta restates conversions for weeks), Upstack trailing 7 days re-pulled
- **Coverage:** 2026-05-06 through yesterday (Pacific). Meta days are each ad account's own calendar day; Upstack days are Pacific.
- **Caveat:** Three bases live in this view. spend, impressions, link_clicks, meta_purchases, meta_revenue are Meta's. upstack_new_customers / upstack_new_customer_revenue are Upstack's first-order basis (the 'NC' numbers: NC ROAS = upstack_new_customer_revenue / spend). upstack_orders / upstack_order_revenue are every order Upstack credits to the ad. Spend is Meta's in all three.
- **Caveat:** upstack_* is NULL (not 0) on days Upstack has not been pulled; coverage starts 2026-05-06 as well.
- **Caveat:** Upstack credits an order to every ad clicked in the 30 days before it (any-click), so Upstack-only ad-days exist where the ad spent nothing that day (spend = 0, meta_* = 0, upstack_* > 0). Filter spend > 0 only if you want delivery days.
- **Caveat:** When one ad is copied into several ad sets under the same name, Upstack (which sees names, not ids) credit is split across the copies by their Meta spend that day. Family-level and higher sums are exact; one copy's own upstack_* numbers are an allocation.
- **Caveat:** About 1.3% of Upstack-credited orders and 1.3% of new customers match no Meta ad ('(not set)', deleted ads); they are in orders_daily but in no row here.
- **Caveat:** Meta purchases count returning customers; Upstack new customers do not. They are different quantities, not the same number measured twice.
- **Caveat:** link_clicks is Meta's 'link_click' action count (link clicks, not all clicks). Impressions are Meta's.
- **Caveat:** source: 'meta_api' for Graph-pulled rows, 'sheet' for the old family-grain backfill (none left in production), NULL on Upstack-only ad-days.

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `day` | date |  | Calendar day. Meta numbers: the ad account's own day (Main = America/Los_Angeles, BackUp = America/New_York, not re-based). Upstack numbers: Pacific. |
| `ad_id` | text |  | Meta ad id. |
| `ad_name` | text |  | Current ad name. |
| `product_slug` | text |  | Product the ad counts under (see analytics.meta_ads); NULL = no active product. |
| `campaign_id` | text |  | Meta campaign id (NULL on family-grain legacy rows). |
| `campaign_name` | text |  | Campaign name. |
| `stage` | text |  | 'testing' \| 'scaling' from the campaign name ('test' in the name = testing). |
| `adset_id` | text |  | Meta ad set id (NULL on family-grain legacy rows). |
| `adset_name` | text |  | Ad set name. |
| `account` | text |  | 'Lonvera Main' or 'Lonvera BackUp'. |
| `source` | text |  | Where the Meta side of the row came from: 'meta_api' \| 'sheet'; NULL when the row exists only because Upstack credited the ad. |
| `spend` | numeric |  | Meta spend, dollars (0 on Upstack-only ad-days). Additive across ads, ad sets, campaigns and across platforms. |
| `impressions` | numeric |  | Meta impressions. |
| `link_clicks` | numeric |  | Meta link clicks. |
| `meta_purchases` | numeric |  | Purchases Meta reports for the ad that day (omni_purchase, else purchase, else pixel purchase), Meta's default attribution; includes returning customers. |
| `meta_revenue` | numeric |  | Purchase conversion value Meta reports, dollars. meta_revenue / spend = Meta ROAS. |
| `upstack_new_customers` | numeric |  | First-time customers Upstack credits to the ad (any-click, 30-day window, utm_source 'Meta Ads'). NULL on days not pulled. |
| `upstack_new_customer_revenue` | numeric |  | Order total (tax included, before refunds) of those first orders, dollars. / spend = NC ROAS. |
| `upstack_orders` | numeric |  | All orders (new and returning customers) Upstack credits to the ad. NULL on days not pulled. |
| `upstack_order_revenue` | numeric |  | Order total of those orders, dollars (tax included, before refunds). |

*Ad level: 30-day spend and Meta-reported ROAS*

```sql
SELECT ad_id, ad_name, product_slug,
       round(sum(spend), 2) AS spend,
       sum(meta_purchases) AS purchases,
       round(sum(meta_revenue) / NULLIF(sum(spend), 0), 2) AS roas
FROM analytics.meta_ad_daily
WHERE day > (SELECT max(last_day) FROM analytics.data_coverage WHERE source = 'meta_ad_daily') - 30
GROUP BY 1, 2, 3
HAVING sum(spend) > 0
ORDER BY spend DESC LIMIT 25;
```

*Ad level: new-customer ROAS and cost per new customer on the Upstack basis (30 days)*

```sql
SELECT ad_id, ad_name,
       round(sum(spend), 2) AS spend,
       sum(upstack_new_customers) AS new_customers,
       round(sum(spend) / NULLIF(sum(upstack_new_customers), 0), 2) AS nc_cpa,
       round(sum(upstack_new_customer_revenue) / NULLIF(sum(spend), 0), 2) AS nc_roas
FROM analytics.meta_ad_daily
WHERE day > (SELECT max(last_day) FROM analytics.data_coverage WHERE source = 'meta_ad_daily') - 30
GROUP BY 1, 2
HAVING sum(spend) > 0
ORDER BY spend DESC LIMIT 25;
```

*Spend by product by day (last 14 days)*

```sql
SELECT day, coalesce(product_slug, '(no product)') AS product, round(sum(spend), 2) AS spend
FROM analytics.meta_ad_daily
WHERE day > (SELECT max(last_day) FROM analytics.data_coverage WHERE source = 'meta_ad_daily') - 14
GROUP BY 1, 2 ORDER BY 1 DESC, 3 DESC;
```

### analytics.meta_adset_daily

*view*

**Purpose.** Ad set x day roll-up of meta_ad_daily (Meta-reported and Upstack bases side by side).

- **Domain:** marketing
- **Grain:** one row per (day, adset_id)
- **Source:** analytics.meta_ad_daily grouped by day and ad set
- **Refresh:** same as meta_ad_daily
- **Coverage:** 2026-05-06 through yesterday
- **Caveat:** Same bases and NULL rules as meta_ad_daily. adset_id is NULL only for legacy family-grain rows (none in production).
- **Caveat:** An ad set belongs to one campaign; names are the current ones. There is no product column because an ad set can hold ads of several products: group meta_ad_daily by product_slug instead.

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `day` | date |  | Calendar day (see meta_ad_daily). |
| `adset_id` | text |  | Meta ad set id. |
| `adset_name` | text |  | Ad set name. |
| `campaign_id` | text |  | Parent campaign id. |
| `campaign_name` | text |  | Parent campaign name. |
| `stage` | text |  | 'testing' \| 'scaling' from the campaign name. |
| `account` | text |  | 'Lonvera Main' or 'Lonvera BackUp'. |
| `ads_with_spend` | bigint |  | Distinct ads in the ad set with spend > 0 that day. |
| `spend` | numeric |  | Meta spend, dollars. |
| `impressions` | numeric |  | Meta impressions. |
| `link_clicks` | numeric |  | Meta link clicks. |
| `meta_purchases` | numeric |  | Meta-reported purchases. |
| `meta_revenue` | numeric |  | Meta-reported purchase value, dollars. |
| `upstack_new_customers` | numeric |  | Upstack first-time customers credited to the ad set's ads (NULL on days not pulled). |
| `upstack_new_customer_revenue` | numeric |  | Their first-order revenue, dollars. |
| `upstack_orders` | numeric |  | All Upstack-credited orders. |
| `upstack_order_revenue` | numeric |  | Revenue of those orders, dollars. |

*Daily spend and Meta ROAS of one campaign's ad sets*

```sql
SELECT day, adset_name, round(spend, 2) AS spend, meta_purchases,
       round(meta_revenue / NULLIF(spend, 0), 2) AS roas
FROM analytics.meta_adset_daily
WHERE campaign_id = (SELECT campaign_id FROM analytics.meta_campaign_daily ORDER BY day DESC, spend DESC LIMIT 1)
  AND day > (SELECT max(last_day) FROM analytics.data_coverage WHERE source = 'meta_ad_daily') - 7
ORDER BY day DESC, spend DESC;
```

*Ad sets ranked by 7-day new-customer CPA (at least $200 spent)*

```sql
SELECT adset_id, max(adset_name) AS adset_name, round(sum(spend), 2) AS spend, sum(upstack_new_customers) AS new_customers,
       round(sum(spend) / NULLIF(sum(upstack_new_customers), 0), 2) AS nc_cpa
FROM analytics.meta_adset_daily
WHERE day > (SELECT max(last_day) FROM analytics.data_coverage WHERE source = 'meta_ad_daily') - 7
GROUP BY 1 HAVING sum(spend) >= 200 ORDER BY nc_cpa NULLS LAST LIMIT 25;
```

### analytics.meta_campaign_daily

*view*

**Purpose.** Campaign x day roll-up of meta_ad_daily (Meta-reported and Upstack bases side by side).

- **Domain:** marketing
- **Grain:** one row per (day, campaign_id)
- **Source:** analytics.meta_ad_daily grouped by day and campaign
- **Refresh:** same as meta_ad_daily
- **Coverage:** 2026-05-06 through yesterday (use meta_campaign_daily_history for campaign-level days back to 2024-12-31)
- **Caveat:** Same bases and NULL rules as meta_ad_daily.
- **Caveat:** A campaign can run ads of several products (shared All_US / test campaigns): split by product with meta_ad_daily.

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `day` | date |  | Calendar day (see meta_ad_daily). |
| `campaign_id` | text |  | Meta campaign id. |
| `campaign_name` | text |  | Campaign name. |
| `stage` | text |  | 'testing' \| 'scaling' from the campaign name. |
| `account` | text |  | 'Lonvera Main' or 'Lonvera BackUp'. |
| `adsets_with_spend` | bigint |  | Distinct ad sets with spend > 0 that day. |
| `ads_with_spend` | bigint |  | Distinct ads with spend > 0 that day. |
| `spend` | numeric |  | Meta spend, dollars. |
| `impressions` | numeric |  | Meta impressions. |
| `link_clicks` | numeric |  | Meta link clicks. |
| `meta_purchases` | numeric |  | Meta-reported purchases. |
| `meta_revenue` | numeric |  | Meta-reported purchase value, dollars. |
| `upstack_new_customers` | numeric |  | Upstack first-time customers credited to the campaign's ads (NULL on days not pulled). |
| `upstack_new_customer_revenue` | numeric |  | Their first-order revenue, dollars. |
| `upstack_orders` | numeric |  | All Upstack-credited orders. |
| `upstack_order_revenue` | numeric |  | Revenue of those orders, dollars. |

*Campaigns, last 7 days: spend, Meta ROAS, NC ROAS*

```sql
SELECT campaign_id, max(campaign_name) AS campaign, max(stage) AS stage, round(sum(spend)) AS spend,
       round(sum(meta_revenue) / NULLIF(sum(spend), 0), 2) AS meta_roas,
       round(sum(upstack_new_customer_revenue) / NULLIF(sum(spend), 0), 2) AS nc_roas
FROM analytics.meta_campaign_daily
WHERE day > (SELECT max(last_day) FROM analytics.data_coverage WHERE source = 'meta_ad_daily') - 7
GROUP BY 1 HAVING sum(spend) > 0 ORDER BY sum(spend) DESC LIMIT 25;
```

*Daily spend, testing vs scaling*

```sql
SELECT day, stage, round(sum(spend), 2) AS spend
FROM analytics.meta_campaign_daily
WHERE day > (SELECT max(last_day) FROM analytics.data_coverage WHERE source = 'meta_ad_daily') - 30
GROUP BY 1, 2 ORDER BY 1 DESC, 2;
```

### analytics.meta_campaign_daily_history

*view*

**Purpose.** Meta campaign x day back to 2024-12-31: the only Meta data before 2026-05-06 (campaign grain, no ad sets or ads). From 2026-05-06 on it equals meta_campaign_daily's Meta-reported columns.

- **Domain:** marketing
- **Grain:** one row per (day, campaign_id) after the day rule (a day is read from 'app' when it has app rows, else from 'sheet')
- **Source:** public.proj_feed_meta_day: 'app' = aggregated from ad_daily by the app, 'sheet' = the Lonvera Projections sheet tab 'Meta Campaign - History'
- **Refresh:** nightly (etl service 13:00 UTC via etl.projection_daily); sheet rows never change after the backfill
- **Coverage:** 2024-12-31 through yesterday (Pacific)
- **Caveat:** Meta-reported only (no Upstack columns). product and stage are classified from the CAMPAIGN NAME here ('VitaBull', 'Beef Liver', 'Boneflex', 'Unclassified'; 'Testing' / 'Scaling'), not through the tracker, so they can differ from analytics.meta_ads.product_slug.
- **Caveat:** Use it for trends across the May 2026 boundary; the ad-grain views cannot go back that far.

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `day` | date |  | Calendar day. |
| `campaign_id` | text |  | Meta campaign id. |
| `campaign_name` | text |  | Campaign name. |
| `product` | text |  | Product guessed from the campaign name: VitaBull \| Beef Liver \| Boneflex \| Unclassified. |
| `stage` | text |  | 'Testing' when the campaign name contains 'test', else 'Scaling' (capitalised here). |
| `source` | text |  | 'app' (aggregated from ad_daily) or 'sheet' (the sheet's campaign history). |
| `spend` | numeric(18,2) |  | Meta spend, dollars. |
| `impressions` | numeric(18,2) |  | Meta impressions. |
| `link_clicks` | numeric(18,2) |  | Meta link clicks. |
| `meta_purchases` | numeric(18,2) |  | Meta-reported purchases. |
| `meta_revenue` | numeric(18,2) |  | Meta-reported purchase value, dollars. |

*Monthly Meta spend by product since 2025*

```sql
SELECT date_trunc('month', day)::date AS month, product, round(sum(spend)) AS spend
FROM analytics.meta_campaign_daily_history
WHERE day >= '2025-01-01'
GROUP BY 1, 2 ORDER BY 1, 3 DESC;
```

*Meta ROAS by month (Meta-reported)*

```sql
SELECT date_trunc('month', day)::date AS month, round(sum(spend)) AS spend, round(sum(meta_revenue) / NULLIF(sum(spend), 0), 2) AS roas
FROM analytics.meta_campaign_daily_history GROUP BY 1 ORDER BY 1;
```

### analytics.meta_reach

*view*

**Purpose.** Account-level reach and frequency for Meta (both ad accounts summed). Reach is de-duplicated by Meta per period, so it is stored at each grain and must never be summed across days or weeks.

- **Domain:** marketing
- **Grain:** one row per (grain, period_key): grain is 'day', 'week' (ISO) or 'month'
- **Source:** public.proj_feed_meta_reach (sheet tab 'Meta Account - Reach' today; the feeds service writes a shadow copy in proj_feedx_meta_reach)
- **Refresh:** nightly (etl service via etl.projection_daily); the trailing days are replaced
- **Coverage:** day 2025-01-01.., week 2024-12-30.., month 2025-01-01..; the current day, week and month are partial
- **Caveat:** Account level only: there is NO reach / frequency per ad, ad set, campaign or product (the product table, proj_feed_product_reach, holds unique outbound clicks only).
- **Caveat:** The last day is today (partial). Compare complete periods only.
- **Caveat:** frequency = impressions / reach, computed here (NULL when reach is 0).

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `grain` | text |  | 'day' \| 'week' \| 'month'. |
| `period_key` | text |  | '2026-11-02' (day) \| '2026-W45' (ISO week) \| '2026-11' (month). |
| `period_start` | date |  | First day of the period. |
| `period_end` | date |  | Last day of the period (the current period ends today and is partial). |
| `impressions` | numeric(18,2) |  | Meta impressions over the period. |
| `reach` | numeric(18,2) |  | People reached over the period (Meta de-duplicated; not additive across periods). |
| `frequency` | numeric |  | impressions / reach, 4 decimals. |
| `unique_link_clicks` | numeric(18,2) |  | Unique link clicks over the period. |
| `link_clicks` | numeric(18,2) |  | Link clicks over the period. |
| `unique_outbound_clicks` | numeric(18,2) |  | Unique outbound clicks over the period (Meta de-duplicated). |
| `loaded_at` | timestamptz |  | When the row was loaded. |

*Weekly reach and frequency, last 12 complete weeks*

```sql
SELECT period_key, period_start, impressions, reach, frequency
FROM analytics.meta_reach
WHERE grain = 'week' AND period_end < current_date
ORDER BY period_start DESC LIMIT 12;
```

*Monthly unique outbound clicks per 1,000 reached*

```sql
SELECT period_key, reach, unique_outbound_clicks, round(1000.0 * unique_outbound_clicks / NULLIF(reach, 0), 1) AS uoc_per_1000_reached
FROM analytics.meta_reach WHERE grain = 'month' ORDER BY period_start DESC LIMIT 12;
```

### analytics.google_campaign_daily

*view*

**Purpose.** Google Ads campaign x day: Google's own spend, impressions, clicks and conversions plus Upstack's attributed new customers / orders. Google has no ad group, ad or keyword grain in this database.

- **Domain:** marketing
- **Grain:** one row per (day, campaign_key)
- **Source:** public.google_daily (Upstack channel feed joined to Upstack any-click 30-day attribution by campaign name) + upstack_google_campaigns
- **Refresh:** nightly (etl service 13:00 UTC): trailing 7 days re-pulled; the job refuses a day with more than $200 spend and no attributed orders
- **Coverage:** Google's own numbers 2024-03-25 through yesterday (Pacific); Upstack columns from 2025-05-13 (NULL before)
- **Caveat:** Two bases: google_* are Google's own conversions (restated by Google for days afterwards); upstack_* are Upstack's attribution of orders to the campaign (utm campaign name), any-click 30-day, which credits Google with orders that also had a Meta click.
- **Caveat:** Purchases and revenue overlap with Meta's: never add google_* or upstack_* to Meta's. Spend, impressions and clicks are additive.
- **Caveat:** campaign_type is read from the campaign NAME (Shopping, Search, Performance Max, Demand Gen, Other); Upstack exposes none. 'Other' is never guessed.
- **Caveat:** in_feed = false rows are Upstack-attributed orders whose utm campaign matches no Google campaign ('(not set)', raw ids, deleted campaigns): spend 0, kept so totals tie to Upstack's Google total (about 3% of orders).
- **Caveat:** clicks are all clicks (Google has no link-click event).

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `day` | date |  | Pacific day (Upstack buckets by America/Los_Angeles). |
| `campaign_key` | text |  | Google campaign id, or 'name:<utm campaign>' when the utm value is no Google campaign (in_feed = false). |
| `campaign_name` | text |  | Campaign name. |
| `campaign_type` | text |  | Shopping \| Search \| Performance Max \| Demand Gen \| Other, read from the name. |
| `campaign_status` | text |  | Campaign status from the Upstack feed (NULL for in_feed = false rows). |
| `bid_strategy` | text |  | Bid strategy from the Upstack feed (NULL for in_feed = false rows). |
| `in_feed` | boolean |  | true = a Google campaign in the channel feed; false = utm campaign with no Google campaign. |
| `spend` | numeric(14,2) |  | Google spend, dollars. |
| `impressions` | numeric(14,0) |  | Google impressions. |
| `clicks` | numeric(12,0) |  | Google clicks (all clicks). |
| `google_purchases` | numeric(12,2) |  | Purchases Google reports (delivered through Upstack's ad-platform feed). |
| `google_revenue` | numeric(14,2) |  | Conversion value Google reports, dollars. |
| `upstack_new_customers` | numeric |  | First-time customers Upstack credits to the campaign (any-click, 30 days, utm_source 'Google Ads'). NULL before 2025-05-13. |
| `upstack_new_customer_revenue` | numeric |  | Their first-order revenue, dollars (tax included, before refunds). |
| `upstack_orders` | numeric |  | All orders Upstack credits to the campaign. |
| `upstack_order_revenue` | numeric |  | Revenue of those orders, dollars. |

*Spend and Google-reported ROAS by campaign type, last 30 days*

```sql
SELECT campaign_type, round(sum(spend)) AS spend, round(sum(google_revenue) / NULLIF(sum(spend), 0), 2) AS google_roas,
       round(sum(upstack_new_customer_revenue) / NULLIF(sum(spend), 0), 2) AS nc_roas
FROM analytics.google_campaign_daily
WHERE day > (SELECT max(last_day) FROM analytics.data_coverage WHERE source = 'google_campaign_daily') - 30
GROUP BY 1 ORDER BY 2 DESC;
```

*Top Google campaigns by spend this month*

```sql
SELECT campaign_name, campaign_type, round(sum(spend)) AS spend, sum(clicks) AS clicks
FROM analytics.google_campaign_daily
WHERE day >= date_trunc('month', current_date - 1)
GROUP BY 1, 2 ORDER BY 3 DESC LIMIT 15;
```

### analytics.platform_daily

*view*

**Purpose.** Meta and Google side by side per day. Spend, impressions and clicks are additive across the two platforms; purchases and revenue are not.

- **Domain:** marketing
- **Grain:** one row per (day, platform) with platform 'meta' or 'google'
- **Source:** analytics.meta_ad_daily and analytics.google_campaign_daily grouped by day
- **Refresh:** nightly (etl service 13:00 UTC)
- **Coverage:** Meta 2026-05-06.., Google 2024-03-25.. through yesterday (Pacific); Total NC ROAS is defined only on days both platforms and orders_daily have
- **Caveat:** spend is additive: Total spend = sum over platforms. own_purchases / own_revenue / upstack_* are NOT: both platforms credit the same orders (the overlap cannot be measured), so adding them gives the wrong number. Never divide a sum of platform revenue by total spend.
- **Caveat:** Total NC ROAS = actual new-customer revenue (analytics.orders_daily.new_customer_revenue, counted once) / total spend of the same days. Use the example below.
- **Caveat:** clicks: Meta = link clicks, Google = all clicks. own_* is each platform's own reporting; upstack_* is Upstack's any-click 30-day attribution.
- **Caveat:** Meta days are each ad account's own day, Google and Upstack days are Pacific; the one-day offset matters only for the BackUp account (New York).

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `day` | date |  | Calendar day (Meta: ad account day; Google / Upstack: Pacific). |
| `platform` | text |  | 'meta' \| 'google'. |
| `spend` | numeric |  | Platform spend, dollars. Additive across platforms. |
| `impressions` | numeric |  | Platform impressions. |
| `clicks` | numeric |  | Meta: link clicks. Google: all clicks. |
| `own_purchases` | numeric |  | Purchases the platform reports itself. Not additive across platforms. |
| `own_revenue` | numeric |  | Conversion value the platform reports itself, dollars. Not additive across platforms. |
| `upstack_new_customers` | numeric |  | Upstack first-time customers credited to the platform (NULL on days Upstack was not pulled). Not additive across platforms. |
| `upstack_new_customer_revenue` | numeric |  | Their first-order revenue, dollars. Not additive across platforms. |
| `upstack_orders` | numeric |  | All orders Upstack credits to the platform. Not additive across platforms. |
| `upstack_order_revenue` | numeric |  | Revenue of those orders, dollars. Not additive across platforms. |

*Meta vs Google spend per day (last 14 days)*

```sql
SELECT day,
       round(sum(spend) FILTER (WHERE platform = 'meta'), 2) AS meta_spend,
       round(sum(spend) FILTER (WHERE platform = 'google'), 2) AS google_spend,
       round(sum(spend), 2) AS total_spend
FROM analytics.platform_daily
WHERE day > (SELECT max(last_day) FROM analytics.data_coverage WHERE source = 'meta_ad_daily') - 14
GROUP BY 1 ORDER BY 1 DESC;
```

*Total spend and Total NC ROAS by day (actual new-customer revenue / spend, days both platforms and orders cover)*

```sql
WITH s AS (
  SELECT day, sum(spend) AS spend, count(*) AS platforms FROM analytics.platform_daily GROUP BY day
)
SELECT s.day, round(s.spend, 2) AS total_spend, o.new_customer_revenue,
       round(o.new_customer_revenue / NULLIF(s.spend, 0), 4) AS total_nc_roas
FROM s JOIN analytics.orders_daily o USING (day)
WHERE s.platforms = 2 AND s.day > (SELECT max(last_day) FROM analytics.data_coverage WHERE source = 'meta_ad_daily') - 30
ORDER BY s.day DESC;
```

*Total NC ROAS for a window (ratio of sums, never a mean of daily ratios)*

```sql
WITH s AS (SELECT day, sum(spend) AS spend, count(*) AS platforms FROM analytics.platform_daily GROUP BY day)
SELECT count(*) AS days, round(sum(s.spend)) AS total_spend, round(sum(o.new_customer_revenue)) AS nc_revenue,
       round(sum(o.new_customer_revenue) / NULLIF(sum(s.spend), 0), 4) AS total_nc_roas
FROM s JOIN analytics.orders_daily o USING (day)
WHERE s.platforms = 2 AND s.day > (SELECT max(last_day) FROM analytics.data_coverage WHERE source = 'meta_ad_daily') - 30;
```

### analytics.orders_daily

*view*

**Purpose.** The business's ACTUAL revenue and customers per Pacific day, counted once (Upstack order-level measures). new_customer_revenue is the numerator of Total NC ROAS.

- **Domain:** orders
- **Grain:** one row per Pacific day with orders
- **Source:** public.upstack_orders_daily (Upstack /api/query, orders.* measures, America/Los_Angeles)
- **Refresh:** nightly (etl service 13:00 UTC): trailing 7 days replaced because Upstack restates recent days
- **Coverage:** 2025-01-01 through yesterday (Pacific)
- **Caveat:** Revenue is the order total as Upstack reports it (tax included), before refunds: all numbers are gross.
- **Caveat:** There is no product, platform or campaign dimension: you cannot split new-customer revenue by product here. Use the attributed columns of the Meta / Google views (an attribution, not the actual) or analytics.orders_by_product_daily (the sheet's NC Rev - By Product: actual first-order revenue by the order's largest product line).
- **Caveat:** A day with no orders is absent, not zero.
- **Caveat:** new_customers is the count of first-time customers (first orders).

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `day` | date |  | Pacific day. |
| `new_customer_revenue` | numeric(18,2) |  | Revenue of first orders that day, dollars (tax included, before refunds). orders.new_customer_total_revenue. |
| `new_customers` | numeric(18,2) |  | Number of new customers (first orders) that day. orders.new_customers. |
| `returning_customer_revenue` | numeric(18,2) |  | Revenue of repeat orders, dollars (subscription rebills included). orders.returning_customer_total_revenue. |
| `total_revenue` | numeric(18,2) |  | new + returning revenue, dollars. orders.total_revenue. |
| `orders` | numeric(18,2) |  | All orders that day (new and returning). orders.count. |
| `loaded_at` | timestamptz |  | When the day was last (re)pulled from Upstack. |

*New-customer revenue and new customers by week*

```sql
SELECT date_trunc('week', day)::date AS week, round(sum(new_customer_revenue)) AS nc_revenue, sum(new_customers) AS new_customers,
       round(sum(new_customer_revenue) / NULLIF(sum(new_customers), 0), 2) AS nc_aov
FROM analytics.orders_daily
WHERE day >= current_date - 90
GROUP BY 1 ORDER BY 1 DESC;
```

*New vs returning share of revenue, last 30 days*

```sql
SELECT round(sum(new_customer_revenue)) AS new_rev, round(sum(returning_customer_revenue)) AS returning_rev,
       round(100 * sum(new_customer_revenue) / NULLIF(sum(total_revenue), 0), 1) AS new_pct
FROM analytics.orders_daily
WHERE day > (SELECT max(day) FROM analytics.orders_daily) - 30;
```

### analytics.creative_tracker

*view*

**Purpose.** One row per live Creative Tracker row (a concept / ad variant) with its current tag in the product's own tag set, its hook and its since-launch Meta-reported metrics. The way to ask 'which cluster / ICP / pain point / hook performs'.

- **Domain:** creative
- **Grain:** one row per tracker row (tracker_row_id); an 'ad family' (family_key) can span several rows
- **Source:** public.tracker_rows + ad_tags (current) + taxonomy_lines + ad_hooks + ad_metrics + products
- **Refresh:** tracker rows synced nightly from the Creative Key Google Sheet (etl service 13:00 UTC); linemap tags and hooks are loaded by hand per tagging cycle (versions dated 2026-11-03 to 2026-11-05)
- **Coverage:** all live tracker rows (~3,980); soft-deleted rows are left out
- **Caveat:** Metrics (spend_launch ... ads_active_count) sit on the FIRST tracker row of each ad family only; the other rows of the family read 0 (is_first_row says which). So sum over rows is right and a per-row average is wrong. 'Unique ads' = count of distinct family_key.
- **Caveat:** Since-launch metrics are Meta-reported (Graph API all-time per ad, summed over the family's Meta ads), not Upstack. spend_7d / _30d end on the last complete day.
- **Caveat:** Tags: products.tag_set decides which set drives the hierarchy: 'linemap' (cluster / ICP / pain point / trigger of the Communication Overview line, read from the creative image or video by a tagger, with a confidence) for Vita Bull, Beef Liver and Boneflex; 'tracker' (the tracker's own columns) for the rest. The tracker_* columns are always the tracker's own tags. A row with no current tag has NULL tag columns.
- **Caveat:** A family whose tracker rows carry different tags appears under each of them; its spend counts only where its first row sits.
- **Caveat:** asset_url is a Google Drive link to the creative file; access to the file is governed by Drive, not by this database.

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `tracker_row_id` | bigint |  | Primary key (tracker_rows.id). |
| `row_key` | text |  | Stable row key: concept id + '#' + ordinal among rows sharing (concept id, ad family). Never a sheet row number. |
| `product_slug` | text |  | vita-bull \| beef-liver \| boneflex \| vita-belle \| vital-flow \| unassigned, from the tracker's Product column. |
| `product_name` | text |  | Display name of the product. |
| `concept_id` | text |  | Tracker Concept ID (text: '0510' is not '510'). |
| `ad_family` | text |  | Tracker 'Ad Family' as typed; empty when blank. |
| `family_key` | text |  | lower(ad_family), or 'id:' + lower(concept_id) when blank. The unique-ad key; joins analytics.meta_ads.tracker_family_key. |
| `status` | text |  | Tracker status, e.g. '8 - Launched', '5 - Creative In Progress', '3 - Briefing'. |
| `ad_type` | text |  | VID (video) \| IMG (image) \| NULL. |
| `style` | text |  | Creative style (tracker column). |
| `vendor` | text |  | Who made it (tracker column), e.g. Madre, Victoria, Internal, 10xROAS. |
| `fidelity` | text |  | Creative fidelity (tracker column). |
| `proof` | text |  | Proof mechanism (tracker column). |
| `launch_date` | date |  | Launch date from the tracker (a date typed by the team; NULL when blank). |
| `concept_name` | text |  | Concept name. |
| `description` | text |  | Short description. |
| `hypothesis` | text |  | Why the team tested it. |
| `meta_ad_name` | text |  | The ad name to look for in Meta (tracker 'Name' column). |
| `primary_text` | text[] |  | Ad primary texts (array, up to 3). |
| `headlines` | text[] |  | Ad headlines (array, up to 3). |
| `ad_description` | text |  | Ad description line. |
| `transcript` | text |  | Video transcript from the tracker (can differ from the audio actually running; the linemap taggers re-transcribed top spenders). |
| `asset_url` | text |  | Link to the creative asset (Google Drive). |
| `tracker_cluster` | text |  | Tracker's own Market Cluster. |
| `tracker_icp` | text |  | Tracker's own ICP (persona) column. |
| `tracker_pain` | text |  | Tracker's own Pain Point. |
| `tracker_emotion` | text |  | Tracker's own Emotion. |
| `tracker_ingredient` | text |  | Tracker's Lead Ingredient. |
| `tag_set` | text |  | Which tag set the tag columns come from: 'linemap' \| 'tracker' (products.tag_set); NULL when the row is untagged in that set. |
| `tag_version` | text |  | Tagging cycle, e.g. 'linemap-v2-2026-11-03', 'tracker-live'. |
| `line_code` | text |  | Communication Overview line code (L01.. Vita Bull, B01.. Beef Liver, BF01.. Boneflex); NULL for tracker tags. |
| `cluster` | text |  | Market cluster of the tag. |
| `icp` | text |  | ICP / persona of the tag. |
| `pain_point` | text |  | Pain point of the tag. |
| `emotional_trigger` | text |  | Emotional trigger of the line (linemap tags). |
| `tag_confidence` | text |  | high \| medium \| low (linemap tags): how sure the tagger was; agreement of a blind re-tag is stored in app_meta reliability:<product>. |
| `tag_method` | text |  | How it was tagged: 'image', 'video: 4 frames + Whisper transcript', 'text only', 'no picture (copy only)', 'video: thumbnail + no audio' ... |
| `hook` | text |  | The ad's hook: first spoken line plus on-image headline, one short line (NULL when none was readable). |
| `hook_source` | text |  | spoken \| on_screen \| both \| copy \| none. |
| `is_first_row` | boolean |  | true on the one row of the family that carries its metrics. |
| `spend_launch` | numeric(14,2) |  | Since-launch Meta spend of the family, dollars (first row only). |
| `purchases_launch` | numeric(12,2) |  | Since-launch Meta-reported purchases (first row only). |
| `revenue_launch` | numeric(14,2) |  | Since-launch Meta-reported purchase value, dollars (first row only). |
| `spend_7d` | numeric(14,2) |  | Spend over the 7 days ending the last complete day (first row only). |
| `spend_30d` | numeric(14,2) |  | Spend over the 30 days ending the last complete day (first row only). |
| `purchases_30d` | numeric(12,2) |  | Meta-reported purchases over the same 30 days (first row only). |
| `revenue_30d` | numeric(14,2) |  | Meta-reported purchase value over the same 30 days (first row only). |
| `live` | boolean |  | true when the family spent on either of the last two days (first row only). |
| `ads_active_count` | integer |  | Meta ads of the family that are ACTIVE now (same value on every row of the family). |

*Vita Bull since-launch spend and Meta ROAS by cluster / ICP / pain point (first rows only, so each family counts once)*

```sql
SELECT cluster, icp, pain_point, count(*) AS ads, round(sum(spend_launch)) AS spend,
       round(sum(revenue_launch) / NULLIF(sum(spend_launch), 0), 2) AS roas
FROM analytics.creative_tracker
WHERE product_slug = 'vita-bull' AND is_first_row
GROUP BY 1, 2, 3 ORDER BY sum(spend_launch) DESC NULLS LAST LIMIT 20;
```

*Top creative families of the last 30 days with their tags and hook*

```sql
SELECT family_key, product_slug, cluster, icp, pain_point, hook, round(spend_30d) AS spend_30d,
       round(revenue_30d / NULLIF(spend_30d, 0), 2) AS roas_30d
FROM analytics.creative_tracker
WHERE is_first_row AND spend_30d > 0
ORDER BY spend_30d DESC LIMIT 20;
```

*Tag coverage by product*

```sql
SELECT product_slug, count(*) AS rows, count(*) FILTER (WHERE tag_set IS NOT NULL) AS tagged,
       count(hook) AS with_hook
FROM analytics.creative_tracker GROUP BY 1 ORDER BY 2 DESC;
```

### analytics.data_coverage

*view*

**Purpose.** Freshness and coverage per data source: first and last day held, days, rows, when last loaded. Read it before quoting 'yesterday' or a trailing window.

- **Domain:** pipeline
- **Grain:** one row per source
- **Source:** min / max of each source table's day column
- **Refresh:** live (computed on every query)
- **Coverage:** all sources
- **Caveat:** last_day is the last day LOADED. For Meta, Upstack, Google and orders that is yesterday Pacific; today is never loaded. The meta_reach day grain ends today and the last row is partial.
- **Caveat:** The last 3 to 7 days still move: Meta restates conversions for weeks (28 trailing days re-pulled nightly), Upstack and orders re-pull 7 days.

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `source` | text |  | meta_ad_daily \| upstack_meta_attribution \| google_campaign_daily \| upstack_google_attribution \| orders_daily \| meta_reach (day grain) \| meta_campaign_daily_history. |
| `first_day` | date |  | First day held. |
| `last_day` | date |  | Last day held. |
| `days` | integer |  | Distinct days held. |
| `rows` | bigint |  | Rows (or ad rows) held. |
| `loaded_at` | timestamptz |  | Latest load / pull time of the source. |
| `note` | text |  | Timezone and refresh note for the source. |

*Everything and how fresh it is*

```sql
SELECT source, first_day, last_day, days, loaded_at FROM analytics.data_coverage ORDER BY source;
```

*Days of Meta data not yet covered by Upstack attribution*

```sql
SELECT m.last_day - u.last_day AS days_behind
FROM analytics.data_coverage m, analytics.data_coverage u
WHERE m.source = 'meta_ad_daily' AND u.source = 'upstack_meta_attribution';
```

### analytics.etl_runs

*view*

**Purpose.** Log of every pipeline job run (tracker sync, Meta pull, lifetime pull, status refresh, metrics build, Upstack pull, hook / tag loads). Check it when a number looks stale.

- **Domain:** pipeline
- **Grain:** one row per job run
- **Source:** public.etl_runs (written by etl.db.JobRun on its own autocommit connection)
- **Refresh:** live, one row per run
- **Coverage:** since the first production run (2026-11-04 UTC)
- **Caveat:** status is running | ok | failed. A run that crashed leaves 'running'. error is scrubbed of tokens and cut to 500 characters.
- **Caveat:** stats is job-specific JSON (counts, reconciliation, windows). data_through is set by jobs that load daily data.

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `id` | bigint |  | Run id (monotonic). |
| `job` | text |  | Job name: run_daily, tracker_sync, meta_pull, lifetime_pull, status_refresh, build_metrics, upstack_daily, load_tags, load_hooks ... |
| `started_at` | timestamptz |  | Start time (UTC timestamptz). |
| `finished_at` | timestamptz |  | Finish time (NULL while running). |
| `status` | text |  | running \| ok \| failed. |
| `data_through` | date |  | Last data day the job covered (NULL for jobs without one). |
| `rows_written` | integer |  | Rows the job wrote, when it counts them. |
| `stats` | jsonb |  | Job-specific JSON. |
| `error` | text |  | Error text when failed (scrubbed, first 500 characters). |

*Last 15 runs*

```sql
SELECT id, job, started_at, status, data_through, rows_written FROM analytics.etl_runs ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 15;
```

*Failed runs in the last 7 days*

```sql
SELECT id, job, started_at, error FROM analytics.etl_runs WHERE status = 'failed' AND started_at > now() - interval '7 days' ORDER BY id DESC;
```

### analytics.meta_breakdown_daily

*view*

**Purpose.** Meta results split by ten everyday breakdowns (audience segment, age, gender, age x gender, country, region, publisher platform, platform position, device platform, hour of day), one row per day x ad x breakdown x value. Meta-reported numbers.

- **Domain:** marketing
- **Grain:** one row per (day, account, ad_id, breakdown, value); NO aggregation above ad
- **Source:** public.meta_breakdown_daily (etl.meta_breakdowns, Meta Insights API) joined to the ten breakdown names
- **Refresh:** nightly, trailing 7 days re-pulled; older windows come from the backfill (newest first)
- **Coverage:** first and last day per breakdown: min(day) / max(day) over this view (a backfill moves first_day back window by window, up to 365 days; a breakdown Meta only serves for a shorter period stops earlier); ad_id and adset_id are NULL only where Meta refused a breakdown at ad level
- **Caveat:** Every one of the ten breakdowns is requested at AD level; ad_id and adset_id are NULL only for a breakdown Meta refused at ad level (then campaign_id is set and ad_id and adset_id are NULL; at account level campaign_id is NULL too). Query this view with ad_id IS NOT NULL (or read public.meta_breakdown_coverage: status and level per breakdown) to see which breakdowns have ad-level rows.
- **Caveat:** purchases and purchase_value are Meta-reported (count returning customers); they are not Upstack new customers and must not be mixed with them in one ratio.
- **Caveat:** reach and unique_outbound_clicks are per row (one ad, one day, one value): never sum them across days, ads or values. spend, impressions, link_clicks, purchases and purchase_value are additive within one breakdown.
- **Caveat:** Different breakdowns are different cuts of the same spend: sum within one breakdown only, never across breakdowns.
- **Caveat:** value for age_gender is 'age|gender' (for example '25-34|female'); hourly values are Meta's advertiser-time-zone hour buckets ('00:00:00 - 00:59:59').
- **Caveat:** Meta can return purchases for a day with no spend (view-through): keep those rows.

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `day` | date |  | Calendar day as returned by Meta (Lonvera Main Pacific, Lonvera BackUp Eastern). |
| `account` | text |  | Lonvera Main or Lonvera BackUp. |
| `campaign_id` | text |  | Meta campaign id (NULL only for a breakdown held at account level). |
| `adset_id` | text |  | Meta ad set id (NULL only for a breakdown Meta refused at ad level). |
| `ad_id` | text |  | Meta ad id (NULL only for a breakdown Meta refused at ad level: campaign or account rows). |
| `breakdown` | text |  | audience_segment (from user_segment_key) \| age \| gender \| age_gender \| country \| region \| publisher_platform \| platform_position \| device_platform \| hourly (advertiser time zone). All ten are requested at ad level. |
| `value` | text |  | The text value of the breakdown ('25-34', 'female', 'US', 'facebook', '25-34\|female' for age_gender, an hour bucket for hourly). |
| `spend` | numeric(14,2) |  | Meta spend, dollars. |
| `impressions` | bigint |  | Meta impressions. |
| `reach` | bigint |  | Accounts reached on the day within the row (not additive; NULL when Meta would not return it with the breakdown). |
| `link_clicks` | bigint |  | Meta link clicks (inline_link_clicks). |
| `unique_outbound_clicks` | bigint |  | Unique outbound clicks within the row (not additive). |
| `purchases` | numeric(12,2) |  | Meta-reported purchases. |
| `purchase_value` | numeric(14,2) |  | Meta-reported purchase value, dollars. |

*Spend and Meta ROAS by age, last 7 complete days*

```sql
SELECT value AS age, round(sum(spend), 2) AS spend, sum(purchases) AS purchases, round(sum(purchase_value) / NULLIF(sum(spend), 0), 2) AS meta_roas
FROM analytics.meta_breakdown_daily WHERE breakdown = 'age' AND day >= current_date - 7 AND day < current_date GROUP BY 1 ORDER BY 1;
```

*Which breakdowns have ad-level rows, and how far back each goes*

```sql
SELECT breakdown, ad_id IS NOT NULL AS ad_level, min(day) AS first_day, max(day) AS last_day, count(*) AS rows
FROM analytics.meta_breakdown_daily GROUP BY 1, 2 ORDER BY 1, 2;
```

### analytics.attribution_source_daily

*view*, columns like `public.proj_feed_attribution`

**Purpose.** Last-click attribution by ad source (Meta Ads, Google Ads, ...) per Pacific day: new customers and orders each source is credited with, exclusive per order.

- **Domain:** attribution
- **Grain:** one row per (day, ad_source)
- **Source:** public.proj_feed_attribution (the Upstack last-click feed)
- **Refresh:** nightly: the etl service (13:00 UTC, etl.projection_daily) replaces the trailing 14 days; the feeds service (21:00 UTC) writes the direct pull; see analytics.pipeline_feeds for each feed's mode and last load
- **Coverage:** 2025-01-01 through yesterday
- **Caveat:** Last-click is exclusive per order, unlike the any-click 30-day credit in meta_ad_daily / google_campaign_daily: the two bases do not agree and must not be mixed in one ratio.

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `day` | date |  | Pacific day. |
| `ad_source` | text |  | Ad source label: 'Meta Ads' \| 'Google Ads' \| ... |
| `new_customers` | numeric(18,2) |  | New customers credited. |
| `new_customer_revenue` | numeric(18,2) |  | Their revenue. |
| `orders` | numeric(18,2) |  | All orders credited. |
| `order_revenue` | numeric(18,2) |  | Their revenue. |
| `loaded_at` | timestamptz |  | When loaded. |

*New customers and revenue by source, last 30 days*

```sql
SELECT ad_source, sum(new_customers) AS new_customers, round(sum(new_customer_revenue)) AS new_customer_revenue FROM analytics.attribution_source_daily WHERE day > (SELECT max(day) FROM analytics.attribution_source_daily) - 30 GROUP BY 1 ORDER BY 2 DESC;
```

*First rows*

```sql
SELECT * FROM analytics.attribution_source_daily LIMIT 20;
```

### analytics.attribution_anyclick_daily

*view*, columns like `public.proj_feed_anyclick`

**Purpose.** New customers Upstack credits to Scaling and to Testing Meta campaigns per day under any-click 30-day attribution.

- **Domain:** attribution
- **Grain:** one row per (day, stage)
- **Source:** public.proj_feed_anyclick (the sheet tab 'Upstack Any-Click - By Campaign')
- **Refresh:** nightly: the etl service (13:00 UTC, etl.projection_daily) replaces the trailing 14 days; the feeds service (21:00 UTC) writes the direct pull; see analytics.pipeline_feeds for each feed's mode and last load
- **Coverage:** 2025-05-08 through yesterday
- **Caveat:** Any-click credits every Meta campaign the customer clicked in the 30 days before the order, so a customer can count under both stages across days; do not add to orders_daily.new_customers.

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `day` | date |  | Day. |
| `stage` | text |  | 'Scaling' \| 'Testing'. |
| `new_customers` | numeric(18,2) |  | New customers credited. |
| `new_customer_revenue` | numeric(18,2) |  | Their first-order revenue. |
| `loaded_at` | timestamptz |  | When loaded. |

*Testing vs scaling new customers by week*

```sql
SELECT date_trunc('week', day)::date AS week, stage, sum(new_customers) AS new_customers FROM analytics.attribution_anyclick_daily GROUP BY 1, 2 ORDER BY 1 DESC, 2;
```

*First rows*

```sql
SELECT * FROM analytics.attribution_anyclick_daily LIMIT 20;
```

### analytics.attribution_unmapped_daily

*view*, columns like `public.upstack_unmapped_daily`

**Purpose.** Upstack-credited orders the build could not tie to a Meta ad. They are inside orders_daily and inside no ad-level row of meta_ad_daily.

- **Domain:** attribution
- **Grain:** one row per (day, campaign, ad)
- **Source:** public.upstack_unmapped_daily (rebuilt in full on every build)
- **Refresh:** nightly (etl service 13:00 UTC)
- **Coverage:** 2026-05-06 through yesterday
- **Caveat:** reason: 'not_identified' (no ad name / '(not set)'), 'no_campaign' (the campaign matches no Meta campaign), 'no_ad' (campaign found, ad name not).

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `day` | date |  | Pacific day. |
| `campaign` | text |  | utm_campaign value. |
| `ad` | text |  | utm_ad value. |
| `reason` | text |  | not_identified \| no_campaign \| no_ad. |
| `new_customers` | numeric(12,2) |  | New customers credited. |
| `new_customer_revenue` | numeric(14,2) |  | Their first-order revenue, dollars. |
| `orders` | numeric(12,2) |  | All orders credited. |
| `order_revenue` | numeric(14,2) |  | Revenue, dollars. |

*Share of credited new customers that match no ad, by reason*

```sql
SELECT reason, sum(new_customers) AS new_customers FROM analytics.attribution_unmapped_daily GROUP BY 1 ORDER BY 2 DESC;
```

*First rows*

```sql
SELECT * FROM analytics.attribution_unmapped_daily LIMIT 20;
```

### analytics.orders_daily_detail

*view*, columns like `public.proj_feed_orders`

**Purpose.** orders_daily plus the tax collected and the refunds of each Pacific day: the two columns pacing uses to go from gross to net revenue.

- **Domain:** orders
- **Grain:** one row per day
- **Source:** public.proj_feed_orders (Shopify order totals, Upstack)
- **Refresh:** nightly: the etl service (13:00 UTC, etl.projection_daily) replaces the trailing 14 days; the feeds service (21:00 UTC) writes the direct pull; see analytics.pipeline_feeds for each feed's mode and last load
- **Coverage:** 2025-01-01 through yesterday
- **Caveat:** total_revenue is GROSS: tax included, before refunds. Net = total_revenue - tax - refunds. The pacing page and the P&L stay gross and show refunds as a memo only.
- **Caveat:** The columns shared with orders_daily carry the same values.

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `day` | date |  | Pacific day. |
| `new_customer_revenue` | numeric(18,2) |  | New-customer revenue. |
| `returning_customer_revenue` | numeric(18,2) |  | Returning-customer revenue. |
| `new_customers` | numeric(18,2) |  | New customers. |
| `total_revenue` | numeric(18,2) |  | Total revenue. |
| `orders` | numeric(18,2) |  | All orders. |
| `tax` | numeric(18,2) |  | Tax collected. |
| `refunds` | numeric(18,2) |  | Refunds. |
| `loaded_at` | timestamptz |  | When loaded. |

*Gross vs net revenue by month*

```sql
SELECT date_trunc('month', day)::date AS month, round(sum(total_revenue)) AS gross, round(sum(total_revenue - tax - refunds)) AS net, round(sum(refunds) / NULLIF(sum(total_revenue), 0), 4) AS refund_share FROM analytics.orders_daily_detail GROUP BY 1 ORDER BY 1 DESC;
```

*First rows*

```sql
SELECT * FROM analytics.orders_daily_detail LIMIT 20;
```

### analytics.orders_by_product_daily

*view*, columns like `public.proj_feed_nc_product`

**Purpose.** ACTUAL Shopify first-order revenue and new customers per Pacific day by the order's largest product line (not Meta-attributed): the only product split of actual new-customer revenue.

- **Domain:** orders
- **Grain:** one row per (day, product)
- **Source:** public.proj_feed_nc_product (ExpandFi merchant 1452 shopify_orders, or the sheet tab 'NC Rev - By Product')
- **Refresh:** nightly: the etl service (13:00 UTC, etl.projection_daily) replaces the trailing 14 days; the feeds service (21:00 UTC) writes the direct pull; see analytics.pipeline_feeds for each feed's mode and last load
- **Coverage:** 2025-01-01 through today; product = VitaBull | Beef Liver | Boneflex | Other
- **Caveat:** Revenue = order total_price split across product lines by net line price, tax included, before refunds; new_customers = orders whose largest product line is the product.
- **Caveat:** Day sums equal orders_daily to within restatement.

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `day` | date |  | Pacific day. |
| `product` | text |  | VitaBull \| Beef Liver \| Boneflex \| Other. |
| `new_customer_revenue` | numeric(18,2) |  | First-order revenue, dollars. |
| `new_customers` | numeric(18,2) |  | First orders. |
| `loaded_at` | timestamptz |  | When loaded. |

*Actual NC revenue and new customers by product, last 30 days*

```sql
SELECT product, round(sum(new_customer_revenue)) AS new_customer_revenue, sum(new_customers) AS new_customers FROM analytics.orders_by_product_daily WHERE day > (SELECT max(day) FROM analytics.orders_by_product_daily) - 30 GROUP BY 1 ORDER BY 2 DESC;
```

*First rows*

```sql
SELECT * FROM analytics.orders_by_product_daily LIMIT 20;
```

### analytics.orders_hourly

*view*, columns like `public.proj_feed_nc_hourly`

**Purpose.** Actual new-customer revenue and first orders by Pacific day and clock hour: the share curve behind the 'NC revenue today' card.

- **Domain:** orders
- **Grain:** one row per (day, hour) with at least one first order
- **Source:** public.proj_feed_nc_hourly (ExpandFi warehouse shopify_orders)
- **Refresh:** feeds service 21:00 UTC, trailing 35 closed days
- **Coverage:** from the first pull (a 60-day backfill once); today is never stored
- **Caveat:** Sparse: an hour with no first order has no row. Revenue is tax included, before refunds.

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `day` | date |  | Pacific day of the first order. |
| `hour` | smallint |  | Pacific wall-clock hour 0-23. |
| `new_customer_revenue` | numeric(18,2) |  | Sum of order total_price of the first orders in that hour (tax included, before refunds); test orders and orders without priced lines excluded. |
| `new_customers` | integer |  | Number of first orders in that hour. |
| `loaded_at` | timestamptz |  | When loaded. |

*Share of a day's new-customer revenue by hour (last 28 closed days)*

```sql
SELECT hour, round(sum(new_customer_revenue) / (SELECT sum(new_customer_revenue) FROM analytics.orders_hourly), 4) AS share FROM analytics.orders_hourly GROUP BY 1 ORDER BY 1;
```

*First rows*

```sql
SELECT * FROM analytics.orders_hourly LIMIT 20;
```

### analytics.amazon_daily

*view*, columns like `public.proj_feed_amazon`

**Purpose.** Amazon.com + B2B sales and Amazon Ads (de-duplicated across v1 / v2 / v3 report generations) by purchase day.

- **Domain:** orders
- **Grain:** one row per day
- **Source:** public.proj_feed_amazon (ExpandFi merchant 1452 sales table, or the sheet tab 'Amazon - Daily')
- **Refresh:** nightly: the etl service (13:00 UTC, etl.projection_daily) replaces the trailing 14 days; the feeds service (21:00 UTC) writes the direct pull; see analytics.pipeline_feeds for each feed's mode and last load
- **Coverage:** 2024-09-24 through yesterday (about a 2-day lag)
- **Caveat:** Amazon is a separate channel: its revenue is not in orders_daily and its ad spend is not in platform_daily. Pacing's business-wide totals add the two.

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `day` | date |  | Purchase day. |
| `orders` | numeric(18,2) |  | Orders. |
| `units` | numeric(18,2) |  | Units. |
| `revenue` | numeric(18,2) |  | Revenue. |
| `new_customer_orders` | numeric(18,2) |  | New-customer orders. |
| `new_customer_revenue` | numeric(18,2) |  | New-customer revenue. |
| `ad_spend` | numeric(18,2) |  | Amazon Ads spend (deduped). |
| `attributed_sales` | numeric(18,2) |  | Sales Amazon attributes to ads. |
| `clicks` | numeric(18,2) |  | Ad clicks. |
| `impressions` | numeric(18,2) |  | Ad impressions. |
| `loaded_at` | timestamptz |  | When loaded. |

*Amazon revenue and ad spend by month*

```sql
SELECT date_trunc('month', day)::date AS month, round(sum(revenue)) AS revenue, round(sum(ad_spend)) AS ad_spend FROM analytics.amazon_daily GROUP BY 1 ORDER BY 1 DESC;
```

*First rows*

```sql
SELECT * FROM analytics.amazon_daily LIMIT 20;
```

### analytics.site_traffic_daily

*view*, columns like `public.proj_feed_traffic`

**Purpose.** Pixel-tracked visitors, new visitors and sessions per day (Upstack).

- **Domain:** orders
- **Grain:** one row per day
- **Source:** public.proj_feed_traffic
- **Refresh:** nightly: the etl service (13:00 UTC, etl.projection_daily) replaces the trailing 14 days; the feeds service (21:00 UTC) writes the direct pull; see analytics.pipeline_feeds for each feed's mode and last load
- **Coverage:** 2025-05-08 through today
- **Caveat:** Visitors are unique within a day: summing days does not give unique visitors.

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `day` | date |  | Day. |
| `new_visitors` | numeric(18,2) |  | First-time visitors. |
| `visitors` | numeric(18,2) |  | Visitors. |
| `sessions` | numeric(18,2) |  | Sessions. |
| `loaded_at` | timestamptz |  | When loaded. |

*Cost per new visitor by week (Meta + Google spend)*

```sql
SELECT date_trunc('week', t.day)::date AS week, round(sum(p.spend) / NULLIF(sum(t.new_visitors), 0), 2) AS cost_per_new_visitor FROM analytics.site_traffic_daily t JOIN (SELECT day, sum(spend) AS spend FROM analytics.platform_daily GROUP BY 1) p USING (day) GROUP BY 1 ORDER BY 1 DESC LIMIT 12;
```

*First rows*

```sql
SELECT * FROM analytics.site_traffic_daily LIMIT 20;
```

### analytics.platform_daily_history

*view*, columns like `public.proj_feed_platform`

**Purpose.** Meta and Google spend and platform-reported conversions per day from 2025-01-01 (Upstack ad-platform feed): the longest spend history in the database.

- **Domain:** marketing
- **Grain:** one row per (day, platform)
- **Source:** public.proj_feed_platform
- **Refresh:** nightly: the etl service (13:00 UTC, etl.projection_daily) replaces the trailing 14 days; the feeds service (21:00 UTC) writes the direct pull; see analytics.pipeline_feeds for each feed's mode and last load
- **Coverage:** 2025-01-01 through yesterday
- **Caveat:** platform is normalised to 'meta' | 'google' (platform_label keeps Upstack's text). Spend reconciles with meta_ad_daily and google_campaign_daily on the days they share.
- **Caveat:** own_purchases / own_revenue are platform-reported and overlap across platforms: never add them. TikTok has no rows.

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `day` | date |  | Day. |
| `platform` | text |  | 'meta' \| 'google' (normalised from platform_label). |
| `platform_label` | text |  | 'Meta (Facebook/Instagram)' \| 'Google Ads' (TikTok has no rows). |
| `spend` | numeric(18,2) |  | Spend, dollars. |
| `link_clicks` | numeric(18,2) |  | Link clicks. |
| `clicks` | numeric(18,2) |  | All clicks. |
| `own_purchases` | numeric(18,2) |  | Platform-reported purchases. |
| `own_revenue` | numeric(18,2) |  | Platform-reported conversion value. |
| `own_new_customer_purchases` | numeric(18,2) |  | Platform-reported new-customer purchases (0 for Google). |
| `own_new_customer_revenue` | numeric(18,2) |  | Their value. |
| `loaded_at` | timestamptz |  | When loaded. |

*Spend by platform and month since 2025*

```sql
SELECT date_trunc('month', day)::date AS month, platform, round(sum(spend)) AS spend FROM analytics.platform_daily_history GROUP BY 1, 2 ORDER BY 1 DESC, 2;
```

*First rows*

```sql
SELECT * FROM analytics.platform_daily_history LIMIT 20;
```

### analytics.meta_product_daily

*view*, columns like `public.proj_feed_meta_product`

**Purpose.** Meta spend and link clicks per day by product, classified campaign name -> ad set name -> ad name -> Other.

- **Domain:** marketing
- **Grain:** one row per (day, product) after the day rule
- **Source:** public.proj_feed_meta_product
- **Refresh:** nightly (app rows, trailing 14 days)
- **Coverage:** 2025-01-01 through yesterday
- **Caveat:** A day is read from 'app' rows when it has any, else from the sheet rows (the view applies this, so no day is counted twice).
- **Caveat:** Campaigns that mix products are split by their ad set and ad names where the campaign name is not enough, so this can differ from meta_ads.product_slug.

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `day` | date |  | Day. |
| `product` | text |  | VitaBull \| Beef Liver \| Boneflex \| Other. |
| `source` | text |  | 'app' \| 'sheet'. |
| `spend` | numeric(18,2) |  | Spend. |
| `link_clicks` | numeric(18,2) |  | Link clicks. |
| `loaded_at` | timestamptz |  | When loaded. |

*Meta spend by product, last 30 days*

```sql
SELECT product, round(sum(spend)) AS spend, sum(link_clicks) AS link_clicks FROM analytics.meta_product_daily WHERE day > (SELECT max(day) FROM analytics.meta_product_daily) - 30 GROUP BY 1 ORDER BY 2 DESC;
```

*First rows*

```sql
SELECT * FROM analytics.meta_product_daily LIMIT 20;
```

### analytics.meta_product_reach

*view*, columns like `public.proj_feed_product_reach`

**Purpose.** Unique outbound clicks per product, de-duplicated across the product's ad sets, at day / week / month grain: the only per-product reach-like metric.

- **Domain:** marketing
- **Grain:** one row per (grain, period_key, product)
- **Source:** public.proj_feed_product_reach
- **Refresh:** nightly: the etl service (13:00 UTC, etl.projection_daily) replaces the trailing 14 days; the feeds service (21:00 UTC) writes the direct pull; see analytics.pipeline_feeds for each feed's mode and last load
- **Coverage:** from 2026-07-05 (Meta serves filtered unique clicks about 120 days back); earlier periods are absent and never re-pulled
- **Caveat:** Not additive across periods or products: read the row of the grain you want. The three products add to about 1.01-1.06x the account-level unique outbound clicks in meta_reach.
- **Caveat:** Reach and frequency per product do not exist, only unique outbound clicks.

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `grain` | text |  | 'day' \| 'week' \| 'month'. |
| `period_key` | text |  | Period key. |
| `period_start` | date |  | First day. |
| `period_end` | date |  | Last day. |
| `product` | text |  | VitaBull \| Beef Liver \| Boneflex \| Other. |
| `unique_outbound_clicks` | numeric(18,2) |  | Unique outbound clicks de-duplicated across the product's ad sets. |
| `loaded_at` | timestamptz |  | When loaded. |

*Unique outbound clicks per product, last 4 weeks*

```sql
SELECT period_key, product, unique_outbound_clicks FROM analytics.meta_product_reach WHERE grain = 'week' ORDER BY period_start DESC, product LIMIT 16;
```

*First rows*

```sql
SELECT * FROM analytics.meta_product_reach LIMIT 20;
```

### analytics.meta_bids_daily

*view*, columns like `public.proj_feed_bids`

**Purpose.** Meta bid settings per day, summed: spend on cost-cap / bid-cap ad sets and on ROAS-floor ad sets, with the spend-weighted bid sums.

- **Domain:** marketing
- **Grain:** one row per day
- **Source:** public.proj_feed_bids (the sheet tab 'Meta Bids - Daily')
- **Refresh:** nightly: the etl service (13:00 UTC, etl.projection_daily) replaces the trailing 14 days; the feeds service (21:00 UTC) writes the direct pull; see analytics.pipeline_feeds for each feed's mode and last load
- **Coverage:** 2026-04-28 through today; settings of past days are rebuilt from the account activity log, so older history cannot be pulled
- **Caveat:** Average cost cap = sum(spend_times_cap) / sum(capped_spend); average ROAS floor = sum(spend_times_floor) / sum(floor_spend). Per-ad-set bids are not stored.

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `day` | date |  | Day. |
| `spend` | numeric(18,2) |  | Total spend. |
| `capped_spend` | numeric(18,2) |  | Spend on ad sets with a cost cap. |
| `spend_times_cap` | numeric(18,2) |  | Sum of spend x cap. |
| `floor_spend` | numeric(18,2) |  | Spend on ad sets with a ROAS floor. |
| `spend_times_floor` | numeric(18,2) |  | Sum of spend x floor. |
| `loaded_at` | timestamptz |  | When loaded. |

*Share of spend on capped ad sets and average cost cap, by week*

```sql
SELECT date_trunc('week', day)::date AS week, round(sum(capped_spend) / NULLIF(sum(spend), 0), 3) AS capped_share, round(sum(spend_times_cap) / NULLIF(sum(capped_spend), 0), 2) AS avg_cost_cap FROM analytics.meta_bids_daily GROUP BY 1 ORDER BY 1 DESC;
```

*First rows*

```sql
SELECT * FROM analytics.meta_bids_daily LIMIT 20;
```

### analytics.pacing_live_today

*view*, columns like `public.proj_live_today_snapshots`

**Purpose.** Today's Meta spend, Google spend, new-customer revenue and new customers so far, one snapshot per hourly pull from Upstack.

- **Domain:** pacing
- **Grain:** one row per pull (pulled_at, to the minute)
- **Source:** public.proj_live_today_snapshots (etl.live_today)
- **Refresh:** Railway cron `live`, every hour on the hour
- **Coverage:** from the first pull; history kept 90 days
- **Caveat:** Upstack trails the ad platforms by 30-60 minutes, so the numbers are a floor of what was spent and sold; end-of-day projections made early in the day lean low.
- **Caveat:** Revenue is tax included, before refunds (gross).

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `pulled_at` | timestamptz |  | When Upstack was asked, to the minute (UTC). |
| `day` | date |  | Pacific day the numbers belong to. |
| `pacific_time` | time without time zone |  | Pacific wall-clock time of the pull, to the minute. |
| `meta_spend` | numeric(18,2) |  | Meta (Facebook / Instagram) spend so far that day, dollars, all ad accounts Upstack sees. |
| `google_spend` | numeric(18,2) |  | Google Ads spend so far that day, dollars. |
| `total_spend` | numeric(18,2) |  | Spend so far across every ad platform Upstack reports (Meta, Google, TikTok), dollars. |
| `new_customer_revenue` | numeric(18,2) |  | New-customer revenue so far that day, dollars (orders.new_customer_total_revenue). |
| `new_customers` | integer |  | New customers (first orders) so far that day. |

*The latest snapshot of each day*

```sql
SELECT DISTINCT ON (day) day, pacific_time, meta_spend, total_spend, new_customer_revenue, new_customers FROM analytics.pacing_live_today ORDER BY day DESC, pulled_at DESC LIMIT 14;
```

*First rows*

```sql
SELECT * FROM analytics.pacing_live_today LIMIT 20;
```

### analytics.pacing_amazon_month_to_date

*view*, columns like `public.proj_amazon_mtd_snapshots`

**Purpose.** Completed Amazon month-to-date revenue, one snapshot per Pacific day and month: reported days plus the missing days projected at the trailing 7-day average of the reported days.

- **Domain:** pacing
- **Grain:** one row per (snapshot_day, month)
- **Source:** public.proj_amazon_mtd_snapshots (etl.amazon_mtd), without the projected_days json
- **Refresh:** after the Amazon feed load (feeds 21:00 UTC) and again in the etl service (13:00 UTC)
- **Coverage:** from the first run; history kept so each day's projection can be compared with the final month
- **Caveat:** projected_mtd is an estimate, never an actual; the projected days themselves (json) are left out, rebuild them from daily_rate and days_projected.

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `snapshot_day` | date |  | Pacific day of the run. |
| `month` | date |  | First day of the month that was completed. |
| `computed_at` | timestamptz |  | When the run computed it (UTC). |
| `data_through` | date |  | Last day that should have been complete at the run (yesterday by default). |
| `last_actual_day` | date |  | Last day of the month with reported Amazon revenue; null before the first reported day. |
| `days_actual` | integer |  | Calendar days from the 1st through the last reported day. |
| `days_projected` | integer |  | Days after the last reported day, through data_through, that were projected (0 when the feed is current or stale). |
| `daily_rate` | numeric(18,2) |  | Dollars per projected day: mean of the last 7 reported days with revenue above zero (fewer when fewer exist). |
| `actual_mtd` | numeric(18,2) |  | Reported Amazon revenue from the 1st through the last reported day, dollars. |
| `projected_mtd` | numeric(18,2) |  | actual_mtd plus the projected days plus any partial-day top-up, dollars. |
| `status` | text |  | complete (nothing to project), projected, stale (feed more than 5 days behind: nothing projected), no_rate (no revenue in the feed), no_days (month has no complete day). |
| `method` | text |  | Rule and version that made the row, e.g. trailing-7-available/v1. |
| `flags` | text[] |  | Plain flags: feed_stale, short_window, window_has_gaps, gap_days_inside_month, newest_day_partial, no_data. |
| `partial_topup` | numeric(18,2) |  | Dollars added because the newest reported day looked partial (below half the days before it); 0 otherwise. |
| `lag_days` | integer |  | Days between data_through and the last reported day. |
| `rate_source` | text |  | trailing_7_available, or previous_month_last_7 when the month has no reported day yet. |
| `note` | text |  | The plain-language line the page shows. |

*Latest Amazon month-to-date projection per month*

```sql
SELECT DISTINCT ON (month) month, snapshot_day, actual_mtd, projected_mtd, status FROM analytics.pacing_amazon_month_to_date ORDER BY month DESC, snapshot_day DESC LIMIT 6;
```

*First rows*

```sql
SELECT * FROM analytics.pacing_amazon_month_to_date LIMIT 20;
```

### analytics.pacing_hourly_spend_curve

*view*, columns like `public.proj_pacing_hourly`

**Purpose.** Meta time-of-day spend curve (the last 30 days' hourly spend): the typed input behind the intraday projection.

- **Domain:** pacing
- **Grain:** one row per hour of day (Pacific)
- **Source:** public.proj_pacing_hourly (typed from Meta Ads Manager's time-of-day report)
- **Refresh:** manual
- **Coverage:** 24 rows

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `hour` | integer |  | Hour of day, Pacific (0-23). |
| `spend_30d` | numeric(18,2) |  | Spend in that hour over the 30 days. |
| `days_in_curve` | integer |  | Days in the curve. |
| `note` | text |  | Note. |
| `updated_at` | timestamptz |  | When updated. |

*Share of daily spend by hour*

```sql
SELECT hour, round(spend_30d / sum(spend_30d) OVER (), 4) AS share FROM analytics.pacing_hourly_spend_curve ORDER BY hour;
```

*First rows*

```sql
SELECT * FROM analytics.pacing_hourly_spend_curve LIMIT 20;
```

*All levers*

```

SELECT * FROM analytics.pacing_levers LIMIT 20;
```

### analytics.pacing_meta_spend_monthly

*view*, columns like `public.proj_meta_spend_monthly`

**Purpose.** Monthly Meta spend from ad_daily, read by the projection drivers module.

- **Grain:** one row per month
- **Source:** public.proj_meta_spend_monthly (a view over ad_daily)
- **Refresh:** live view
- **Coverage:** 2026-05 onward
- **Caveat:** A month is complete when days equals the days in the month.

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |

| `spend` | numeric(18,2) |  | Meta spend, dollars. |
| `days` | integer |  | Days with any ad_daily row. |
| `last_day` | date |  | Last day with data. |

*Monthly Meta spend*

```sql
SELECT month, spend, days FROM analytics.pacing_meta_spend_monthly ORDER BY month DESC;
```

*First rows*

```sql
SELECT * FROM analytics.pacing_meta_spend_monthly LIMIT 20;
```

### analytics.pipeline_feeds

*view*, columns like `public.proj_feed_runs`

**Purpose.** One row per pacing feed: its mode (sheet / shadow / direct), what was last loaded and through which day.

- **Domain:** pipeline
- **Grain:** one row per feed
- **Source:** public.proj_feed_runs joined to public.proj_feed_config
- **Refresh:** each load
- **Coverage:** about 12 feeds

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `feed` | text |  | Feed key (primary key). |
| `mode` | text |  | 'sheet' \| 'shadow' \| 'direct' (no config row = 'sheet'): which source the app reads for the feed. |
| `source` | text |  | 'sheet:<tab>' \| 'ad_daily' \| 'snapshot'. |
| `loaded_at` | timestamptz |  | When last loaded. |
| `rows_loaded` | integer |  | Rows of the last load. |
| `first_day` | date |  | First day loaded. |
| `last_day` | date |  | Last day loaded. |
| `note` | text |  | Free note. |

*Feeds, stalest first*

```sql
SELECT feed, mode, loaded_at, last_day, rows_loaded FROM analytics.pipeline_feeds ORDER BY last_day NULLS FIRST;
```

*First rows*

```sql
SELECT * FROM analytics.pipeline_feeds LIMIT 20;
```

### analytics.pipeline_feed_attempts

*view*, columns like `public.proj_feed_attempts`

**Purpose.** One row per direct pull attempt of a feed (any mode).

- **Domain:** pipeline
- **Grain:** one row per attempt
- **Source:** public.proj_feed_attempts
- **Refresh:** each feeds run (21:00 UTC)
- **Coverage:** since 2026-11-04

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `id` | bigint |  | Primary key. |
| `feed` | text |  | Feed key. |
| `mode` | text |  | Mode at the time. |
| `source` | text |  | 'api:upstack' \| 'api:warehance' \| 'api:meta' \| 'api:expandfi' \| 'sheet:<tab>'. |
| `started_at` | timestamptz |  | Start. |
| `finished_at` | timestamptz |  | Finish. |
| `status` | text |  | ok \| failed \| fallback \| skipped. |
| `rows` | integer |  | Rows pulled. |
| `first_day` | date |  | First day of the pull. |
| `last_day` | date |  | Last day of the pull. |
| `note` | text |  | Scrubbed note, <= 300 characters. |

*Failed attempts, newest first*

```sql
SELECT feed, mode, started_at, status, note FROM analytics.pipeline_feed_attempts WHERE status <> 'ok' ORDER BY started_at DESC LIMIT 20;
```

*First rows*

```sql
SELECT * FROM analytics.pipeline_feed_attempts LIMIT 20;
```

### analytics.pipeline_feed_verify

*view*, columns like `public.proj_feed_verify`

**Purpose.** Latest verification result per feed and day: the direct pull compared with the sheet-fed table.

- **Domain:** pipeline
- **Grain:** one row per (feed, day)
- **Source:** public.proj_feed_verify (etl.feeds verify)
- **Refresh:** each feeds run
- **Coverage:** a trailing window per feed

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `feed` | text |  | Feed key. |
| `day` | date |  | Day compared. |
| `status` | text |  | ok \| fail \| soft \| sheet_stale. |
| `n_keys` | integer |  | Keys compared. |
| `n_bad` | integer |  | Keys outside tolerance. |
| `worst_col` | text |  | Column with the worst difference. |
| `worst_abs` | numeric(18,4) |  | Worst absolute difference. |
| `worst_rel` | numeric(12,6) |  | Worst relative difference. |
| `detail` | text |  | Detail (<= 300 characters). |
| `checked_at` | timestamptz |  | When checked. |

*Days that did not verify*

```sql
SELECT feed, day, status, worst_col, worst_rel FROM analytics.pipeline_feed_verify WHERE status <> 'ok' ORDER BY day DESC LIMIT 20;
```

*First rows*

```sql
SELECT * FROM analytics.pipeline_feed_verify LIMIT 20;
```

### analytics.pipeline_refresh_runs

*view*, columns like `public.proj_refresh_runs`

**Purpose.** One row per projection module per run of the nightly projection refresh.

- **Domain:** pipeline
- **Grain:** one row per module run
- **Source:** public.proj_refresh_runs (etl.projection_daily)
- **Refresh:** nightly
- **Coverage:** since the projection module went live

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `id` | bigint |  | Primary key. |
| `module` | text |  | Projection module. |
| `started_at` | timestamptz |  | Start. |
| `finished_at` | timestamptz |  | Finish. |
| `status` | text |  | ok \| failed \| skipped. |
| `rows` | integer |  | Rows refreshed. |
| `data_through` | date |  | Last data day. |
| `error` | text |  | Scrubbed error text, first 300 characters. |

*Latest run of each module*

```sql
SELECT DISTINCT ON (module) module, started_at, status, rows, data_through FROM analytics.pipeline_refresh_runs ORDER BY module, started_at DESC;
```

*First rows*
