| `template_id` | text |  | Klaviyo template id the email renders from; NULL for SMS or when the template was not returned. |
| `editor_type` | text |  | Klaviyo editor of the template: SIMPLE (plain text email), CODE, DRAG_AND_DROP, USER_DRAFTED; NULL for SMS. |
| `html` | text |  | Full template HTML of the email; NULL for SMS or when not returned. |
| `body_text` | text |  | Readable plain text of the email (or the SMS body); NULL when nothing could be fetched or the email is images only. |
| `image_count` | integer |  | Real images in the email (tracking pixels excluded); 1 for an SMS with a picture. |
| `link_urls` | text[] |  | Distinct http(s) links in the message, in order of appearance. |
| `content_hash` | text |  | sha256 of the HTML (email) or body (SMS); changes when the message is edited. |
| `fetched_at` | timestamptz |  | When the content last changed in our copy (moves only when content_hash changes). |

*First rows*

```sql
SELECT * FROM analytics.klaviyo_message_content LIMIT 20;
```

*Row count*

```sql
SELECT count(*) AS rows FROM analytics.klaviyo_message_content;
```

### analytics.klaviyo_message_performance

*view*

**Purpose.** One row per Klaviyo message with its copy (subject, preview text, body text) and its totals and rates over everything in klaviyo_daily: the table to read which copy earns.

- **Domain:** email
- **Grain:** one row per message_id (campaign or flow message with at least one klaviyo_daily row)
- **Source:** klaviyo_daily (grouped by message_id) joined to klaviyo_message_content
- **Refresh:** live (a view over klaviyo_daily; moves with the nightly Klaviyo load)
- **Coverage:** the days loaded in klaviyo_daily (120 days at launch, then trailing); first_send_day is the first loaded day with sends, not the message's real first send
- **Caveat:** unique_opens_daily_sum and unique_clicks_daily_sum are sums of per-day uniques, not window uniques: a person who opens on two days counts twice. open_rate_daily_uniques and click_rate_daily_uniques are built on them and read a little high; use them to compare messages, not as Klaviyo's reported rates.
- **Caveat:** Orders and revenue are Klaviyo-attributed conversions of 'Placed Order - Upstackified', dated by the order day, so a message can show revenue with no sends when it sent before the loaded window.
- **Caveat:** Rates are NULL when delivered is 0. SMS messages have no opens. Messages without fetched copy have NULL subject / body_text and has_copy false.
- **Caveat:** SMS campaign unsubscribes are matched to the campaign, not the message, so they are not in this view.

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `message_id` | text |  | Klaviyo message id. |
| `source` | text |  | 'campaign' or 'flow'. |
| `parent_id` | text |  | Campaign id or flow id. |
| `channel` | text |  | 'email' or 'sms'. |
| `parent_name` | text |  | Campaign or flow name. |
| `subject` | text |  | Email subject (current copy); NULL for SMS or when not fetched. |
| `preview_text` | text |  | Email preview text (current copy). |
| `body_text` | text |  | Readable body text (current copy). |
| `image_count` | integer |  | Real images in the email. |
| `editor_type` | text |  | Klaviyo template editor type. |
| `first_send_day` | date |  | First loaded day with sends. |
| `last_send_day` | date |  | Last loaded day with sends. |
| `sends` | bigint |  | Sum of sends. |
| `delivered` | bigint |  | Sum of delivered. |
| `bounced` | bigint |  | Sum of bounced or failed. |
| `opens` | bigint |  | Sum of opens (includes machine opens). |
| `unique_opens_daily_sum` | bigint |  | Sum of per-day unique opens (not a window unique). |
| `clicks` | bigint |  | Sum of clicks, bots excluded. |
| `unique_clicks_daily_sum` | bigint |  | Sum of per-day unique clicks (not a window unique). |
| `unsubscribes` | bigint |  | Sum of unsubscribes. |
| `spam_complaints` | bigint |  | Sum of spam complaints. |
| `orders` | bigint |  | Sum of attributed orders. |
| `revenue` | numeric(14,2) |  | Sum of attributed revenue in dollars. |
| `open_rate_daily_uniques` | numeric |  | unique_opens_daily_sum / delivered; NULL when delivered is 0. |
| `click_rate_daily_uniques` | numeric |  | unique_clicks_daily_sum / delivered; NULL when delivered is 0. |
| `orders_per_delivered` | numeric |  | orders / delivered; NULL when delivered is 0. |
| `revenue_per_delivered` | numeric |  | revenue / delivered in dollars; NULL when delivered is 0. |
| `has_copy` | boolean |  | True when body_text was fetched for the message. |

*First rows*

```sql
SELECT * FROM analytics.klaviyo_message_performance LIMIT 20;
```

*Row count*

```sql
SELECT count(*) AS rows FROM analytics.klaviyo_message_performance;
```

### analytics.landing_pages

*view*

**Purpose.** Every landing page (sales page, advertorial, product page, funnel step) that at least one Meta ad sends people to, with when we last read it.

- **Domain:** landing_pages
- **Grain:** one row per normalised page address (id is the primary key; url is unique)
- **Source:** etl/landing_pages.py: the destination links found on Meta ad creatives
- **Refresh:** each run of python -m etl.landing_pages (not scheduled yet; see docs/LANDING_PAGES.md)
- **Coverage:** pages of ads that had spend in the window of the first run onward
- **Caveat:** The address is normalised: https, lower-case host, tracking parameters and the part after # removed, no trailing slash. Parameters that change which page loads are kept.
- **Caveat:** current_version_id points at the newest landing_page_versions row whose copy matches the last read (the copy can go back to an older version if the page is changed back).

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `id` | bigint |  | Primary key (bigserial). |
| `url` | text |  | Normalised page address (unique). |
| `host` | text |  | Host name of the page, lower case. |
| `path` | text |  | Path and any kept query string of the page. |
| `first_seen_at` | timestamptz |  | When an ad was first found pointing at this page. |
| `last_seen_at` | timestamptz |  | When an ad was last found pointing at this page. |
| `last_fetched_at` | timestamptz |  | When the page was last read (successfully or not); NULL until the first read. |
| `last_status` | integer |  | HTTP status of the last successful read; NULL before the first. |
| `last_error` | text |  | Short scrubbed error text when the last read failed; NULL after a good read. |
| `current_version_id` | bigint |  | landing_page_versions.id of the copy seen on the last good read. |
| `active` | boolean |  | False stops the page being read again; set by hand, nothing sets it. |

*First rows*

```sql
SELECT * FROM analytics.landing_pages LIMIT 20;
```

*Row count*

```sql
SELECT count(*) AS rows FROM analytics.landing_pages;
```

### analytics.landing_page_versions

*view*

**Purpose.** The copy of a landing page as it was on a given read: title, description, ordered text blocks with heading level, buttons, prices, guarantee sentences, review snippets and image alt text.

- **Domain:** landing_pages
- **Grain:** one row per (landing page, content hash): a new row exists only when the copy changed (id is the primary key)
- **Source:** etl/landing_pages.py: a plain public web request per page, parsed with the standard library (headless Chromium for pages built in the browser)
- **Refresh:** each run of python -m etl.landing_pages; a row is added only when the text hash changes
- **Coverage:** from the first read of each page
- **Caveat:** content_hash covers the text only (title, description, text blocks, button text, image alt text), not image addresses or scripts, so changing a picture without changing its alt text is not a new version.
- **Caveat:** Pages built in the browser have little text in the server HTML; those are read again with Chromium and rendered_in_browser is true. Text shown only after a click or inside an iframe is not captured.
- **Caveat:** fetched_at is when this exact copy was first seen, not when it was last confirmed (landing_pages.last_fetched_at has that).

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `id` | bigint |  | Primary key (bigserial). |
| `landing_page_id` | bigint |  | Page this copy belongs to (landing_pages.id, foreign key). |
| `content_hash` | text |  | sha256 of the page text (see caveats); unique per page. |
| `fetched_at` | timestamptz |  | When this copy was first seen. |
| `http_status` | integer |  | HTTP status of the read that produced this copy. |
| `final_url` | text |  | Address after redirects. |
| `title` | text |  | Page title (the title tag, else the Open Graph title). |
| `meta_description` | text |  | Meta description (else the Open Graph description). |
| `rendered_in_browser` | boolean |  | True when the copy came from headless Chromium because the server HTML held almost no text. |
| `word_count` | integer |  | Words in the page text. |
| `ctas` | jsonb |  | Button and call to action text as JSON: [{text, href, tag}]. |
| `prices` | jsonb |  | Dollar amounts found in the text as JSON: [{price, context}]. |
| `guarantees` | jsonb |  | Sentences that mention a guarantee, refund, money back or risk free, as a JSON array of strings. |
| `reviews` | jsonb |  | Review or testimonial snippets (quote blocks, star lines, structured data), as a JSON array of strings. |
| `full_text` | text |  | All text blocks joined by new lines, for searching. |

*First rows*

```sql
SELECT * FROM analytics.landing_page_versions LIMIT 20;
```

*Row count*

```sql
SELECT count(*) AS rows FROM analytics.landing_page_versions;
```

### analytics.landing_page_ads

*view*

**Purpose.** Which Meta ad sends people to which landing page, and how that link was found.

- **Domain:** landing_pages
- **Grain:** one row per (ad, landing page) (ad_id + landing_page_id is the primary key)
- **Source:** etl/landing_pages.py: the Meta ad creative, the Page post an ad reuses, or the ad preview
- **Refresh:** each run of python -m etl.landing_pages (new ads with spend only; --refresh-ads looks at all again)
- **Coverage:** ads with spend in the 30 days before each run, from the first run onward
- **Caveat:** An ad whose creative lists several links has one row per page. Its spend counts in full under each page in landing_page_top_ads, so spend must not be added across pages.
- **Caveat:** ad_id is not a foreign key to meta_ads because ads can be deleted in Meta.

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `ad_id` | text |  | Meta ad id. |
| `landing_page_id` | bigint |  | landing_pages.id (foreign key). |
| `source` | text |  | How the link was found: 'creative' (the ad's own creative), 'post' (the Page post the ad reuses), or 'preview' (the ad preview). |
| `first_seen_date` | date |  | Date the ad was first found with this link. |
| `last_seen_date` | date |  | Date the link was last confirmed. |

*First rows*

```sql
SELECT * FROM analytics.landing_page_ads LIMIT 20;
```

*Row count*

```sql
SELECT count(*) AS rows FROM analytics.landing_page_ads;
```

### analytics.landing_page_ad_checks

*view*

**Purpose.** Ads that etl/landing_pages.py has already looked at, so an ad with no readable link is not asked about every night.

- **Domain:** landing_pages
- **Grain:** one row per ad (ad_id is the primary key)
- **Source:** etl/landing_pages.py
- **Refresh:** each run of python -m etl.landing_pages
- **Coverage:** ads with spend in the window of the runs
- **Caveat:** An ad with result no_link or error is looked at again 14 days after checked_at.

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `ad_id` | text |  | Meta ad id (primary key). |
| `checked_at` | timestamptz |  | When the ad was last looked at. |
| `result` | text |  | 'linked' (a landing page was found), 'no_link' (none could be read) or 'error' (Meta returned an error). |
| `detail` | text |  | Short reason for no_link or error; NULL when linked. |

*First rows*

```sql
SELECT * FROM analytics.landing_page_ad_checks LIMIT 20;
```

*Row count*

```sql
SELECT count(*) AS rows FROM analytics.landing_page_ad_checks;
```

### analytics.landing_page_top_ads

*view*

**Purpose.** The ads that drive each landing page, ranked by spend over the last 7 and the last 30 days, with Meta-reported purchases, revenue and ROAS (revenue divided by spend).

- **Domain:** landing_pages
- **Grain:** one row per (landing page, window_days, ad): window_days is 7 or 30; only ads with spend in the window appear
- **Source:** public.landing_pages, public.landing_page_ads, public.ad_daily (Meta-reported) and public.meta_ads (name)
- **Refresh:** live view; the ad to page map moves with each run of python -m etl.landing_pages, spend with the nightly Meta pull
- **Coverage:** ads mapped to a page by the landing page job
- **Caveat:** Purchases and revenue are what Meta reports (all customers), not Upstack new-customer numbers; join analytics.meta_ad_daily on ad_id when you need those.
- **Caveat:** An ad that lists several links appears under each page with its full spend: do not add spend across pages.
- **Caveat:** The window is the last N Pacific calendar days including today, which is a partial day.

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `landing_page_id` | bigint |  | landing_pages.id. |
| `url` | text |  | Normalised page address. |
| `window_days` | integer |  | 7 or 30. |
| `ad_id` | text |  | Meta ad id. |
| `ad_name` | text |  | Ad name from meta_ads; NULL when the ad is not there. |
| `campaign_id` | text |  | Campaign id from meta_ads; NULL when the ad is not there. |
| `spend` | numeric |  | Spend in the window, dollars (Meta). |
| `purchases` | numeric |  | Meta-reported purchases in the window. |
| `revenue` | numeric |  | Meta-reported purchase revenue in the window, dollars. |
| `roas` | numeric |  | revenue divided by spend, 3 decimals. |
| `rank_by_spend` | bigint |  | 1 = biggest spender for this page and window. |

*First rows*

```sql
SELECT * FROM analytics.landing_page_top_ads LIMIT 20;
```

*Row count*

```sql
SELECT count(*) AS rows FROM analytics.landing_page_top_ads;
```

### analytics.shopify_orders

*view*

**Purpose.** Order-level Shopify orders of the last 120 days (rolling), one row per order, with money, status, source, discount codes and ship-to country / region. The place to answer order-level questions (refunds, rebills, gifts, discounts) that the daily roll-ups hide.

- **Domain:** orders
- **Grain:** one row per Shopify order (order_id is the primary key)
- **Source:** ExpandFi warehouse, merchant 1452, table shopify_orders (read-only), loaded by python -m etl.shopify_orders (etl/shopify_orders.py); fields come from the order's raw Shopify JSON
- **Refresh:** nightly (feeds service 21:00 UTC): the trailing 14 days are re-pulled every run, the whole 120 days every Sunday (Pacific) and on the first load; orders older than 120 days are deleted
- **Coverage:** orders processed in the last 120 Pacific days, including today so far; the warehouse trails Shopify by minutes to hours
- **Caveat:** No personal data is stored: no email, name, address or phone. customer_id is the Shopify customer id (NULL when the order has none); the ship-to is country and region only.
- **Caveat:** order_date is the Pacific day of processed_at. The warehouse's own created_at is the sync time of a September backfill and is NOT used.
- **Caveat:** is_new_customer = this is the customer's first Shopify order EVER (customer key = Shopify customer id, else the lower-cased email, as in the new-customer revenue feed), judged over the whole warehouse history, not only the 120 days.
- **Caveat:** refunded_amount = total_price - current_total_price (never below 0): Shopify's current total already nets refunds and order edits, and the warehouse's refund transactions are empty for many refunds. It is a net-down amount, not a refund ledger.
- **Caveat:** Orders restated after they fall out of the trailing 14 days are picked up by the Sunday full 120-day pull, so a late refund can lag up to a week.
- **Caveat:** is_rebill = source_name is subscription_contract_checkout_one. is_trybe_gift = the order carries the tag trybe (the $0 gift orders). Gift, test and cancelled orders are kept and flagged, never dropped; filter on the flags.
- **Caveat:** Revenue by product is NOT the sum of total_price by product: use shopify_order_lines.line_net for a split.

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `order_id` | bigint |  | Shopify order id (primary key). Not the ExpandFi row id. |
| `order_number` | text |  | Human-readable order number as the store shows it. |
| `processed_at` | timestamptz |  | When the order was processed (UTC instant). |
| `order_date` | date |  | Pacific calendar day of processed_at; the day to group by. |
| `is_new_customer` | boolean |  | True when this is the customer's first Shopify order ever. |
| `currency` | text |  | Order currency code (shop currency). |
| `subtotal_price` | numeric(12,2) |  | Order subtotal after discounts, before shipping and tax. |
| `total_discounts` | numeric(12,2) |  | Sum of all discounts on the order. |
| `shipping_price` | numeric(12,2) |  | Shipping charged (shop money). |
| `total_tax` | numeric(12,2) |  | Tax charged. |
| `total_price` | numeric(12,2) |  | Order total at checkout: subtotal + shipping + tax. |
| `current_total_price` | numeric(12,2) |  | Order total after refunds and edits, as Shopify reports it now. |
| `refunded_amount` | numeric(12,2) |  | total_price minus current_total_price, never below 0 (see the caveat). |
| `financial_status` | text |  | Shopify financial status (paid, refunded, partially_refunded, voided, ...). |
| `fulfillment_status` | text |  | Shopify fulfillment status; NULL = unfulfilled. |
| `source_name` | text |  | Shopify source name (web, subscription_contract_checkout_one for rebills, tiktok, or an app id). |
| `app_id` | text |  | Shopify app id that created the order; NULL when none. |
| `discount_codes` | text |  | Discount codes used, comma separated; NULL when none. |
| `ship_country` | text |  | Ship-to ISO country code; NULL when the order has no shipping address. |
| `ship_region` | text |  | Ship-to province / state code; NULL when none. |
| `cancelled_at` | timestamptz |  | When the order was cancelled; NULL when it was not. |
| `is_cancelled` | boolean |  | True when cancelled_at is set. |
| `is_test` | boolean |  | True for a Shopify test order. |
| `is_rebill` | boolean |  | True for a subscription rebill (source_name subscription_contract_checkout_one). |
| `is_trybe_gift` | boolean |  | True when the order has the tag trybe (free sample / gift orders). |
| `line_count` | integer |  | Number of line items stored in shopify_order_lines for this order. |
| `loaded_at` | timestamptz |  | When this row was last written by the loader. |

*First rows*

```sql
SELECT * FROM analytics.shopify_orders LIMIT 20;
```

*Row count*

```sql
SELECT count(*) AS rows FROM analytics.shopify_orders;
```

### analytics.shopify_order_lines

*view*

**Purpose.** Line items of shopify_orders: one row per product line with SKU, product bucket, quantity, price and discount.

- **Domain:** orders
- **Grain:** one row per order line (order_id, line_no is the primary key; line_no counts from 1 in the order's own line order)
- **Source:** the line_items array of the same ExpandFi shopify_orders rows, loaded together with shopify_orders by python -m etl.shopify_orders
- **Refresh:** with shopify_orders (nightly 21:00 UTC); a re-pulled order has its lines replaced, an order that leaves the 120-day window takes its lines with it
- **Coverage:** the same orders as shopify_orders
- **Caveat:** product is mapped from the SKU prefix: LV-VBULL = VitaBull, LV-BLC180 and DPCD1-GN180001 = BeefLiver, DPCD1-GN187001 = Boneflex, LV-VBELLE = VitaBelle, DPCD1-GN179001 = VitaFlow, anything else = Other.
- **Caveat:** line_net = max(price x quantity - line_discount, 0); the order's total_price also carries shipping and tax and order-level discounts, so the lines do not add up to it.

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `order_id` | bigint |  | Shopify order id; foreign key to shopify_orders.order_id (cascade delete). |
| `line_no` | integer |  | Position of the line in the order, from 1. |
| `sku` | text |  | SKU as sold; NULL when the line has none. |
| `product_name` | text |  | Line title as it shows on the order. |
| `product` | text |  | Product bucket mapped from the SKU (VitaBull, BeefLiver, Boneflex, VitaBelle, VitaFlow, Other). |
| `quantity` | integer |  | Units on the line. |
| `price` | numeric(12,2) |  | Unit price before discount. |
| `line_discount` | numeric(12,2) |  | Total discount on the line (all units). |
| `line_net` | numeric(12,2) |  | price x quantity minus line_discount, never below 0. |

*First rows*

```sql
SELECT * FROM analytics.shopify_order_lines LIMIT 20;
```

*Row count*

```sql
SELECT count(*) AS rows FROM analytics.shopify_order_lines;
```

## Meta (Facebook / Instagram) ads: ads, campaigns, daily results

### public.ad_daily

*table*

**Purpose.** Meta Graph API results per ad per day: the base fact table behind every Meta number. Meta-reported (Meta's own attribution).

- **Grain:** primary key `(ad_id, day)`; one row per (ad_id, day)
- **Source:** Meta Graph API insights level=ad, time_increment=1, both ad accounts (Lonvera Main, Lonvera BackUp); written by etl.meta_pull
- **Refresh:** nightly (etl service 13:00 UTC, etl.run_daily): the trailing 28 days are re-pulled and replaced in ONE transaction (all or nothing), because Meta restates conversions for weeks
- **Coverage:** 2026-05-06 (app_meta.daily_start, never moves) through yesterday; about 135K rows, 18.7K distinct ads in production
- **Caveat:** day is the ad account's OWN calendar day (Main = America/Los_Angeles, BackUp = New York); not re-based.
- **Caveat:** purchases = Meta action omni_purchase, else purchase, else offsite_conversion.fb_pixel_purchase, under Meta's default attribution (no window is requested); it counts returning customers. conv_value is the matching action value. Neither is Upstack's new-customer number.
- **Caveat:** No row filter on spend: purchases booked on days without delivery are kept, so a row can have spend 0 and purchases > 0.
- **Caveat:** link_clicks is Meta's link_click action (fallback inline_link_clicks). impressions is NULL only on legacy sheet rows (none in production).
- **Caveat:** meta_family_key / meta_family follow the ad's CURRENT name (build_metrics re-syncs them after a rename).
- **Caveat:** Rows with source 'sheet' / ad_id 'fam:<family>' were a family-grain backfill; none remain in production.
- **Caveat:** Reach, frequency, video metrics (thruplays, watch time), demographic / placement breakdowns, ad-level budgets and bids are NOT stored.

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `ad_id` | text | no | Meta ad id (text). 'fam:<family key>' on legacy family-grain rows. |
| `day` | date | no | Calendar day in the ad account's own timezone. |
| `meta_family_key` | text | no | lower(ad family): ad name without ' - Copy N' / merge-tag suffixes. Joins meta_family_map. |
| `meta_family` | text | no | The ad family with its original case. |
| `campaign_id` | text | no | Meta campaign id ('' = unknown, legacy rows only). |
| `spend` | numeric(14,2) | no | Meta spend, dollars. |
| `purchases` | numeric(12,2) | no | Meta-reported purchases (omni_purchase > purchase > pixel purchase), Meta default attribution; includes returning customers. |
| `conv_value` | numeric(14,2) | no | Meta-reported purchase conversion value, dollars (revenue for Meta ROAS). |
| `link_clicks` | numeric(12,0) | no | Meta link clicks. |
| `source` | text | no | 'meta_api' \| 'sheet'. |
| `loaded_at` | timestamptz | no | When the row was written. |
| `impressions` | numeric(14,0) | yes | Meta impressions (NULL = unknown, legacy rows). |

*Meta spend and ROAS by day, last 14 days*

```sql
SELECT day, round(sum(spend), 2) AS spend, sum(purchases) AS purchases, round(sum(conv_value) / NULLIF(sum(spend), 0), 2) AS roas
FROM ad_daily
WHERE day > (SELECT max(day) FROM ad_daily) - 14
GROUP BY 1 ORDER BY 1 DESC;
```

*Top ads of the last 7 days (join meta_ads for names)*

```sql
SELECT d.ad_id, a.ad_name, round(sum(d.spend), 2) AS spend, round(sum(d.conv_value) / NULLIF(sum(d.spend), 0), 2) AS roas
FROM ad_daily d JOIN meta_ads a USING (ad_id)
WHERE d.day > (SELECT max(day) FROM ad_daily) - 7
GROUP BY 1, 2 ORDER BY 3 DESC LIMIT 20;
```

### public.meta_ads

*table*

**Purpose.** One row per Meta ad: name, family, campaign, ad set, status, creation time. The raw dimension behind analytics.meta_ads.

- **Grain:** primary key `(ad_id)`; one row per ad_id
- **Source:** Meta Graph API: names and ids from the daily and all-time insights pulls, status / name / created_time from etl.status_refresh
- **Refresh:** nightly (etl.run_daily); status is refreshed for every ad that delivered in the last 60 days and every ad not ARCHIVED / DELETED
- **Coverage:** 42K ads: every ad in the daily window plus every ad the all-time pull returned
- **Caveat:** first_day / last_day are the first / last ad_daily day of the ad (NULL for the ~23K ads that never delivered since 2026-05-06).
- **Caveat:** status is Meta's effective_status at status_checked_at.
- **Caveat:** family_name / meta_family_key are fam(ad_name): ' - Copy N' and ' | x-merge-DATE' suffixes stripped.

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `ad_id` | text | no | Meta ad id. |
| `account` | text | yes | 'Lonvera Main' \| 'Lonvera BackUp'. |
| `ad_name` | text | yes | Current ad name. |
| `meta_family_key` | text | yes | lower(family_name). Joins meta_family_map.meta_family_key and ad_daily.meta_family_key. |
| `campaign_id` | text | yes | Meta campaign id. Joins meta_campaigns. |
| `adset_id` | text | yes | Meta ad set id. |
| `adset_name` | text | yes | Ad set name. |
| `created_date` | date | yes | Pacific calendar date of created_at. |
| `status` | text | yes | Meta effective_status. |
| `first_day` | date | yes | First ad_daily day for the ad. |
| `last_day` | date | yes | Last ad_daily day for the ad. |
| `created_at` | timestamptz | yes | Exact creation instant (UTC). |
| `family_name` | text | yes | Ad name with copy / merge suffixes stripped, original case. |
| `status_checked_at` | timestamptz | yes | When status was last read from Graph. |

*Ads per status*

```sql
SELECT status, count(*) AS ads FROM meta_ads GROUP BY 1 ORDER BY 2 DESC;
```

*Ad sets of the most recently delivering campaign*

```sql
SELECT adset_name, count(*) AS ads FROM meta_ads WHERE campaign_id = (SELECT campaign_id FROM meta_campaigns ORDER BY last_day DESC NULLS LAST LIMIT 1) GROUP BY 1 ORDER BY 2 DESC;
```

### public.meta_campaigns

*table*

**Purpose.** One row per Meta campaign with its stage (testing / scaling). Stage is a naming rule applied on every pull.

- **Grain:** primary key `(campaign_id)`; one row per campaign_id
- **Source:** Meta Graph API campaign names seen in the daily and all-time pulls; stage recomputed by etl.build_metrics
- **Refresh:** nightly (etl.run_daily)
- **Coverage:** 185 campaigns, 2026-05-06 onward
- **Caveat:** stage = 'testing' when lower(name) contains 'test', else 'scaling'. It is a convention of the campaign naming, not a Meta setting.
- **Caveat:** first_day / last_day = first / last ad_daily day with spend rows for the campaign.

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `campaign_id` | text | no | Meta campaign id (text). |
| `account` | text | yes | Ad account name. |
| `name` | text | no | Campaign name. |
| `stage` | text | no | 'testing' \| 'scaling'. |
| `first_day` | date | yes | First day with ad_daily rows. |
| `last_day` | date | yes | Last day with ad_daily rows. |

*Campaigns by stage*

```sql
SELECT stage, count(*) AS campaigns, min(first_day) AS first_day, max(last_day) AS last_day FROM meta_campaigns GROUP BY 1;
```

### public.meta_ad_lifetime

*table*

**Purpose.** All-time Meta totals per ad (Meta-reported), the source of every 'since launch' number.

- **Grain:** primary key `(ad_id)`; one row per ad_id
- **Source:** Meta Graph API insights level=ad over the whole account life (etl.lifetime_pull), both accounts
- **Refresh:** nightly (etl.run_daily): the table is REPLACED as a whole, only when both accounts reconcile with account-level lifetime spend within 0.5%
- **Coverage:** 41.7K ads, through the last complete day
- **Caveat:** Totals cover the ad's whole life, which reaches back before ad_daily's 2026-05-06 start.
- **Caveat:** tracker_family_key is only filled on legacy sheet-sourced rows; use meta_family_map for the tracker match. source is 'meta_api' for every production row.
- **Caveat:** Meta-reported (default attribution); purchases include returning customers.

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `ad_id` | text | no | Meta ad id. |
| `meta_family_key` | text | yes | lower(ad family), re-synced to the ad's current name. |
| `tracker_family_key` | text | yes | Legacy: the sheet tab's Ad Family column (NULL for API rows). |
| `spend` | numeric(14,2) | no | All-time spend, dollars. |
| `purchases` | numeric(12,2) | no | All-time Meta-reported purchases. |
| `conv_value` | numeric(14,2) | no | All-time Meta-reported purchase value, dollars. |
| `impressions` | numeric(14,0) | yes | All-time impressions. |
| `link_clicks` | numeric(12,0) | yes | All-time link clicks. |
| `source` | text | no | 'meta_api' (production) \| 'sheet' (legacy). |
| `pulled_at` | timestamptz | yes | When the all-time pull ran. |
| `first_day` | date | yes | Earliest delivery day if known (legacy). |

*Top 20 ads by all-time spend (Meta-reported)*

```sql
SELECT l.ad_id, a.ad_name, round(l.spend) AS spend, round(l.conv_value / NULLIF(l.spend, 0), 2) AS roas FROM meta_ad_lifetime l JOIN meta_ads a USING (ad_id) ORDER BY l.spend DESC LIMIT 20;
```

### public.ad_lifetime

*table*

**Purpose.** LEGACY: the sheet-era lifetime snapshot per tracker family (through app_meta.lifetime_cutoff 2026-09-17). Superseded by meta_ad_lifetime; do not use for current numbers.

- **Grain:** primary key `(family_key)`; one row per tracker family_key
- **Source:** Creative Key sheet tab 'Meta Ads - Lifetime' (frozen snapshot loaded once at backfill)
- **Refresh:** never (frozen)
- **Coverage:** through 2026-09-17
- **Caveat:** Kept for history only; since-launch figures now come from meta_ad_lifetime via meta_family_map.

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `family_key` | text | no | Tracker family key (lower). |
| `spend` | numeric(14,2) | no | Lifetime spend through the cutoff, dollars. |
| `purchases` | numeric(12,2) | no | Lifetime Meta-reported purchases. |
| `conv_value` | numeric(14,2) | no | Lifetime Meta-reported purchase value, dollars. |

### public.meta_family_map

*table*

**Purpose.** Maps every Meta ad family to a Creative Tracker family (the resolver's output). The bridge between Meta numbers and tracker tags.

- **Grain:** primary key `(meta_family_key)`; one row per meta_family_key
- **Source:** etl.resolve_families (ported resolver: exact / relaunch-encoded / ad-name / concept-prefix / product+type+version rules)
- **Refresh:** nightly (etl.run_daily): new families are mapped and previously unmatched ones retried; existing matches are left alone
- **Coverage:** 16.1K Meta families, 4.1K matched
- **Caveat:** tracker_family_key NULL = no tracker match (about 12K families, mostly old ads, ad copies by other names, and non-tracker products).
- **Caveat:** rule: 'exact' | 'relaunch' | 'adname' | 'prefix' | 'triple' (and 'sheet-windows' for legacy rows); NULL when unmatched.

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `meta_family_key` | text | no | lower(Meta ad family). Joins ad_daily.meta_family_key and meta_ads.meta_family_key. |
| `meta_family` | text | no | Display name of the Meta family. |
| `tracker_family_key` | text | yes | Matched tracker family_key (lower), or NULL. Joins tracker_rows.family_key. |
| `rule` | text | yes | Which resolver rule matched. |
| `resolved_at` | timestamptz | no | When the row was resolved. |

*How Meta families matched tracker families*

```sql
SELECT coalesce(rule, '(unmatched)') AS rule, count(*) AS families FROM meta_family_map GROUP BY 1 ORDER BY 2 DESC;
```

### public.meta_breakdown_daily

*table*

**Purpose.** Meta Ads results split by one breakdown set (age, gender, country, region, dma, placement, device, hour of day, creative asset ...), pulled at AD grain from the Insights API for both ad accounts. Partitioned by month on day.

- **Grain:** primary key `(day, account_id, level, entity_id, breakdown_set, dims_hash)`; one row per day x ad account x level (ad; campaign or account only for a set Meta refused at ad level) x entity x breakdown set x dimension values
- **Source:** Meta Marketing API Insights (read only), etl.meta_breakdowns
- **Refresh:** every 2 hours by the dedicated `breakdowns` service: the backfill (newest 30-day window first) until finished, then the trailing 7 days once a day
- **Coverage:** see meta_breakdown_coverage for the first and last day and the level per set
- **Caveat:** purchases and purchase_value are Meta-reported (they count returning customers, not Upstack new customers).
- **Caveat:** Reach and unique outbound clicks are per row (one ad, one day, one dimension value): never sum them across days, ads or sets.
- **Caveat:** Action breakdown sets (action_device, action_destination ...) split only purchases and purchase_value; their spend, impressions and click columns are NULL.
- **Caveat:** Rows of different breakdown sets are different cuts of the same spend: never add across sets.
- **Caveat:** Meta can return a row for a day with purchases but no spend (view-through), so keep rows with zero spend.
- **Caveat:** Up to three dimension values per row sit in dim1, dim2, dim3 in the order of the names in breakdown_set (age,gender: dim1 = age, dim2 = gender); asset breakdowns store the JSON text Meta returned.

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `day` | date | no | Calendar day as returned by Meta (each ad account buckets days in its own time zone: Lonvera Main Pacific, Lonvera BackUp Eastern). |
| `account_id` | text | no | Meta ad account id with the act_ prefix. |
| `level` | text | no | Grain of the entity: ad (the default for every set), campaign or account (only for a set Meta refused at the finer level). |
| `entity_id` | text | no | Ad id, campaign id or ad account id, depending on level. |
| `campaign_id` | text | yes | Campaign of the entity (NULL at account level). |
| `adset_id` | text | yes | Ad set of the entity (ad level only). |
| `breakdown_set` | text | no | Comma-separated breakdown names that were requested together, for example age,gender or publisher_platform,platform_position,impression_device. |
| `dim1` | text | yes | Value of the first name in breakdown_set (text). |
| `dim2` | text | yes | Value of the second name in breakdown_set, NULL when the set has one name. |
| `dim3` | text | yes | Value of the third name in breakdown_set, NULL when the set has fewer than three. |
| `dims_key` | text | no | Stable text form of the dimension values (names sorted, name=value joined with a vertical bar, percent-escaped); any length. Not indexed: the primary key uses dims_hash. |
| `spend` | numeric(14,2) | yes | Meta spend in account currency (US dollars). NULL for action breakdown sets. |
| `impressions` | bigint | yes | Meta impressions. NULL for action breakdown sets. |
| `reach` | bigint | yes | Accounts reached on that day within the row. Not additive across days, ads or sets. NULL for action breakdown sets or when Meta would not return it with the breakdown. |
| `clicks` | bigint | yes | All clicks (Meta clicks field), not only link clicks. NULL for action breakdown sets. |
| `inline_link_clicks` | bigint | yes | Link clicks (Meta inline_link_clicks). NULL for action breakdown sets. |
| `outbound_clicks` | bigint | yes | Outbound clicks (clicks that left Meta; Meta outbound_clicks, action type outbound_click). NULL for action breakdown sets. |
| `unique_outbound_clicks` | bigint | yes | Unique outbound clicks within the row (Meta unique_outbound_clicks). Not additive across days or ads. NULL for action breakdown sets. |
| `purchases` | numeric(12,2) | yes | Meta-reported purchases (omni_purchase, else purchase, else pixel purchase), same rule as ad_daily. |
| `purchase_value` | numeric(14,2) | yes | Meta-reported purchase value (same action type rule), gross dollars. |
| `pulled_at` | timestamptz | no | When this row was last written by the pull. |
| `dims_hash` | text | no | md5 of dims_key (generated column), the fixed-size stand-in for dims_key in the primary key so very long values (ad copy assets) can be indexed. |

### public.meta_breakdown_coverage

*table*

**Purpose.** What the breakdown pull holds per breakdown set and level: whether Meta accepted the request, the first and last day held, the row count and the last error.

- **Grain:** primary key `(breakdown_set, level)`; one row per breakdown set x level
- **Source:** etl.meta_breakdowns (written after every window)
- **Refresh:** nightly and after every backfill window
- **Coverage:** from the first run
- **Caveat:** A set Meta refuses at ad level gets a status unsupported row at level ad (with the reason in last_error) and is pulled at campaign, then account level; each level has its own row.
- **Caveat:** status unsupported is asked again every 14 days. status error means a transient or unexpected failure, so the set may have gaps.
- **Caveat:** first_day, last_day and rows are read from meta_breakdown_daily after each window, so during a backfill first_day moves back window by window.

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `breakdown_set` | text | no | Breakdown set name, as in meta_breakdown_daily.breakdown_set. |
| `level` | text | no | Level the set was requested at: ad, campaign or account. |
| `status` | text | no | ok = Meta accepted it and rows are held; unsupported = Meta refused the request at this level; error = failed for another reason (see last_error). |
| `first_day` | date | yes | Earliest day held in meta_breakdown_daily for this set and level. |
| `last_day` | date | yes | Latest day held in meta_breakdown_daily for this set and level. |
| `last_error` | text | yes | Scrubbed text of the last refusal or failure; NULL when the last check was clean. |
| `rows` | bigint | no | Rows held in meta_breakdown_daily for this set and level. |
| `checked_at` | timestamptz | no | When the set was last requested. |

### public.meta_breakdown_progress

*table*

**Purpose.** Resume bookkeeping of the breakdown backfill: one row per finished window of one breakdown set at one level for one ad account, so running the same backfill again skips what is done.

- **Grain:** primary key `(breakdown_set, level, account_id, window_start, window_end)`; one row per breakdown set x level x ad account x window
- **Source:** etl.meta_breakdowns (backfill and --auto mode; the plain trailing-days pull does not write it)
- **Refresh:** written as each window x set finishes
- **Coverage:** backfill windows and the three bookkeeping rows
- **Caveat:** A window is skipped on a rerun when a recorded window covers it. The backfill anchor is stored here on the first --auto run, so a resume after midnight needs no arguments.
- **Caveat:** Three bookkeeping rows have breakdown_set __anchor__ (window_start = oldest day, window_end = the backfill's --until day, rows = max days), __backfill_done__ (the backfill finished) and __caught_up__ (window_end = the last day the daily catch-up covered); level and account_id are '-'.
- **Caveat:** Safe to delete (or run with --restart): the next backfill simply starts over and pulls again (upserts).

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `breakdown_set` | text | no | Breakdown set name. |
| `level` | text | no | Level the window was pulled at. |
| `account_id` | text | no | Meta ad account id. |
| `window_start` | date | no | First day of the finished window (the oldest day for the __anchor__ row). |
| `window_end` | date | no | Last day of the finished window. |
| `rows` | bigint | no | Rows written for the window (both accounts are separate rows). |
| `done_at` | timestamptz | no | When the window finished. |

### public.landing_pages

*table*

**Purpose.** Every landing page (sales page, advertorial, product page, funnel step) that at least one Meta ad sends people to, with when we last read it.

- **Grain:** primary key `(id)`; one row per normalised page address (id is the primary key; url is unique)
- **Source:** etl/landing_pages.py: the destination links found on Meta ad creatives
- **Refresh:** each run of python -m etl.landing_pages (not scheduled yet; see docs/LANDING_PAGES.md)
- **Coverage:** pages of ads that had spend in the window of the first run onward
- **Caveat:** The address is normalised: https, lower-case host, tracking parameters and the part after # removed, no trailing slash. Parameters that change which page loads are kept.
- **Caveat:** current_version_id points at the newest landing_page_versions row whose copy matches the last read (the copy can go back to an older version if the page is changed back).

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `id` | bigint | no | Primary key (bigserial). |
| `url` | text | no | Normalised page address (unique). |
| `host` | text | no | Host name of the page, lower case. |
| `path` | text | no | Path and any kept query string of the page. |
| `first_seen_at` | timestamptz | no | When an ad was first found pointing at this page. |
| `last_seen_at` | timestamptz | no | When an ad was last found pointing at this page. |
| `last_fetched_at` | timestamptz | yes | When the page was last read (successfully or not); NULL until the first read. |
| `last_status` | integer | yes | HTTP status of the last successful read; NULL before the first. |
| `last_error` | text | yes | Short scrubbed error text when the last read failed; NULL after a good read. |
| `current_version_id` | bigint | yes | landing_page_versions.id of the copy seen on the last good read. |
| `active` | boolean | no | False stops the page being read again; set by hand, nothing sets it. |

### public.landing_page_versions

*table*

**Purpose.** The copy of a landing page as it was on a given read: title, description, ordered text blocks with heading level, buttons, prices, guarantee sentences, review snippets and image alt text.

- **Grain:** primary key `(id)`; one row per (landing page, content hash): a new row exists only when the copy changed (id is the primary key)
- **References:** `(landing_page_id)` -> `public.landing_pages(id)`
- **Source:** etl/landing_pages.py: a plain public web request per page, parsed with the standard library (headless Chromium for pages built in the browser)
- **Refresh:** each run of python -m etl.landing_pages; a row is added only when the text hash changes
- **Coverage:** from the first read of each page
- **Caveat:** content_hash covers the text only (title, description, text blocks, button text, image alt text), not image addresses or scripts, so changing a picture without changing its alt text is not a new version.
- **Caveat:** Pages built in the browser have little text in the server HTML; those are read again with Chromium and rendered_in_browser is true. Text shown only after a click or inside an iframe is not captured.
- **Caveat:** fetched_at is when this exact copy was first seen, not when it was last confirmed (landing_pages.last_fetched_at has that).

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `id` | bigint | no | Primary key (bigserial). |
| `landing_page_id` | bigint | no | Page this copy belongs to (landing_pages.id, foreign key). |
| `content_hash` | text | no | sha256 of the page text (see caveats); unique per page. |
| `fetched_at` | timestamptz | no | When this copy was first seen. |
| `http_status` | integer | yes | HTTP status of the read that produced this copy. |
| `final_url` | text | yes | Address after redirects. |
| `title` | text | yes | Page title (the title tag, else the Open Graph title). |
| `meta_description` | text | yes | Meta description (else the Open Graph description). |
| `rendered_in_browser` | boolean | no | True when the copy came from headless Chromium because the server HTML held almost no text. |
| `word_count` | integer | no | Words in the page text. |
| `blocks` | jsonb | no | Ordered text blocks as JSON: [{i, tag, level, text}], level is the heading level 1 to 6 or null, tag is the nearest label tag (h1..h6, p, li, blockquote, button, a, text). |
| `ctas` | jsonb | no | Button and call to action text as JSON: [{text, href, tag}]. |
| `prices` | jsonb | no | Dollar amounts found in the text as JSON: [{price, context}]. |
| `guarantees` | jsonb | no | Sentences that mention a guarantee, refund, money back or risk free, as a JSON array of strings. |
| `reviews` | jsonb | no | Review or testimonial snippets (quote blocks, star lines, structured data), as a JSON array of strings. |
| `images` | jsonb | no | Image alt text as JSON: [{alt, src}]. |
| `full_text` | text | no | All text blocks joined by new lines, for searching. |

### public.landing_page_ads

*table*

**Purpose.** Which Meta ad sends people to which landing page, and how that link was found.

- **Grain:** primary key `(ad_id, landing_page_id)`; one row per (ad, landing page) (ad_id + landing_page_id is the primary key)
- **References:** `(landing_page_id)` -> `public.landing_pages(id)`
- **Source:** etl/landing_pages.py: the Meta ad creative, the Page post an ad reuses, or the ad preview
- **Refresh:** each run of python -m etl.landing_pages (new ads with spend only; --refresh-ads looks at all again)
- **Coverage:** ads with spend in the 30 days before each run, from the first run onward
- **Caveat:** An ad whose creative lists several links has one row per page. Its spend counts in full under each page in landing_page_top_ads, so spend must not be added across pages.
- **Caveat:** ad_id is not a foreign key to meta_ads because ads can be deleted in Meta.

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `ad_id` | text | no | Meta ad id. |
| `landing_page_id` | bigint | no | landing_pages.id (foreign key). |
| `raw_url` | text | no | The link exactly as Meta returned it (with tracking parameters). |
| `source` | text | no | How the link was found: 'creative' (the ad's own creative), 'post' (the Page post the ad reuses), or 'preview' (the ad preview). |
| `first_seen_date` | date | no | Date the ad was first found with this link. |
| `last_seen_date` | date | no | Date the link was last confirmed. |

### public.landing_page_top_ads

*view*

**Purpose.** The ads that drive each landing page, ranked by spend over the last 7 and the last 30 days, with Meta-reported purchases, revenue and ROAS (revenue divided by spend).

- **Grain:** one row per (landing page, window_days, ad): window_days is 7 or 30; only ads with spend in the window appear
- **Source:** public.landing_pages, public.landing_page_ads, public.ad_daily (Meta-reported) and public.meta_ads (name)
- **Refresh:** live view; the ad to page map moves with each run of python -m etl.landing_pages, spend with the nightly Meta pull
- **Coverage:** ads mapped to a page by the landing page job
- **Caveat:** Purchases and revenue are what Meta reports (all customers), not Upstack new-customer numbers; join analytics.meta_ad_daily on ad_id when you need those.
- **Caveat:** An ad that lists several links appears under each page with its full spend: do not add spend across pages.
- **Caveat:** The window is the last N Pacific calendar days including today, which is a partial day.

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `landing_page_id` | bigint |  | landing_pages.id. |
| `url` | text |  | Normalised page address. |
| `window_days` | integer |  | 7 or 30. |
| `ad_id` | text |  | Meta ad id. |
| `ad_name` | text |  | Ad name from meta_ads; NULL when the ad is not there. |
| `campaign_id` | text |  | Campaign id from meta_ads; NULL when the ad is not there. |
| `spend` | numeric |  | Spend in the window, dollars (Meta). |
| `purchases` | numeric |  | Meta-reported purchases in the window. |
| `revenue` | numeric |  | Meta-reported purchase revenue in the window, dollars. |
| `roas` | numeric |  | revenue divided by spend, 3 decimals. |
| `rank_by_spend` | bigint |  | 1 = biggest spender for this page and window. |

## Upstack attribution of Meta orders (the Meta | Upstack switch)

### public.upstack_attr_days

*table*

**Purpose.** Registry of the days whose Upstack Meta attribution was pulled completely (a day with zero Meta orders is still a pulled day).

- **Grain:** primary key `(day)`; one row per day
- **Source:** etl.upstack_daily (Upstack /api/query-attribution, any-click, 30-day window, utm_source 'Meta Ads')
- **Refresh:** nightly (etl service 13:00 UTC): trailing 7 Pacific days re-pulled
- **Coverage:** 2026-05-06 through yesterday (Pacific), 182 days
- **Caveat:** Upstack's own Meta attribution reaches back to 2025-05-13; this app loaded it from 2026-05-06, where its Meta daily data starts.

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `day` | date | no | Pacific day. |
| `pulled_at` | timestamptz | no | When the day was last pulled. |
| `campaigns` | integer | no | Campaigns with orders or new customers that day. |
| `ad_rows` | integer | no | Ad-grain attribution rows pulled that day. |
| `api_calls` | integer | no | Upstack API calls the day took. |
| `nc_campaigns` | numeric(12,2) | no | New customers Upstack credits to Meta at campaign level (the total). |
| `nc_ads` | numeric(12,2) | no | The same, summed over the ad rows (the gap vs nc_campaigns = orders credited to a campaign but to no identifiable ad). |
| `note` | text | yes | Campaigns that came back without an ad split ('unidentified: ...'). |

*Pulled days and how many new customers Upstack credits to Meta at campaign vs ad level*

```sql
SELECT day, campaigns, ad_rows, nc_campaigns, nc_ads FROM upstack_attr_days ORDER BY day DESC LIMIT 14;
```

### public.upstack_attr_daily

*table*

**Purpose.** Raw Upstack attribution at utm campaign x utm ad NAME grain (as clicked), before mapping to Meta ad ids.

- **Grain:** primary key `(day, campaign, ad)`; one row per (day, campaign, ad) where campaign and ad are utm values
- **Source:** Upstack /api/query-attribution (dimension utm_campaign, then utm_ad per campaign), Meta Ads source
- **Refresh:** nightly: trailing 7 days replaced
- **Coverage:** 2026-05-06 through yesterday (Pacific), 27.7K rows
- **Caveat:** Names, not ids: the same ad name in several ad sets is summed. '(ad not identified)' marks campaigns Upstack could not split by ad.
- **Caveat:** Any-click, 30-day window: an order is credited to every ad clicked in the 30 days before it.
- **Caveat:** Revenue is the order total with tax, before refunds.

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `day` | date | no | Pacific day of the order. |
| `campaign` | text | no | utm_campaign value: usually the campaign NAME, sometimes a raw id or '(not set)'. |
| `ad` | text | no | utm_ad value (ad name); '(ad not identified)' when Upstack could not split the campaign by ad. |
| `nc` | numeric(12,2) | no | orders.new_customers: first-time customers credited. |
| `nc_rev` | numeric(14,2) | no | orders.new_customer_total_revenue: their first-order revenue, dollars. |
| `orders` | numeric(12,2) | no | orders.count: all orders credited. |
| `rev` | numeric(14,2) | no | orders.total_revenue of those orders, dollars. |

*Top utm campaigns by new customers, last 7 pulled days*

```sql
SELECT campaign, sum(nc) AS new_customers, sum(nc_rev) AS nc_revenue FROM upstack_attr_daily WHERE day > (SELECT max(day) FROM upstack_attr_days) - 7 GROUP BY 1 ORDER BY 2 DESC LIMIT 15;
```

### public.upstack_campaign_daily

*table*

**Purpose.** Upstack attribution totals per utm campaign and day (used for reconciliation against the ad-level rows).

- **Grain:** primary key `(day, campaign)`; one row per (day, campaign)
- **Source:** Upstack /api/query-attribution, dimension utm_campaign, Meta Ads source
- **Refresh:** nightly: trailing 7 days replaced
- **Coverage:** 2026-05-06 through yesterday
- **Caveat:** Campaign-level totals are higher than the ad-level rows by the orders Upstack credits to no identifiable ad (about 1.3% of orders).

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `day` | date | no | Pacific day. |
| `campaign` | text | no | utm_campaign value. |
| `nc` | numeric(12,2) | no | New customers credited. |
| `nc_rev` | numeric(14,2) | no | Their first-order revenue, dollars. |
| `orders` | numeric(12,2) | no | All orders credited. |
| `rev` | numeric(14,2) | no | Revenue of all credited orders, dollars. |

### public.upstack_channel_daily

*table*

**Purpose.** Upstack's own campaign-level ad-platform feed for Meta (spend, platform-reported purchases). Used only as the spend check: it equals Meta's spend to the cent from 2026-08-01.

- **Grain:** primary key `(day, campaign_id)`; one row per (day, Meta campaign_id)
- **Source:** Upstack /api/query-channel-details (campaign dimension, facebookIntegration rows)
- **Refresh:** nightly: trailing window replaced
- **Coverage:** loaded for the backfill window; has no data before 2026-06-05 and a one-day shift between 2026-06-22 and 2026-07-07
- **Caveat:** Not a source of truth for Meta spend: use ad_daily. Differences of 1-4% on a few October days come from the BackUp account's New York day.

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `day` | date | no | Day as Upstack buckets it (Pacific). |
| `campaign_id` | text | no | Meta campaign id. |
| `campaign_name` | text | yes | Campaign name. |
| `spend` | numeric(14,2) | no | Spend the feed reports, dollars. |
| `purchases` | numeric(12,2) | no | Purchases the platform reports (delivered through Upstack). |
| `purchases_value` | numeric(14,2) | no | Conversion value the platform reports, dollars. |
| `nc_purchases` | numeric(12,2) | no | Platform-reported new-customer purchases. |
| `nc_purchases_value` | numeric(14,2) | no | Their value, dollars. |
| `link_clicks` | numeric(12,0) | no | Link clicks. |

*Upstack's own Meta spend per day (the spend check)*

```sql
SELECT day, round(sum(spend), 2) AS spend FROM upstack_channel_daily GROUP BY 1 ORDER BY 1 DESC LIMIT 14;
```

### public.upstack_unmapped_daily

*table*

**Purpose.** Upstack attribution rows the build could not tie to a Meta ad. Kept out of every ad, family and tag total; the tie-out reports them.

- **Grain:** primary key `(day, campaign, ad)`; one row per (day, campaign, ad)
- **Source:** etl.upstack_build (rebuilt in full on every build)
- **Refresh:** nightly
- **Coverage:** 2026-05-06 through yesterday; 521 rows (about 1.3% of orders)
- **Caveat:** reason: 'not_identified' (no ad name / '(not set)' / '(ad not identified)'), 'no_campaign' (the utm campaign matches no Meta campaign), 'no_ad' (campaign found, ad name not).

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `day` | date | no | Pacific day. |
| `campaign` | text | no | utm_campaign value. |
| `ad` | text | no | utm_ad value. |
| `reason` | text | no | not_identified \| no_campaign \| no_ad. |
| `nc` | numeric(12,2) | no | New customers credited. |
| `nc_rev` | numeric(14,2) | no | Their first-order revenue, dollars. |
| `orders` | numeric(12,2) | no | All orders credited. |
| `rev` | numeric(14,2) | no | Revenue, dollars. |

*Credit that matched no Meta ad, by reason*

```sql
SELECT reason, count(*) AS rows, sum(nc) AS new_customers, sum(orders) AS orders FROM upstack_unmapped_daily GROUP BY 1 ORDER BY 2 DESC;
```

### public.ad_daily_upstack

*table*

**Purpose.** ad_daily-shaped table for the Upstack source: Meta's spend / clicks / impressions per ad and day plus the Upstack orders and new customers credited to each ad.

- **Grain:** primary key `(ad_id, day)`; one row per (ad_id, day) for every pulled day, plus rows for ads Upstack credited on days they had no ad_daily row
- **Source:** etl.upstack_build: Meta spend copied from ad_daily for every pulled day (so a Meta restatement flows through) + Upstack attribution mapped from names to ad ids
- **Refresh:** nightly (etl.upstack_daily): rebuilt in full from the stored raw pulls and the freshly refreshed ad_daily
- **Coverage:** 2026-05-06 through yesterday (Pacific), 152K rows
- **Caveat:** Upstack reports campaign and ad NAMES, not ids. Mapping rules in order: exact ad name inside the utm campaign, same family inside the campaign, exact name in any campaign, same family anywhere. Where several ads match (one ad copied into several ad sets) the day's credit is split by each ad's Meta spend that day (else lifetime spend, else equally) and always sums back exactly.
- **Caveat:** Any-click credits every ad clicked in the 30 days before an order, so ad-level credits overlap and rows exist with spend 0.
- **Caveat:** purchases / conv_value = ALL attributed orders; nc_purchases / nc_conv_value = first orders only (new customers).

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `ad_id` | text | no | Meta ad id. |
| `day` | date | no | Pacific day. |
| `meta_family_key` | text | no | lower(ad family) of the ad's current name. |
| `meta_family` | text | no | Ad family, original case. |
| `campaign_id` | text | no | Meta campaign id ('' when unknown). |
| `spend` | numeric(14,2) | no | Meta's spend for the ad that day (copy of ad_daily; 0 on credit-only rows), dollars. |
| `purchases` | numeric(12,2) | no | Upstack orders (new and returning customers) credited to the ad. |
| `conv_value` | numeric(14,2) | no | Order revenue of those orders, dollars (tax included, before refunds). |
| `nc_purchases` | numeric(12,2) | no | Upstack new customers (first orders) credited to the ad. |
| `nc_conv_value` | numeric(14,2) | no | First-order revenue of those new customers, dollars. |
| `link_clicks` | numeric(12,0) | no | Meta link clicks (copy of ad_daily). |
| `impressions` | numeric(14,0) | yes | Meta impressions (copy of ad_daily). |
| `source` | text | no | 'upstack'. |
| `loaded_at` | timestamptz | no | When the table was built. |

*Upstack new customers and spend by day*

```sql
SELECT day, round(sum(spend), 2) AS spend, sum(nc_purchases) AS new_customers, round(sum(nc_conv_value) / NULLIF(sum(spend), 0), 2) AS nc_roas
FROM ad_daily_upstack
WHERE day > (SELECT max(day) FROM ad_daily_upstack) - 14
GROUP BY 1 ORDER BY 1 DESC;
```

### public.ad_daily_upstack_nc

*view*, columns like `public.ad_daily`

**Purpose.** View over ad_daily_upstack with the exact column set of ad_daily, on the NEW-CUSTOMER basis: purchases = nc_purchases, conv_value = nc_conv_value. Lets the app run the same SQL on either source.

- **Grain:** one row per (ad_id, day)
- **Source:** public.ad_daily_upstack
- **Refresh:** live view
- **Coverage:** as ad_daily_upstack
- **Caveat:** Spend, clicks, impressions are Meta's; only purchases / conv_value change meaning.

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `ad_id` | text |  | Meta ad id (text). 'fam:<family key>' on legacy family-grain rows. |
| `day` | date |  | Calendar day in the ad account's own timezone. |
| `meta_family_key` | text |  | lower(ad family). |
| `meta_family` | text |  | Ad family, original case. |
| `campaign_id` | text |  | Meta campaign id. |
| `spend` | numeric(14,2) |  | Meta's spend (copy), dollars. |
| `purchases` | numeric(12,2) |  | Upstack new customers (first orders) credited to the ad. |
| `conv_value` | numeric(14,2) |  | Their first-order revenue, dollars. |
| `link_clicks` | numeric(12,0) |  | Meta link clicks (copy). |
| `source` | text |  | 'upstack'. |
| `loaded_at` | timestamptz |  | When ad_daily_upstack was built. |
| `impressions` | numeric(14,0) |  | Meta impressions (copy). |

*Examples: same shape as `public.ad_daily`; query it the same way.*

**Purpose.** View over ad_daily_upstack with the exact column set of ad_daily, on the ALL-ORDERS basis: purchases = purchases, conv_value = conv_value (every credited order).

- **Grain:** one row per (ad_id, day)
- **Source:** public.ad_daily_upstack
- **Refresh:** live view

| `day` | date |  | Calendar day in the ad account's own timezone. |
| `meta_family_key` | text |  | lower(ad family). |
| `meta_family` | text |  | Ad family, original case. |
| `campaign_id` | text |  | Meta campaign id. |
| `spend` | numeric(14,2) |  | Meta's spend (copy), dollars. |
| `purchases` | numeric(12,2) |  | All Upstack orders credited to the ad. |
| `conv_value` | numeric(14,2) |  | Revenue of those orders, dollars. |
| `link_clicks` | numeric(12,0) |  | Meta link clicks (copy). |
