### public.meta_ad_lifetime_upstack_nc

*view*, columns like `public.meta_ad_lifetime`

**Purpose.** Since-launch per ad on the Upstack new-customer basis = every day held in ad_daily_upstack (Upstack has no all-time endpoint at ad grain).

- **Grain:** one row per ad_id
- **Source:** public.ad_daily_upstack_nc grouped by ad
- **Refresh:** live view
- **Coverage:** 2026-05-06 onward
- **Caveat:** 'Since launch' here is since 2026-05-06, not the ad's real launch.

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `ad_id` | text |  | Meta ad id. |
| `meta_family_key` | text |  | lower(ad family). |
| `tracker_family_key` | text |  | NULL. |
| `spend` | numeric |  | Meta spend over the days held. |
| `purchases` | numeric |  | Upstack new customers over the days held. |
| `conv_value` | numeric |  | Their first-order revenue, dollars. |
| `impressions` | numeric |  | Impressions over the days held. |
| `link_clicks` | numeric |  | Link clicks over the days held. |
| `source` | text |  | 'meta_api'. |
| `pulled_at` | timestamptz |  | Latest loaded_at. |
| `first_day` | date |  | First day held for the ad. |

*Examples: same shape as `public.meta_ad_lifetime`; query it the same way.*

### public.meta_ad_lifetime_upstack_all

*view*, columns like `public.meta_ad_lifetime`

**Purpose.** Since-launch per ad on the Upstack all-orders basis = every day held in ad_daily_upstack.

- **Grain:** one row per ad_id
- **Source:** public.ad_daily_upstack_all grouped by ad
- **Refresh:** live view
- **Coverage:** 2026-05-06 onward
- **Caveat:** 'Since launch' here is since 2026-05-06, not the ad's real launch.

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `ad_id` | text |  | Meta ad id. |
| `meta_family_key` | text |  | lower(ad family). |
| `tracker_family_key` | text |  | NULL. |
| `spend` | numeric |  | Meta spend over the days held. |
| `purchases` | numeric |  | All Upstack orders credited over the days held. |
| `conv_value` | numeric |  | Their revenue, dollars. |
| `impressions` | numeric |  | Impressions over the days held. |
| `link_clicks` | numeric |  | Link clicks over the days held. |
| `source` | text |  | 'meta_api'. |
| `pulled_at` | timestamptz |  | Latest loaded_at. |
| `first_day` | date |  | First day held for the ad. |

*Examples: same shape as `public.meta_ad_lifetime`; query it the same way.*

## Google Ads (campaign grain, from Upstack)

### public.upstack_google_campaigns

*table*

**Purpose.** Google Ads campaign dimension from the Upstack channel feed.

- **Grain:** primary key `(campaign_id)`; one row per Google campaign_id
- **Source:** Upstack /api/query-channel-details (googleAdsIntegration rows); campaign_type derived from the name by etl.upstack_google.campaign_type
- **Refresh:** nightly (etl.upstack_daily)
- **Coverage:** 90 campaigns
- **Caveat:** Upstack exposes no campaign type; it is read from the campaign name ('PMax' = Performance Max, 'DG..' / 'IL | Demand Gen' = Demand Gen, 'Standard Shopping' / 'SS |' = Shopping, 'Brand / Prospecting / Competitors Search' = Search, anything else = Other).

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `campaign_id` | text | no | Google campaign id. |
| `name` | text | no | Campaign name. |
| `status` | text | yes | Campaign status as the feed reports it. |
| `bid_strategy` | text | yes | Bid strategy as the feed reports it. |
| `campaign_type` | text | no | Shopping \| Search \| Performance Max \| Demand Gen \| Other. |
| `updated_at` | timestamptz | no | When the row was last refreshed. |

*Google campaigns by type*

```sql
SELECT campaign_type, count(*) AS campaigns FROM upstack_google_campaigns GROUP BY 1 ORDER BY 2 DESC;
```

### public.upstack_google_channel_daily

*table*

**Purpose.** Google's own daily numbers per campaign (spend, impressions, clicks, conversions) delivered through Upstack's ad-platform feed.

- **Grain:** primary key `(day, campaign_id)`; one row per (day, campaign_id); zero-activity days are not stored
- **Source:** Upstack /api/query-channel-details, Google rows, campaign grain, Pacific days
- **Refresh:** nightly: trailing window replaced
- **Coverage:** 2024-03-25 (first day with Google spend) through yesterday; 8.9K rows
- **Caveat:** Google restates conversions after the fact, so purchases / purchases_value of recent days move by up to about 1%.
- **Caveat:** Google's new-customer measures are empty in this feed: there is no Google-reported new-customer number, only Upstack's attribution.

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `day` | date | no | Pacific day. |
| `campaign_id` | text | no | Google campaign id. |
| `spend` | numeric(14,2) | no | Spend, dollars. |
| `impressions` | numeric(14,0) | no | Impressions. |
| `clicks` | numeric(12,0) | no | Clicks (all clicks). |
| `purchases` | numeric(12,2) | no | Purchases Google counts (channel.purchases). |
| `purchases_value` | numeric(14,2) | no | Conversion value Google reports, dollars. |

*Google spend per day*

```sql
SELECT day, round(sum(spend), 2) AS spend, sum(clicks) AS clicks FROM upstack_google_channel_daily GROUP BY 1 ORDER BY 1 DESC LIMIT 14;
```

### public.upstack_google_attr_daily

*table*

**Purpose.** Raw Upstack attribution for Google Ads at utm campaign NAME grain.

- **Grain:** primary key `(day, campaign)`; one row per (day, campaign)
- **Source:** Upstack /api/query-attribution, utm_source 'Google Ads', any-click, 30-day window
- **Refresh:** nightly: trailing 7 days replaced
- **Coverage:** 2025-05-13 (first day Upstack has any attribution) through yesterday; 4.8K rows
- **Caveat:** Any-click credits Google with orders that also had a Meta click, so Upstack's Google orders exceed Google's own count (about 536 vs 339 over 30 days in Oct 2026).

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `day` | date | no | Pacific day of the order. |
| `campaign` | text | no | utm_campaign value as clicked. |
| `nc` | numeric(12,2) | no | orders.new_customers. |
| `nc_rev` | numeric(14,2) | no | orders.new_customer_total_revenue, dollars. |
| `orders` | numeric(12,2) | no | orders.count. |
| `rev` | numeric(14,2) | no | orders.total_revenue, dollars. |

*Upstack-attributed Google new customers per day*

```sql
SELECT day, sum(nc) AS new_customers, sum(nc_rev) AS nc_revenue FROM upstack_google_attr_daily GROUP BY 1 ORDER BY 1 DESC LIMIT 14;
```

### public.upstack_google_days

*table*

**Purpose.** Registry of the days whose Google attribution was pulled completely.

- **Grain:** primary key `(day)`; one row per day
- **Source:** etl.upstack_google
- **Refresh:** nightly
- **Coverage:** 2025-05-13 through yesterday, 540 days

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `day` | date | no | Pacific day. |
| `pulled_at` | timestamptz | no | When the day was last pulled. |
| `campaigns` | integer | no | utm campaigns with attribution that day. |
| `nc` | numeric(12,2) | no | New customers Upstack credits to Google that day. |
| `orders` | numeric(12,2) | no | Orders Upstack credits to Google that day. |

*First and last pulled Google attribution day*

```sql
SELECT min(day) AS first_day, max(day) AS last_day, count(*) AS days FROM upstack_google_days;
```

### public.google_daily

*table*

**Purpose.** Derived Google campaign x day table: the channel feed rows joined to the attribution rows by campaign name. Behind analytics.google_campaign_daily.

- **Grain:** primary key `(day, campaign_key)`; one row per (day, campaign_key)
- **Source:** etl.upstack_google.build (rebuilt in full from upstack_google_channel_daily + upstack_google_attr_daily)
- **Refresh:** nightly (etl.upstack_daily)
- **Coverage:** 2024-03-25 through yesterday (Pacific); 9.4K rows
- **Caveat:** Attribution is keyed by campaign NAME; when two campaign ids share a name its numbers go to the one with the most spend.
- **Caveat:** Before 2025-05-13 the nc / nc_rev / orders / rev columns are 0 because Upstack had no attribution then, NOT because there were no orders; analytics.google_campaign_daily turns those into NULL.
- **Caveat:** in_feed = false rows are utm campaigns that match no Google campaign (spend 0).

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `day` | date | no | Pacific day. |
| `campaign_key` | text | no | Google campaign id, or 'name:<utm campaign>' for in_feed = false rows. |
| `campaign_name` | text | no | Campaign name. |
| `campaign_type` | text | no | Shopping \| Search \| Performance Max \| Demand Gen \| Other. |
| `in_feed` | boolean | no | true = a campaign of the Google channel feed. |
| `spend` | numeric(14,2) | no | Google spend, dollars. |
| `impressions` | numeric(14,0) | no | Impressions. |
| `clicks` | numeric(12,0) | no | Clicks. |
| `purchases` | numeric(12,2) | no | Google-reported purchases. |
| `purchases_value` | numeric(14,2) | no | Google-reported conversion value, dollars. |
| `nc` | numeric(12,2) | no | Upstack new customers credited (attribution). |
| `nc_rev` | numeric(14,2) | no | Their first-order revenue, dollars. |
| `orders` | numeric(12,2) | no | Upstack orders credited. |
| `rev` | numeric(14,2) | no | Their revenue, dollars. |

*Google spend by campaign type, last 30 days*

```sql
SELECT campaign_type, round(sum(spend)) AS spend, sum(clicks) AS clicks
FROM google_daily
WHERE day > (SELECT max(day) FROM google_daily) - 30
GROUP BY 1 ORDER BY 2 DESC;
```

## Actual orders: the business's real new-customer revenue

### public.upstack_orders_daily

*table*

**Purpose.** The business's actual revenue and customers per Pacific day, counted once. Numerator of Total NC ROAS. Behind analytics.orders_daily.

- **Grain:** primary key `(day)`; one row per Pacific day
- **Source:** Upstack /api/query, orders.* measures, granularity day, America/Los_Angeles (etl.upstack_orders)
- **Refresh:** nightly (etl.upstack_daily): trailing 7 days replaced; the job refuses an empty answer
- **Coverage:** 2025-01-01 through yesterday, 672 days
- **Caveat:** Order totals include tax, before refunds. No product / platform dimension. A day with no orders is absent.

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `day` | date | no | Pacific day. |
| `nc_rev` | numeric(18,2) | no | orders.new_customer_total_revenue: first-order revenue, dollars. |
| `rc_rev` | numeric(18,2) | no | orders.returning_customer_total_revenue: repeat-order revenue, dollars. |
| `nc_orders` | numeric(18,2) | no | orders.new_customers: number of new customers. |
| `total_rev` | numeric(18,2) | no | orders.total_revenue, dollars. |
| `orders` | numeric(18,2) | no | orders.count: all orders. |
| `pulled_at` | timestamptz | no | When the day was last pulled. |

*Last 7 days*

```sql
SELECT * FROM upstack_orders_daily ORDER BY day DESC LIMIT 7;
```

### public.shopify_orders

*table*

**Purpose.** Order-level Shopify orders of the last 120 days (rolling), one row per order, with money, status, source, discount codes and ship-to country / region. The place to answer order-level questions (refunds, rebills, gifts, discounts) that the daily roll-ups hide.

- **Grain:** primary key `(order_id)`; one row per Shopify order (order_id is the primary key)
- **Source:** ExpandFi warehouse, merchant 1452, table shopify_orders (read-only), loaded by python -m etl.shopify_orders (etl/shopify_orders.py); fields come from the order's raw Shopify JSON
- **Refresh:** nightly (feeds service 21:00 UTC): the trailing 14 days are re-pulled every run, the whole 120 days every Sunday (Pacific) and on the first load; orders older than 120 days are deleted
- **Coverage:** orders processed in the last 120 Pacific days, including today so far; the warehouse trails Shopify by minutes to hours
- **Caveat:** No personal data is stored: no email, name, address or phone. customer_id is the Shopify customer id (NULL when the order has none); the ship-to is country and region only.
- **Caveat:** order_date is the Pacific day of processed_at. The warehouse's own created_at is the sync time of a September backfill and is NOT used.
- **Caveat:** is_new_customer = this is the customer's first Shopify order EVER (customer key = Shopify customer id, else the lower-cased email, as in the new-customer revenue feed), judged over the whole warehouse history, not only the 120 days.
- **Caveat:** refunded_amount = total_price - current_total_price (never below 0): Shopify's current total already nets refunds and order edits, and the warehouse's refund transactions are empty for many refunds. It is a net-down amount, not a refund ledger.
- **Caveat:** Orders restated after they fall out of the trailing 14 days are picked up by the Sunday full 120-day pull, so a late refund can lag up to a week.
- **Caveat:** is_rebill = source_name is subscription_contract_checkout_one. is_trybe_gift = the order carries the tag trybe (the $0 gift orders). Gift, test and cancelled orders are kept and flagged, never dropped; filter on the flags.
- **Caveat:** Revenue by product is NOT the sum of total_price by product: use shopify_order_lines.line_net for a split.

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `order_id` | bigint | no | Shopify order id (primary key). Not the ExpandFi row id. |
| `order_number` | text | yes | Human-readable order number as the store shows it. |
| `processed_at` | timestamptz | no | When the order was processed (UTC instant). |
| `order_date` | date | no | Pacific calendar day of processed_at; the day to group by. |
| `customer_id` | text | yes | Shopify customer id; NULL when the order has none. No other customer data is stored. |
| `is_new_customer` | boolean | no | True when this is the customer's first Shopify order ever. |
| `currency` | text | yes | Order currency code (shop currency). |
| `subtotal_price` | numeric(12,2) | yes | Order subtotal after discounts, before shipping and tax. |
| `total_discounts` | numeric(12,2) | yes | Sum of all discounts on the order. |
| `shipping_price` | numeric(12,2) | yes | Shipping charged (shop money). |
| `total_tax` | numeric(12,2) | yes | Tax charged. |
| `total_price` | numeric(12,2) | yes | Order total at checkout: subtotal + shipping + tax. |
| `current_total_price` | numeric(12,2) | yes | Order total after refunds and edits, as Shopify reports it now. |
| `refunded_amount` | numeric(12,2) | yes | total_price minus current_total_price, never below 0 (see the caveat). |
| `financial_status` | text | yes | Shopify financial status (paid, refunded, partially_refunded, voided, ...). |
| `fulfillment_status` | text | yes | Shopify fulfillment status; NULL = unfulfilled. |
| `source_name` | text | yes | Shopify source name (web, subscription_contract_checkout_one for rebills, tiktok, or an app id). |
| `app_id` | text | yes | Shopify app id that created the order; NULL when none. |
| `tags` | text | yes | Order tags, comma separated, as Shopify stores them. |
| `discount_codes` | text | yes | Discount codes used, comma separated; NULL when none. |
| `ship_country` | text | yes | Ship-to ISO country code; NULL when the order has no shipping address. |
| `ship_region` | text | yes | Ship-to province / state code; NULL when none. |
| `cancelled_at` | timestamptz | yes | When the order was cancelled; NULL when it was not. |
| `is_cancelled` | boolean | no | True when cancelled_at is set. |
| `is_test` | boolean | no | True for a Shopify test order. |
| `is_rebill` | boolean | no | True for a subscription rebill (source_name subscription_contract_checkout_one). |
| `is_trybe_gift` | boolean | no | True when the order has the tag trybe (free sample / gift orders). |
| `line_count` | integer | no | Number of line items stored in shopify_order_lines for this order. |
| `loaded_at` | timestamptz | no | When this row was last written by the loader. |

### public.shopify_order_lines

*table*

**Purpose.** Line items of shopify_orders: one row per product line with SKU, product bucket, quantity, price and discount.

- **Grain:** primary key `(order_id, line_no)`; one row per order line (order_id, line_no is the primary key; line_no counts from 1 in the order's own line order)
- **References:** `(order_id)` -> `public.shopify_orders(order_id)`
- **Source:** the line_items array of the same ExpandFi shopify_orders rows, loaded together with shopify_orders by python -m etl.shopify_orders
- **Refresh:** with shopify_orders (nightly 21:00 UTC); a re-pulled order has its lines replaced, an order that leaves the 120-day window takes its lines with it
- **Coverage:** the same orders as shopify_orders
- **Caveat:** product is mapped from the SKU prefix: LV-VBULL = VitaBull, LV-BLC180 and DPCD1-GN180001 = BeefLiver, DPCD1-GN187001 = Boneflex, LV-VBELLE = VitaBelle, DPCD1-GN179001 = VitaFlow, anything else = Other.
- **Caveat:** line_net = max(price x quantity - line_discount, 0); the order's total_price also carries shipping and tax and order-level discounts, so the lines do not add up to it.

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `order_id` | bigint | no | Shopify order id; foreign key to shopify_orders.order_id (cascade delete). |
| `line_no` | integer | no | Position of the line in the order, from 1. |
| `sku` | text | yes | SKU as sold; NULL when the line has none. |
| `product_name` | text | yes | Line title as it shows on the order. |
| `product` | text | no | Product bucket mapped from the SKU (VitaBull, BeefLiver, Boneflex, VitaBelle, VitaFlow, Other). |
| `quantity` | integer | no | Units on the line. |
| `price` | numeric(12,2) | yes | Unit price before discount. |
| `line_discount` | numeric(12,2) | yes | Total discount on the line (all units). |
| `line_net` | numeric(12,2) | yes | price x quantity minus line_discount, never below 0. |

## Creative tracker, tags, hooks, content

### public.products

*table*

**Purpose.** The product list (Vita Bull, Beef Liver, Boneflex, Vita Belle, Vital Flow, plus 'unassigned') and the rules that route tracker rows and Meta ads to a product.

- **Grain:** primary key `(id)`; one row per product
- **Source:** seeded by migration 001; tag_set is flipped to 'linemap' by etl.load_tags once a product's line-map coverage is high enough
- **Refresh:** rarely changes
- **Coverage:** 6 rows
- **Caveat:** meta_regex is applied (case-insensitive, lowest meta_priority first) to a Meta family name to guess a product for ads that are on no tracker row.
- **Caveat:** tag_set: which ad_tags.tag_set drives the hierarchy for the product: 'linemap' (Communication Overview line) or 'tracker' (the tracker's own columns).

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `id` | integer | no | Primary key. |
| `slug` | text | no | 'vita-bull' \| 'beef-liver' \| 'boneflex' \| 'vita-belle' \| 'vital-flow' \| 'unassigned'. |
| `name` | text | no | Display name. |
| `tracker_names` | text[] | no | Values of the Creative Tracker 'Product' column that map to this product (array). |
| `meta_regex` | text | yes | Regular expression used to guess the product from a Meta family name (NULL for 'unassigned'). |
| `meta_priority` | integer | no | Lower is tested first. |
| `tag_set` | text | no | 'linemap' \| 'tracker'. |
| `sort_order` | integer | no | Display order. |
| `active` | boolean | no | false hides the product from the Products table. |

*The product list*

```sql
SELECT id, slug, name, tag_set, active FROM products ORDER BY sort_order;
```

### public.taxonomy_lines

*table*

**Purpose.** The Communication Overview: one line per market cluster x ICP x pain point x emotional trigger for each product (codes L01.. Vita Bull, B01.. Beef Liver, BF01.. Boneflex).

- **Grain:** primary key `(id)`; one row per line (product_id, line_code)
- **References:** `(product_id)` -> `public.products(id)`
- **Source:** Creative Key sheet tab 'Communication Overview' via etl.load_taxonomy
- **Refresh:** manual (run etl.load_taxonomy after the sheet changes); not in the nightly cron
- **Coverage:** 157 lines
- **Caveat:** Codes are stable across reloads; a changed trigger keeps its row and code.
- **Caveat:** added_after_tagging = true marks a line the sheet gained after the ads were tagged: it reads 0 ads by construction, not 'untested'.

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `id` | integer | no | Primary key. |
| `product_id` | integer | no | products.id. |
| `line_code` | text | no | L01.. / B01.. / BF01.. (unique per product). |
| `cluster` | text | no | Market cluster. |
| `icp` | text | no | Ideal customer profile / persona. |
| `pain_point` | text | no | Pain point. |
| `emotional_trigger` | text | yes | Emotional trigger (can be NULL). |
| `story` | text | yes | Short story the ad would tell (15 words or fewer). |
| `cluster_color` | text | yes | Hex colour of the cluster in the sheet. |
| `sort_order` | integer | no | Communication Overview order. |
| `added_after_tagging` | boolean | no | true = the line was added after the product's ads were tagged. |

*Lines per product and cluster*

```sql
SELECT p.slug, l.cluster, count(*) AS lines FROM taxonomy_lines l JOIN products p ON p.id = l.product_id GROUP BY 1, 2 ORDER BY 1, 3 DESC;
```

### public.line_ingredients

*table*

**Purpose.** Ingredient rows that hang off a taxonomy line: what each ingredient attacks and supports.

- **Grain:** primary key `(id)`; one row per (line, ingredient)
- **References:** `(line_id)` -> `public.taxonomy_lines(id)`
- **Source:** Creative Key sheet tab 'Communication Overview' via etl.load_taxonomy
- **Refresh:** manual with load_taxonomy
- **Coverage:** 285 rows

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `id` | integer | no | Primary key. |
| `line_id` | integer | no | taxonomy_lines.id. |
| `ingredient` | text | yes | Nutrient / active compound that addresses the pain point (e.g. Zinc, Heme Iron, Glucosamine). |
| `attacks` | text | yes | 'What It Attacks': the mechanism it counters. |
| `supports` | text | yes | 'What It Supports'. |
| `sort_order` | integer | no | Order within the line. |

*Ingredients of the first lines*

```sql
SELECT l.line_code, l.pain_point, i.ingredient, i.attacks FROM line_ingredients i JOIN taxonomy_lines l ON l.id = i.line_id ORDER BY l.line_code, i.sort_order LIMIT 20;
```

### public.tracker_rows

*table*

**Purpose.** Creative Tracker rows for every product: one row per concept / ad variant with its status, copy, transcript and the tracker's own tags.

- **Grain:** primary key `(id)`; one row per tracker row (row_key); an ad family (family_key) can span several rows
- **References:** `(product_id)` -> `public.products(id)`
- **Source:** Creative Key Google Sheet tab 'Creative Tracker' via etl.tracker_sync
- **Refresh:** nightly (etl.run_daily); the sync is refused (no writes) on a renamed required header or a >20% jump in blank families / drop in rows
- **Coverage:** 3,982 rows (3,980 live)
- **Caveat:** Identity is row_key = concept_id + '#' + ordinal, never the sheet row. concept_id is text ('0510' is not '510').
- **Caveat:** family_key = lower(ad_family), or 'id:' + lower(concept_id) when the family is blank; rows with a blank family can never match Meta spend.
- **Caveat:** Rows the sheet no longer has get deleted_at (never removed); deleted rows are ignored by the metrics.
- **Caveat:** launch_date is a date typed by the team, not a Meta field.

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `id` | bigint | no | Primary key. |
| `product_id` | integer | yes | products.id. |
| `row_key` | text | no | Stable row key ('<concept id>#<n>'). |
| `concept_id` | text | no | Tracker Concept ID (text). |
| `ad_family` | text | no | Tracker 'Ad Family' as typed ('' when blank). |
| `family_key` | text | no | The unique-ad key (see caveat). |
| `sheet_row` | integer | yes | Row number on the sheet; informational, used only to order rows (the first row of a family has the lowest). |
| `status` | text | yes | Tracker status. |
| `ad_type` | text | yes | VID \| IMG. |
| `style` | text | yes | Creative style. |
| `vendor` | text | yes | Creative vendor. |
| `fidelity` | text | yes | Creative fidelity. |
| `proof` | text | yes | Proof mechanism. |
| `launch_date` | date | yes | Launch date from the tracker. |
| `concept_name` | text | yes | Concept name. |
| `description` | text | yes | Short description. |
| `hypothesis` | text | yes | Why the concept is tested. |
| `meta_ad_name` | text | yes | Ad name in Meta (tracker 'Name' column). |
| `primary_text` | text[] | yes | Primary text 1-3 (array). |
| `headlines` | text[] | yes | Headlines 1-3 (array). |
| `ad_description` | text | yes | Ad description. |
| `transcript` | text | yes | Video transcript from the tracker. |
| `asset_url` | text | yes | Link to the creative asset. |
| `tracker_cluster` | text | yes | Tracker's Market Cluster. |
| `tracker_icp` | text | yes | Tracker's ICP (persona). |
| `tracker_icp_legacy` | text | yes | The older ICP column kept by the team. |
| `tracker_pain` | text | yes | Tracker's Pain Point. |
| `tracker_emotion` | text | yes | Tracker's Emotion. |
| `tracker_ingredient` | text | yes | Tracker's Lead Ingredient. |
| `raw` | jsonb | yes | Every tracker column by header (jsonb). Free-form; not exposed in analytics. |
| `synced_at` | timestamptz | no | Last sync that saw the row. |
| `deleted_at` | timestamptz | yes | Set when a sync no longer sees the row; cleared if it reappears. |
| `fingerprint` | text | yes | md5 of concept name \| description \| hypothesis \| brief date: the stable identity for duplicate (concept, family) groups. |

*Live tracker rows and unique ads per product*

```sql
SELECT p.slug, count(*) AS rows, count(DISTINCT t.family_key) AS unique_ads FROM tracker_rows t JOIN products p ON p.id = t.product_id WHERE t.deleted_at IS NULL GROUP BY 1 ORDER BY 2 DESC;
```

*Rows by status*

```sql
SELECT status, count(*) AS rows FROM tracker_rows WHERE deleted_at IS NULL GROUP BY 1 ORDER BY 1;
```

### public.ad_tags

*table*

**Purpose.** Tags per tracker row. 'linemap' = the Communication Overview line a tagger chose after looking at the creative; 'tracker' = the tracker's own Market Cluster / ICP / Pain Point.

- **Grain:** primary key `(id)`; one current row per (tracker_row_id, tag_set); older versions kept with is_current = false
- **References:** `(line_id)` -> `public.taxonomy_lines(id)`; `(tracker_row_id)` -> `public.tracker_rows(id)`
- **Source:** tag_set 'tracker': etl.tracker_sync on every sync (version 'tracker-live'); 'linemap': etl.load_tags from a tagging cycle file (Sonnet taggers reading the image / video frames + transcript)
- **Refresh:** 'tracker' nightly; 'linemap' by hand per cycle (versions dated 2026-11-03, 2026-11-04, 2026-11-05)
- **Coverage:** 7.8K rows (3.8K current linemap rows for Vita Bull / Beef Liver / Boneflex)
- **Caveat:** Confidence and method matter: 'text only' / 'no picture (copy only)' tags came from copy alone (about 580 rows) and are the weakest; high-confidence image / video tags are the most reliable.
- **Caveat:** A tracker row with no current tag in the product's tag_set shows as '(untagged)' in the app.

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `id` | bigint | no | Primary key. |
| `tracker_row_id` | bigint | no | tracker_rows.id. |
| `tag_set` | text | no | 'linemap' \| 'tracker'. |
| `version` | text | no | Tagging cycle, e.g. 'linemap-v2-2026-11-03', 'tracker-live'. |
| `is_current` | boolean | no | true for the tag in force. |
| `line_id` | integer | yes | taxonomy_lines.id (linemap tags). |
| `cluster` | text | yes | Market cluster. |
| `icp` | text | yes | ICP / persona. |
| `pain_point` | text | yes | Pain point. |
| `emotional_trigger` | text | yes | Emotional trigger. |
| `confidence` | text | yes | high \| medium \| low \| NULL. |
| `method` | text | yes | How it was tagged: 'image', 'video: 4 frames + Whisper transcript', 'text only', 'no picture (copy only)', 'video: thumbnail + no audio' ... |
| `seen` | text | yes | 'What Was Seen': the tagger's description of the creative. |
| `why` | text | yes | Why the line was chosen. |
| `runner_up` | text | yes | Second-best line. |
| `copy_only_line` | text | yes | Line the copy-only first pass picked when different. |
| `tagger` | text | yes | Who / what tagged it. |
| `tagged_at` | timestamptz | no | When the tag was written. |
| `extra` | jsonb | yes | Cycle-specific extras (jsonb): what was heard, gaps, media notes. |

*Current tags by set, version and confidence*

```sql
SELECT tag_set, version, confidence, count(*) AS tags FROM ad_tags WHERE is_current GROUP BY 1, 2, 3 ORDER BY 1, 2, 3;
```

### public.ad_hooks

*table*

**Purpose.** One hook per tracker row: the ad's first spoken line plus on-image headline boiled down to one short line.

- **Grain:** primary key `(tracker_row_id)`; one row per tracker_row_id
- **References:** `(tracker_row_id)` -> `public.tracker_rows(id)`
- **Source:** etl.load_hooks from an extraction cycle file (Sonnet reading the opening frame and the first transcript sentences)
- **Refresh:** manual per cycle; a newer version replaces the older hook of every row it covers
- **Coverage:** 3.4K rows (about 82% / 87% / 94% of the Vita Bull / Beef Liver / Boneflex tracker rows)
- **Caveat:** Rows without a readable source (silent videos, missing thumbnails, no copy) have no hook. About 120 hooks run past 18 words.

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `tracker_row_id` | bigint | no | tracker_rows.id (primary key). |
| `hook` | text | no | The one-line hook. |
| `spoken` | text | yes | First spoken line (video audio / transcript). |
| `on_screen` | text | yes | On-image headline / first on-screen text. |
| `hook_source` | text | yes | spoken \| on_screen \| both \| copy \| none. |
| `confidence` | text | yes | high \| medium \| low. |
| `note` | text | yes | Extractor remark. |
| `version` | text | no | Extraction cycle, e.g. 'hooks-v1-2026-11-05'. |
| `extractor` | text | yes | Who produced it. |
| `extracted_at` | timestamptz | no | When the row was written. |

*Hook source and confidence mix*

```sql
SELECT hook_source, confidence, count(*) AS hooks FROM ad_hooks GROUP BY 1, 2 ORDER BY 3 DESC;
```

### public.family_content

*table*

**Purpose.** Per tracker family: the copy Meta runs, transcript, post link. Feeds the ad page of the app.

- **Grain:** primary key `(family_key)`; one row per tracker family_key
- **Source:** etl.load_assets from a tagging cycle folder (Meta copy, whisper transcripts, tracker transcripts)
- **Refresh:** manual per cycle
- **Coverage:** 3.7K families

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `family_key` | text | no | Tracker family key (primary key). |
| `meta_body` | text | yes | Primary text Meta runs. |
| `meta_title` | text | yes | Headline Meta runs. |
| `meta_desc` | text | yes | Link description Meta runs. |
| `transcript` | text | yes | Video transcript used by the taggers. |
| `transcript_seconds` | numeric | yes | Length of the transcribed audio. |
| `story_url` | text | yes | Facebook post link. |
| `is_video` | boolean | yes | true for video ads. |
| `ad_id` | text | yes | A Meta ad id of the family. |
| `video_id` | text | yes | Meta video id. |
| `source` | text | yes | 'cycle-2026-11-04', 'cycle-2026-11-04:whisper', 'cycle-2026-11-03:tracker' ... |

### public.creative_assets

*table*

**Purpose.** Creative thumbnails and video frames (image bytes) per tracker family, shown in the app. The only binary data in the database.

- **Grain:** primary key `(id)`; one row per (family_key, kind, idx)
- **Source:** etl.load_assets (tagging cycle media) and etl.assets_pull (Meta Graph thumbnails, best effort)
- **Refresh:** manual / best effort
- **Coverage:** 10.6K images
- **Caveat:** bytes holds the JPEG itself: never SELECT it casually.

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `id` | bigint | no | Primary key. |
| `family_key` | text | no | Tracker family key. |
| `kind` | text | no | 'thumb' \| 'frame'. |
| `idx` | integer | no | Frame index (0 for a thumb). |
| `mime` | text | no | Image mime type. |
| `width` | integer | yes | Pixel width. |
| `height` | integer | yes | Pixel height. |
| `bytes` | bytea | no | The image bytes. |
| `source` | text | yes | 'cycle-2026-11-03' \| 'meta-api' ... |
| `created_at` | timestamptz | no | When loaded. |

### public.creative_priorities_snapshots

*table*

**Purpose.** The weekly creative priorities one-sheeter: per product, the market cluster / ICP / pain point / story lines to iterate on (ads that work) and to produce for the first time, with the number of new concepts to brief and the numbers behind each row. One snapshot per week, kept as produced.

- **Grain:** primary key `(week_start)`; one row per week (week_start = the Sunday, Pacific)
- **Source:** etl.creative_priorities from ad_metrics, family_daily (and the _upstack_nc twin), creative_stage_moves, ad_tags (linemap), taxonomy_lines, line_ingredients, ad_hooks
- **Refresh:** Sundays, in the nightly etl (gated on Pacific time, non-fatal); by hand with python -m etl.creative_priorities [--week YYYY-MM-DD]; re-running a week replaces its snapshot
- **Coverage:** from the week the migration was deployed; one row per Sunday
- **Caveat:** Scored on Meta-reported numbers; the Upstack new-customer ROAS only raises a flag. Prioritisation, not a causal claim.
- **Caveat:** rows is a JSON array; each element carries section ('iterate' | 'new'), product, rank, the line (cluster, icp, pain_point, story), concepts, score, confidence, flags, why, metrics and hooks. Scoring and thresholds: docs/CREATIVE_PRIORITIES.md.

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `week_start` | date | no | The Sunday (Pacific) the snapshot belongs to (primary key). |
| `built_at` | timestamptz | no | When the snapshot was last written. |
| `data_through` | date | no | Last day of Meta data the snapshot used. |
| `basis` | text | no | Numbers basis of the scoring: 'meta' (Meta-reported). |
| `thresholds` | jsonb | no | JSON object: every threshold and weight constant of etl/creative_priorities.py as used for this snapshot. |
| `baselines` | jsonb | no | JSON object by product slug: the product's own averages (ROAS since launch / last 30 days, CPA, hit rate, new ads in 30 days) the lines are compared with. |
| `rows` | jsonb | no | JSON array of the ranked rows (iterate and new sections, all products). |
| `stats` | jsonb | no | JSON object: candidate counts, skipped counts, tagged and untagged spend, data_through. |

### public.creative_priorities_views

*table*

**Purpose.** Saved views of the creative priorities sheet: the rows someone selected on /priorities, frozen with the snapshot's baselines and legend, opened by an unguessable link /priorities/v/<token>.

- **Grain:** primary key `(id)`; one row per saved view (token is unique)
- **Source:** app/creative_priorities.py (Save view on /priorities), copied from creative_priorities_snapshots
- **Refresh:** written on demand by the app; never rebuilt
- **Coverage:** from the day migration 198 was applied
- **Caveat:** payload is a frozen copy: a view does not change when a later snapshot replaces its week. The link is the access (the app has no password).

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `id` | bigint | no | Surrogate key (primary key). |
| `token` | text | no | Unguessable link token (unique). |
| `name` | text | no | View name, '' when unnamed; shown as the page title and used in the CSV file name. |
| `week_start` | date | no | The snapshot week (Sunday, Pacific) the rows came from. |
| `keys` | text[] | no | Stable row keys of the selected rows. |
| `payload` | jsonb | no | JSON object: the selected rows plus the snapshot's baselines, legend and competitor as-of date. |
| `created_at` | timestamptz | no | When the view was saved. |
| `kind` | text | no | 'rows' (a fixed set of selected rows, shown with the latest numbers where the row still exists) or 'filters' (a saved filter state applied live to the current snapshot). |
| `filters` | jsonb | yes | JSON object for kind 'filters': products (slugs), sections (keys), q (search text), key and dir (sort). NULL for kind 'rows'. |
| `updated_at` | timestamptz | yes | When the view was last replaced by Update this view; NULL if never. |
| `briefed` | jsonb | no | JSON object: stable row key (product\|section\|cluster\|icp\|pain_point, or manual:<id>) -> {at, note} for the concepts marked briefed in this view; absent = not briefed. |

### public.creative_priorities_manual

*table*

**Purpose.** Concepts added by hand on /priorities (Add concept): product, market cluster, ICP, pain point, story, notes and a why-now line. Not tied to a weekly snapshot: a row stays on the sheet every week until it is marked done or deleted.

- **Grain:** primary key `(id)`; one row per manual concept
- **Source:** the /priorities page via POST /api/priorities/manual (app/priorities_manual.py)
- **Refresh:** on demand (a person adds, edits, marks done or deletes)
- **Coverage:** from the day the migration was deployed
- **Caveat:** No author column: the app has no login of its own, only timestamps.
- **Caveat:** A cluster / ICP / pain point / story that is not in the Communication Overview catalog is stored as typed and flagged with the new_* columns (a proposal, not a line of ours).

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `id` | bigint | no | Primary key (bigserial). |
| `product` | text | no | 'vita-bull' \| 'beef-liver' \| 'boneflex'. |
| `section` | text | no | Page section the concept sits in: 'iterate' \| 'faded' \| 'new' \| 'other'. |
| `cluster` | text | no | Market cluster (catalog value or typed). |
| `icp` | text | no | ICP (catalog value or typed). |
| `pain_point` | text | no | Pain point (catalog value or typed). |
| `story` | text | no | Story; empty when none. |
| `notes` | text | no | Free-text notes, up to 2,000 characters, line breaks kept. |
| `why_now` | text | no | Optional short why-now / what-to-test line, up to 300 characters. |
| `status` | text | no | 'open' (shown) or 'done' (hidden until Show done is ticked). |
| `new_cluster` | boolean | no | True when the cluster is not in the catalog for that product. |
| `new_icp` | boolean | no | True when the ICP is not in the catalog under that cluster. |
| `new_pain_point` | boolean | no | True when the pain point is not in the catalog under that ICP. |
| `new_story` | boolean | no | True when the story is typed text not in the catalog for that pain point. |
| `created_at` | timestamptz | no | When the concept was added. |
| `updated_at` | timestamptz | no | When it was last edited or its status changed. |
| `done_at` | timestamptz | yes | When it was marked done; NULL while open. |

### public.creative_priorities_assignments

*table*

**Purpose.** Production assignments from /priorities: a concept given to a vendor to make for a week (Assign to vendor), with the brief link, the creative link, the stage timestamps and the Creative Tracker Concept IDs of the ads that came out of it. The concept itself stays in the ideation list unchanged.

- **Grain:** primary key `(id)`; one row per concept x vendor x week (open rows are unique; a dropped row can be assigned again)
- **References:** `(format_code)` -> `public.creative_formats(code)`
- **Source:** the /priorities and /priorities/production pages and the vendor link page via /api/priorities/assignments (app/priorities_assign.py)
- **Refresh:** on demand (a person assigns, adds links, marks ready or launched, drops; a vendor adds a creative link and a note)
- **Coverage:** from the day the migration was deployed
- **Caveat:** The stage is not stored: it is derived at read time from the links, the timestamps and the status of the linked Creative Tracker rows (the tracker wins when it is further along).
- **Caveat:** Nothing here is written to the Google Sheet or Meta. The tag text is copied from the row at assign time, so history survives a taxonomy reload; line_id has no foreign key on purpose.
- **Caveat:** created_by and updated_by hold the signed-in user, or vendor:<name> for a write made on a vendor link page.

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `id` | bigint | no | Primary key (bigserial). |
| `concept_key` | text | no | Stable concept handle: 'line:<taxonomy_lines.id>' when the row has a line, 'manual:<id>' for a manual concept, else 'text:<product>\|<cluster>\|<icp>\|<pain point>' (lowercased, spaces collapsed). |
| `text_key` | text | no | The text form of the key ('text:<product>\|<cluster>\|<icp>\|<pain point>'), always stored, so a proposal that later becomes a real line still matches. |
| `product` | text | no | 'vita-bull' \| 'beef-liver' \| 'boneflex'. |
| `vendor` | text | no | Vendor, spelled as in the Creative Tracker Vendor column (Madre, Victoria, TrybeDCShop, TrybeOrion, 10xROAS, Internal, PuredMedia, CreateMastery, OpenProgram). |
| `line_id` | integer | yes | taxonomy_lines.id at assign time; NULL for manual or proposed concepts. No foreign key. |
| `manual_id` | bigint | yes | creative_priorities_manual.id for a manual concept; NULL otherwise. |
| `line_code` | text | no | Line code (for example BL12) at assign time; empty when none. |
| `cluster` | text | no | Market cluster at assign time. |
| `icp` | text | no | ICP at assign time. |
| `pain_point` | text | no | Pain point at assign time. |
| `story` | text | no | Story at assign time; empty when none. |
| `section_at_assign` | text | no | Action the concept had when assigned: 'revive' \| 'make_more' \| 'test' \| 'add' \| 'stop' \| 'hold' (rows written before 2026-11-10 hold the old section names 'faded' \| 'iterate' \| 'new' \| 'proposal' \| 'competitor' \| 'rest' \| 'other', shown as the new Action). |
| `note` | text | no | Note typed when assigning or changing the assignment. |
| `vendor_note` | text | no | Note typed by the vendor on their own link page. |
| `for_week` | date | no | The Monday of the week the vendor works on it (a CHECK enforces a Monday). |
| `brief_url` | text | yes | Link to the brief; NULL until added. |
| `creative_url` | text | yes | Link to the finished creative; NULL until added. |
| `brief_at` | timestamptz | yes | When the brief link was first added; NULL until then. |
| `creative_at` | timestamptz | yes | When the creative link was first added; NULL until then. |
| `ready_at` | timestamptz | yes | When someone clicked Ready to launch; NULL if not (or cleared). |
| `launched_at` | timestamptz | yes | When it was marked launched by hand; NULL if not. A linked tracker row with status 8 or 9 also counts as launched. |
| `dropped_at` | timestamptz | yes | When the assignment was dropped (soft cancel); NULL while active. Rows are never deleted. |
| `tracker_concept_ids` | text[] | no | Creative Tracker Concept IDs of the ads made from this assignment, entered by hand. |
| `created_at` | timestamptz | no | When the assignment was created. |
| `updated_at` | timestamptz | no | When it was last changed. |
| `created_by` | text | yes | Who created the assignment: the signed-in user name; NULL when none. |
| `updated_by` | text | yes | Who last changed it: the signed-in user name, or vendor:<name> for a write made on a vendor link page; NULL when none. |
| `format_code` | text | yes | Creative format chosen when assigning or later (creative_formats.code, foreign key); NULL when none was chosen. Set by a signed-in user only; a vendor sees it but cannot change it. |

### public.creative_priorities_assignment_events

*table*

**Purpose.** The history of each production assignment on /priorities: who assigned the concept to a vendor, and who moved it between stages (brief sent, creative delivered, ready to launch, launched, dropped) and when.

- **Grain:** primary key `(id)`; one row per change to an assignment
- **References:** `(assignment_id)` -> `public.creative_priorities_assignments(id)`
- **Source:** app/priorities_assign.py, written in the same transaction as the change (create, edit, vendor link page); rows before migration 242 rebuilt from the stage timestamps of creative_priorities_assignments
- **Refresh:** on every change made on /priorities/production or a vendor link page
- **Coverage:** every assignment from creation; for assignments older than migration 242 only the first time each stage was reached, with no person except the creator
- **Caveat:** actor is NULL for rebuilt history and when sign-in was off; a vendor link page writes vendor:<name>.
- **Caveat:** stage_before and stage_after are the stage the assignment itself shows (its links and Ready / Launched / Dropped buttons); a stage reached only because a linked Creative Tracker row moved on is not an event.

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `id` | bigint | no | Row number (primary key). |
| `assignment_id` | bigint | no | The assignment that changed (creative_priorities_assignments.id; rows go with the assignment). |
| `at` | timestamptz | no | When the change was made. |
| `actor` | text | yes | Who made it: the signed-in user name, vendor:<name> for a vendor link page, NULL when not known. |
| `kind` | text | no | What changed: assigned, brief_added, brief_removed, creative_added, creative_removed, ready, ready_cleared, launched, launched_cleared, dropped, restored, vendor, week or format. |
| `stage_before` | text | yes | Stage the assignment showed before the change (assigned, brief_sent, creative_in, ready, launched, dropped); NULL on the first row. |
| `stage_after` | text | yes | Stage it showed after the change. |
| `detail` | text | no | Short plain text about the change, for example 'Madre to Victoria'; empty when the kind says it all. |

### public.creative_formats

*table*

**Purpose.** The creative formats a production assignment can be made in (for example Whiteboard explainer): a short list that Josh picks from when assigning a concept to a vendor. Production side only; not part of the concept taxonomy.

- **Grain:** primary key `(code)`; one row per format (code is the primary key)
- **Source:** seeded by migration 204; edited by hand in the database (no page edits it); read by /api/priorities/assignments (app/priorities_assign.py)
- **Refresh:** on demand (a format is added or retired by hand)
- **Coverage:** six formats, F1 to F6, from the day the migration was deployed
- **Caveat:** A format with active = false stays on the assignments that already use it but can no longer be chosen.

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `code` | text | no | Short code (primary key), F1 to F6. |
| `name` | text | no | What the format is, shown in the pick list. |
| `length` | text | yes | Typical running time, as text (for example '45 to 90s'); NULL when not set. |
| `sort` | integer | no | Order in the pick list (ascending). |
| `active` | boolean | no | True while the format can be chosen on a new assignment. |

### public.creative_priorities_vendor_links

*table*

**Purpose.** One permanent unguessable link per vendor for the vendor page /priorities/vendor/<token>, where the vendor sees only their own concepts and adds a creative link and a note.

- **Grain:** primary key `(vendor)`; one row per vendor (vendor is the primary key)
- **Source:** the Production page via /api/priorities/vendor-links (app/priorities_assign.py)
- **Refresh:** on demand (a link is created on first use and replaced by Reset link)
- **Coverage:** from the day the migration was deployed
- **Caveat:** The link is the access: the page needs no login. Reset link replaces the token and the old link stops working.

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `vendor` | text | no | Vendor name (primary key), as in creative_priorities_assignments.vendor. |
| `token` | text | no | Unguessable link token (unique). |
| `created_at` | timestamptz | no | When the link was first created. |
| `rotated_at` | timestamptz | yes | When the token was last replaced by Reset link; NULL if never. |

### public.creative_launch_settings

*table*

**Purpose.** Per-product launch settings for creating paused Meta ads from /priorities production: where the ads go (account, campaign, template ad set), the budget and bid for a new ad set, the default Page, pixel, landing page, URL tags, call to action and the default ad copy.

- **Grain:** primary key `(product)`; one row per product (product is the primary key)
- **Source:** the launch module (app/priorities_launch.py) and the launch worker (settings screen)
- **Refresh:** on demand (edited by a person)
- **Coverage:** from the day the migration was deployed
- **Caveat:** No rows are seeded: the account and campaign ids are entered in the app before anything can launch.
- **Caveat:** Amounts are stored in cents; a launch always creates ads and ad sets PAUSED.

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `product` | text | no | Product the settings apply to (primary key), spelled as in creative_priorities_assignments.product. |
| `account_id` | text | yes | Meta ad account id the ads are created in. |
| `campaign_id` | text | yes | Campaign the new ad sets go into. |
| `template_adset_id` | text | yes | Existing ad set that a new ad set is cloned from (targeting, optimization, attribution). |
| `daily_budget_cents` | integer | no | Daily budget of a new ad set, in cents (default 10000 = $100). |
| `bid_amount_cents` | integer | no | Bid amount of a new ad set, in cents (default 6800 = $68). |
| `default_page_id` | text | yes | Facebook Page the ads run as when the draft does not name one. |
| `pixel_id` | text | yes | Meta pixel id for the product. |
| `landing_url` | text | yes | Landing page link the ads point to. |
| `url_tags` | text | yes | URL parameters appended to the link; NULL means the built-in default tags. |
| `cta_image` | text | no | Call to action for image ads (default LEARN_MORE). |
| `cta_video` | text | no | Call to action for video ads (default SHOP_NOW). |
| `primary_texts` | text[] | no | Default primary texts, used when neither the draft nor the best existing ad supplies any. |
| `headlines` | text[] | no | Default headlines, used when neither the draft nor the best existing ad supplies any. |
| `description` | text | yes | Default link description; NULL when none. |
| `updated_at` | timestamptz | no | When the settings were last changed. |
| `updated_by` | text | yes | Signed-in user who last changed them; NULL when none. |

### public.creative_launch_pages

*table*

**Purpose.** The Facebook Pages (with their Instagram accounts) an ad can run as when launching from /priorities production.

- **Grain:** primary key `(page_id)`; one row per Page (page_id is the primary key)
- **Source:** the launch module (app/priorities_launch.py) and the launch worker (settings screen)
- **Refresh:** on demand (edited by a person)
- **Coverage:** from the day the migration was deployed
- **Caveat:** name_token is the short text put at the end of the ad name for ads on this Page.

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `page_id` | text | no | Facebook Page id (primary key). |
| `name` | text | no | Page name shown in the launch screen. |
| `instagram_user_id` | text | yes | Instagram account id linked to the Page; NULL when none. |
| `name_token` | text | yes | Short token added to the ad name for this Page (for example lonvera). |
| `active` | boolean | no | True while the Page may be chosen; false hides it from the picker. |

### public.creative_launch_vendor_prefixes

*table*

**Purpose.** Concept ID prefix per vendor, used when the app mints Concept IDs for launched creative (for example Lonvera-BeefLiver-Static-A12).

- **Grain:** primary key `(vendor)`; one row per vendor (vendor is the primary key)
- **Source:** the launch module (app/priorities_launch.py) and the launch worker (settings screen); seeded by migration 203
- **Refresh:** on demand (edited by a person)
- **Coverage:** from the day the migration was deployed
- **Caveat:** Seeded with Madre = Lonvera, Victoria = Victoria, 10xROAS = 10x, Internal = Internal; a vendor with no row falls back to its name without punctuation.

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `vendor` | text | no | Vendor name (primary key), spelled as in creative_priorities_assignments.vendor. |
| `prefix` | text | no | Text put at the start of the Concept ID. |

### public.creative_launch_drafts

*table*

**Purpose.** The editable launch form for one assignment: the folder or file link, the tag values (Style, Fidelity, Type, Proof, Page) and the copy overrides, saved as the person types.

- **Grain:** primary key `(assignment_id)`; one row per assignment (assignment_id is the primary key)
- **References:** `(assignment_id)` -> `public.creative_priorities_assignments(id)`
- **Source:** the launch module (app/priorities_launch.py) and the launch worker (launch screen)
- **Refresh:** on demand (saved while a person edits)
- **Coverage:** from the day the migration was deployed
- **Caveat:** fields is free-form JSON validated on read; an unknown key is ignored.

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `assignment_id` | bigint | no | creative_priorities_assignments.id this draft belongs to (primary key, foreign key). |
| `fields` | jsonb | no | The draft as JSON (link, style, fidelity, type, proof, page, primary texts, headlines). |
| `updated_at` | timestamptz | no | When the draft was last saved. |
| `updated_by` | text | yes | Signed-in user who last saved it; NULL when none. |

### public.creative_launch_jobs

*table*

**Purpose.** One prepare or launch run for an assignment, with the exact plan it was approved against and its outcome.

- **Grain:** primary key `(id)`; one row per run (id is the primary key); at most one open (queued or running) run per assignment
- **References:** `(assignment_id)` -> `public.creative_priorities_assignments(id)`
- **Source:** the launch module (app/priorities_launch.py) and the launch worker
- **Refresh:** on demand (a person queues a run; the worker updates it)
- **Coverage:** from the day the migration was deployed
- **Caveat:** A launch only runs the plan whose plan_hash the person approved.
- **Caveat:** dry_run true means nothing is sent to Meta beyond validation.

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `id` | bigint | no | Primary key (bigserial). |
| `assignment_id` | bigint | no | creative_priorities_assignments.id the run is for (foreign key). |
| `kind` | text | no | 'prepare' (list files, mint IDs, build the plan) or 'launch' (create the paused ads). |
| `state` | text | no | 'queued' \| 'running' \| 'done' \| 'failed' \| 'cancelled'. |
| `plan` | jsonb | yes | The plan as JSON (one entry per ad plus the destination and PAUSED status). |
| `plan_hash` | text | yes | sha256 of the canonical plan JSON; the launch must carry the same hash that was approved. |
| `dry_run` | boolean | no | True when the run only validates and sends nothing live. |
| `requested_by` | text | yes | Signed-in user who queued the run. |
| `error` | text | yes | Scrubbed error text when the run failed; NULL otherwise. |
| `created_at` | timestamptz | no | When the run was queued. |
| `started_at` | timestamptz | yes | When the worker picked it up; NULL until then. |
| `finished_at` | timestamptz | yes | When it finished; NULL until then. |

### public.creative_launch_ads

*table*

**Purpose.** One row per file launched as an ad, with its Concept ID, ad name and the Meta ids created step by step, so a half-finished run can be resumed without duplicating anything.

- **Grain:** primary key `(id)`; one row per assignment x Drive file; Concept IDs are unique across all rows
- **References:** `(assignment_id)` -> `public.creative_priorities_assignments(id)`; `(job_id)` -> `public.creative_launch_jobs(id)`
- **Source:** the launch module (app/priorities_launch.py) and the launch worker
- **Refresh:** on demand (the worker updates the row after each Meta step)
- **Coverage:** from the day the migration was deployed
- **Caveat:** Concept IDs are minted once and never reused.
- **Caveat:** state moves planned, media_uploaded, creative_created, created; failed keeps the scrubbed error.

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `id` | bigint | no | Primary key (bigserial). |
| `assignment_id` | bigint | no | creative_priorities_assignments.id (foreign key). |
| `job_id` | bigint | yes | creative_launch_jobs.id of the run that last touched the row (foreign key); NULL when none. |
| `drive_file_id` | text | no | Google Drive file id of the source file. |
| `file_name` | text | no | Name of the source file. |
| `kind` | text | no | 'image' or 'video'. |
| `concept_id` | text | no | Concept ID minted for the ad (unique). |
| `ad_name` | text | no | Ad name sent to Meta (matches the Creative Tracker Name column format). |
| `state` | text | no | 'planned' \| 'media_uploaded' \| 'creative_created' \| 'created' \| 'failed'. |
| `meta_image_hash` | text | yes | Meta image hash after upload; NULL for videos or before upload. |
| `meta_video_id` | text | yes | Meta video id after upload; NULL for images or before upload. |
| `meta_creative_id` | text | yes | Meta ad creative id; NULL until created. |
| `meta_ad_id` | text | yes | Meta ad id; NULL until created. |
| `meta_adset_id` | text | yes | Ad set the ad was created in; NULL until created. |
| `error` | text | yes | Scrubbed error text when the step failed; NULL otherwise. |
| `created_at` | timestamptz | no | When the row was planned. |
| `updated_at` | timestamptz | no | When the row last changed. |

### public.creative_launch_adsets

*table*

**Purpose.** The paused ad set the launch created for a product, vendor and week, so later launches of the same combination reuse it.

- **Grain:** primary key `(product, vendor, for_week)`; one row per product x vendor x week (the primary key)
- **Source:** the launch module (app/priorities_launch.py) and the launch worker
- **Refresh:** on demand (written when an ad set is first created)
- **Coverage:** from the day the migration was deployed
- **Caveat:** Ad set name is <Product>_<Vendor>_<week>_Production.

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `product` | text | no | Product (part of the primary key). |
| `vendor` | text | no | Vendor (part of the primary key). |
| `for_week` | date | no | Monday of the week the work is for (part of the primary key). |
| `meta_adset_id` | text | no | Meta ad set id created for the combination. |
| `created_at` | timestamptz | no | When the ad set was created. |

### public.creative_launch_audit

*table*

**Purpose.** Append-only log of launch actions: settings changed, plan prepared, launch requested, ads created, cancelled.

- **Grain:** primary key `(id)`; one row per action (id is the primary key)
- **Source:** the launch module (app/priorities_launch.py) and the launch worker
- **Refresh:** on demand (a row per action)
- **Coverage:** from the day the migration was deployed
- **Caveat:** Never updated or deleted.

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `id` | bigint | no | Primary key (bigserial). |
| `at` | timestamptz | no | When the action happened. |
| `actor` | text | yes | Signed-in user or worker name. |
| `action` | text | no | Short action name. |
| `assignment_id` | bigint | yes | Assignment the action concerns; NULL when none. No foreign key, so the log outlives its subject. |
| `detail` | jsonb | yes | Action details as JSON, never containing a token. |

## Derived creative-family roll-ups (rebuilt in full by etl.build_metrics)

### public.family_daily

*table*

**Purpose.** ad_daily re-keyed to the TRACKER family (through meta_family_map) and split by campaign stage. Powers any date window in the app without scanning ad_daily.

- **Grain:** primary key `(family_key, day, stage)`; one row per (family_key, day, stage)
- **Source:** etl.build_metrics from ad_daily + meta_ads + meta_family_map + meta_campaigns (Meta-reported)
- **Refresh:** nightly: TRUNCATE and rebuild in the same transaction as the Meta pull
- **Coverage:** 2026-05-06 through yesterday; only Meta families that map to a tracker family
- **Caveat:** Meta families with no tracker match are NOT here (see unmatched_meta), so this table does not sum to the account's spend.
- **Caveat:** A family mapped by two Meta families sums both. The table is derived: query ad_daily or analytics.meta_ad_daily for account totals.
- **Caveat:** Traffic columns: clicks_tr and spend_tr are the base for CTR / CPC / CPM: clicks_tr = link clicks on rows that carry impressions, spend_tr = spend on those rows.

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `family_key` | text | no | Tracker family key (lower). |
| `day` | date | no | Day (Meta ad account day). |
| `stage` | text | no | 'testing' \| 'scaling' \| 'unclassified' (days with no campaign). |
| `spend` | numeric(14,2) | no | Meta spend, dollars. |
| `purchases` | numeric(12,2) | no | Meta-reported purchases. |
| `revenue` | numeric(14,2) | no | Meta-reported purchase value, dollars. |
| `link_clicks` | numeric(12,0) | no | Link clicks. |
| `impressions` | numeric(14,0) | no | Impressions over rows that carry them. |
| `clicks_tr` | numeric(12,0) | no | Link clicks on rows with impressions (CTR numerator). |
| `spend_tr` | numeric(14,2) | no | Spend on rows with impressions (CPC / CPM base). |

*Daily spend by stage of the top-spending creative family*

```sql
SELECT day, stage, round(spend, 2) AS spend, purchases FROM family_daily WHERE family_key = (SELECT family_key FROM family_daily GROUP BY 1 ORDER BY sum(spend) DESC LIMIT 1) ORDER BY day DESC, stage LIMIT 30;
```

### public.family_daily_active

*table*, columns like `public.family_daily`

**Purpose.** family_daily restricted to Meta ads whose CURRENT status is ACTIVE ('what the currently active ads did').

- **Grain:** primary key `(family_key, day, stage)`; one row per (family_key, day, stage)
- **Source:** etl.build_metrics (ad_daily joined to ACTIVE meta_ads)
- **Refresh:** nightly rebuild
- **Coverage:** 2.4K rows: only ads active now (about 441), so recent days mostly
- **Caveat:** Current status is applied to the whole history of the ad.

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `family_key` | text | no | Tracker family key (lower). |
| `day` | date | no | Day (Meta ad account day). |
| `stage` | text | no | 'testing' \| 'scaling' \| 'unclassified' (days with no campaign). |
| `spend` | numeric(14,2) | no | Meta spend, dollars. |
| `purchases` | numeric(12,2) | no | Meta-reported purchases. |
| `revenue` | numeric(14,2) | no | Meta-reported purchase value, dollars. |
| `link_clicks` | numeric(12,0) | no | Link clicks. |
| `impressions` | numeric(14,0) | no | Impressions over rows that carry them. |
| `clicks_tr` | numeric(12,0) | no | Link clicks on rows with impressions (CTR numerator). |
| `spend_tr` | numeric(14,2) | no | Spend on rows with impressions (CPC / CPM base). |

*Examples: same shape as `public.family_daily`; query it the same way.*

### public.ad_metrics

*table*

**Purpose.** One row per tracker row with the family's since-launch and trailing-window metrics, carried by the FIRST row of the family (others read 0). Behind the Tags tables of the app.

- **Grain:** primary key `(tracker_row_id)`; one row per tracker_row_id
- **References:** `(tracker_row_id)` -> `public.tracker_rows(id)`
- **Source:** etl.build_metrics from meta_ad_lifetime + family_daily + meta_family_map + meta_ads (Meta-reported)
- **Refresh:** nightly: TRUNCATE and rebuild
- **Coverage:** 3,980 rows, 3,889 first rows
- **Caveat:** Metrics sit on the first row of each family_key across ALL products (the tracker row whose product matches the family name, else a real product over 'unassigned', else the lowest sheet_row); the other rows read 0. Rows with a blank ad family ('id:...') always read 0 because their spend cannot be matched.
- **Caveat:** Since launch = Meta all-time lifetime per ad summed over the family's Meta ads: no cutoff rule and no launch-date gate. The *_7d / *_30d columns end on the last complete day.
- **Caveat:** The *_active columns are the same figures over currently ACTIVE ads only.

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `tracker_row_id` | bigint | no | tracker_rows.id (primary key). |
| `product_id` | integer | no | products.id. |
| `family_key` | text | no | Tracker family key. |
| `is_first_row` | boolean | no | true on the row that carries the family's metrics. |
| `launch_date` | date | yes | Launch date of the row (tracker). |
| `spend_launch` | numeric(14,2) | no | Since-launch spend, dollars. |
| `purchases_launch` | numeric(12,2) | no | Since-launch Meta-reported purchases (rounded to whole numbers). |
| `revenue_launch` | numeric(14,2) | no | Since-launch Meta-reported purchase value, dollars. |
| `spend_7d` | numeric(14,2) | no | Spend over the 7 days ending the last complete day. |
| `purchases_7d` | numeric(12,2) | no | Purchases over those 7 days. |
| `revenue_7d` | numeric(14,2) | no | Purchase value over those 7 days. |
| `spend_30d` | numeric(14,2) | no | Spend over the 30 days ending the last complete day. |
| `purchases_30d` | numeric(12,2) | no | Purchases over those 30 days. |
| `revenue_30d` | numeric(14,2) | no | Purchase value over those 30 days. |
| `live` | boolean | no | true when the family spent on either of the last two days. |
| `built_at` | timestamptz | no | When the table was built. |
| `impressions_launch` | numeric(14,0) | no | Since-launch impressions. |
| `clicks_launch` | numeric(12,0) | no | Since-launch link clicks. |
| `ads_active_count` | integer | no | ACTIVE Meta ads of the family (same on every row of the family). |
| `spend_launch_active` | numeric(14,2) | no | Since-launch spend of the ACTIVE ads only. |
| `purchases_launch_active` | numeric(12,2) | no | Since-launch purchases of the ACTIVE ads only. |
| `revenue_launch_active` | numeric(14,2) | no | Since-launch purchase value of the ACTIVE ads only. |
| `impressions_launch_active` | numeric(14,0) | no | Since-launch impressions of the ACTIVE ads only. |
| `clicks_launch_active` | numeric(12,0) | no | Since-launch link clicks of the ACTIVE ads only. |
| `live_active` | boolean | no | true when the ACTIVE ads of the family spent on either of the last two days. |

*Since-launch spend and Meta ROAS per product (first rows only, so each family counts once)*

```sql
SELECT p.slug, count(*) AS unique_ads, round(sum(m.spend_launch)) AS spend, round(sum(m.revenue_launch) / NULLIF(sum(m.spend_launch), 0), 2) AS roas FROM ad_metrics m JOIN products p ON p.id = m.product_id WHERE m.is_first_row GROUP BY 1 ORDER BY 3 DESC;
```

### public.creative_stage_moves

*table*

**Purpose.** One row per creative (tracker family) that ever spent: the first day it spent in a Testing campaign and in a Scaling campaign, whether it MOVED from testing to scaling (testing spend, then a later first scaling day), days in test, its numbers in testing before the move and in scaling since, the last 7 days and whether it is live. Behind the Test to scale page. Meta's own purchases and revenue; the _upstack_nc / _upstack_all tables read the Upstack attribution. Precomputed (migration 016; it was a view in 015).

- **Grain:** primary key `(family_key)`; one row per family_key that has spend (family_daily rows with spend > 0)
- **Source:** refresh_creative_stage_moves() from public.family_daily (stage per campaign name: 'test' = testing) + ad_metrics first row + products + ad_tags + taxonomy_lines
- **Refresh:** nightly (etl.stage_moves, after run_daily and upstack_daily): DELETE + INSERT in one transaction; app_meta stage_moves_built_at / stage_moves_data_through say when
- **Coverage:** 2026-05-06 through yesterday; about 2.6K creatives, 610 moved on 2026-11-04
- **Caveat:** A move is inferred from daily spend, not from an event: Meta cannot move an ad between campaigns, the buyer duplicates the creative into a scaling campaign. Testing and scaling on the same first day, scaling first and scaling only are NOT moves (see path).
- **Caveat:** Bulk moves: on 2026-08-22, 2026-10-22 and 2026-10-23 76, 187 and 80 creatives moved in one day (mass relaunches into new scaling campaigns, not test winners). bulk_move_day marks days with 40 or more moves; the app lets you hide them.
- **Caveat:** first_day is the first day with spend in any stage: creatives already running on the first day of data (launched_before_data_start, 2026-05-06) have an earlier real launch day, so days_in_test is a lower bound.
- **Caveat:** test_* counts TESTING-stage rows on days before the first scaling day (all testing days when it never scaled); scale_* counts SCALING-stage rows since the move through the last day. Testing spend that continues after the move is test_after_spend only.
- **Caveat:** Spend and link clicks are Meta's in every view; purchases and revenue are Meta-reported here, Upstack's in the _upstack_ views. Not additive across platforms.

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `family_key` | text | no | Tracker family key (lower); joins family_daily, ad_metrics, creative_assets. |
| `tracker_row_id` | bigint | yes | tracker_rows.id of the family's first row (the one that carries its metrics); the app opens /ad/<id>. |
| `product_id` | integer | yes | products.id of that row (NULL when the family has no first row). |
| `product_slug` | text | yes | Product slug ('unassigned' for creatives of no product). |
| `product_name` | text | yes | Product name. |
| `concept_id` | text | yes | Concept id of the first tracker row. |
| `display` | text | yes | Display name: the ad family as typed in the tracker, else the concept id. |
| `ad_type` | text | yes | VID / IMG (tracker). |
| `tracker_status` | text | yes | Tracker status of the first row, e.g. '8 - Launched'. |
| `tracker_launch_date` | date | yes | Launch date typed in the tracker (undercounts the older ads; first_day is the one the page uses). |
| `cluster` | text | yes | Market cluster of the current tag ('(untagged)' when none). |
| `icp` | text | yes | ICP of the current tag. |
| `pain` | text | yes | Pain point of the current tag. |
| `data_start` | date | yes | First day this view's family_daily holds (2026-05-06 today). |
| `data_through` | date | yes | Last day this view's family_daily holds. |
| `path` | text | yes | moved \| testing_only \| scaling_only \| same_day (testing and scaling both first spent that day) \| scaling_first (scaling before testing) \| unclassified_only. |
| `began_in_testing` | boolean | yes | true for moved and testing_only: the creatives a test-to-scale rate is about. |
| `moved` | boolean | yes | true when path = 'moved': spent in testing and its first scaling day is later. |
| `first_day` | date | yes | First day with spend in any stage = the launch day as far as the data goes. |
| `testing_first_day` | date | yes | First day with spend in a Testing campaign (NULL if none). |
| `scaling_first_day` | date | yes | First day with spend in a Scaling campaign (NULL if none): the day of the move for a moved creative. |
| `last_spend_day` | date | yes | Last day with spend in any stage. |
| `scaling_last_day` | date | yes | Last day with spend in a Scaling campaign. |
| `days_in_test` | integer | yes | scaling_first_day - testing_first_day, for moved creatives only. |
| `launched_before_data_start` | boolean | yes | true when first_day is the first day of data: the creative was already running and its real launch day is earlier. |
| `test_spend` | numeric | yes | Testing-stage spend on days before the first scaling day, dollars. |
| `test_purchases` | numeric | yes | Purchases (Meta-reported here, Upstack in the _upstack_ views) on those days. |
| `test_revenue` | numeric | yes | Purchase value on those days, dollars. |
| `test_link_clicks` | numeric | yes | Link clicks on those days. |
| `test_days` | bigint | yes | Days with testing spend before the first scaling day. |
| `test_after_spend` | numeric | yes | Testing-stage spend on or after the first scaling day (the original kept running), dollars. |
| `scale_spend` | numeric | yes | Scaling-stage spend since the move, through the last day, dollars. |
| `scale_purchases` | numeric | yes | Purchases in the Scaling stage. |
| `scale_revenue` | numeric | yes | Purchase value in the Scaling stage, dollars. |
| `scale_link_clicks` | numeric | yes | Link clicks in the Scaling stage. |
| `scale_days` | bigint | yes | Days with scaling spend. |
| `unclassified_spend` | numeric | yes | Spend on days with no campaign (stage 'unclassified'), dollars; none today. |
| `last7_spend` | numeric | yes | Spend over the last 7 days of data, any stage, dollars. |
| `last7_purchases` | numeric | yes | Purchases over the last 7 days, any stage. |
| `last7_revenue` | numeric | yes | Purchase value over the last 7 days, any stage, dollars. |
| `last7_scale_spend` | numeric | yes | Scaling-stage spend over the last 7 days, dollars. |
| `last7_scale_purchases` | numeric | yes | Scaling-stage purchases over the last 7 days. |
| `last7_scale_revenue` | numeric | yes | Scaling-stage purchase value over the last 7 days, dollars. |
| `live` | boolean | yes | true when the creative spent on the last day of data or the day before (any stage). |
| `live_scaling` | boolean | yes | true when it spent in a Scaling campaign on the last day of data or the day before. |
| `moves_that_day` | bigint | yes | Creatives that moved on the same scaling_first_day (all creatives, not only this product). |
| `bulk_move_day` | boolean | yes | true when moved and 40 or more creatives moved that day: a mass relaunch, not a test winner. |

*Creatives that moved to scaling in the last 30 days, with testing and scaling ROAS*

```sql
SELECT display, product_slug, first_day, scaling_first_day, days_in_test, round(test_spend, 2) AS test_spend,
       round(test_revenue / NULLIF(test_spend, 0), 2) AS test_roas, round(scale_spend, 2) AS scale_spend,
       round(scale_revenue / NULLIF(scale_spend, 0), 2) AS scale_roas, bulk_move_day
FROM creative_stage_moves
WHERE moved AND scaling_first_day > data_through - 30
ORDER BY scale_spend DESC LIMIT 20;
```

*Moves per day and which days were bulk (mass relaunch) days*

```sql
SELECT scaling_first_day AS day, count(*) AS moves, bool_or(bulk_move_day) AS bulk
FROM creative_stage_moves
WHERE moved
GROUP BY 1 ORDER BY 2 DESC LIMIT 10;
```

### public.creative_scaling_placements

*table*

**Purpose.** Where each MOVED creative scales: one row per (creative, scaling campaign, ad set) with the spend, purchases and revenue of its ads there since the move, and how many ads. Behind the ad-set list of the Test to scale page.

- **Grain:** primary key `(family_key, campaign_id, adset_id)`; one row per (family_key, campaign_id, adset_id) for creatives with creative_stage_moves.moved
- **Source:** refresh_creative_stage_moves() from ad_daily x meta_ads x meta_campaigns (stage 'scaling') x meta_family_map, days on or after the creative's first scaling day
- **Refresh:** nightly (etl.stage_moves), with creative_stage_moves
- **Coverage:** 2026-05-06 through yesterday; about 600 moved creatives
- **Caveat:** Spend adds up, per creative, to creative_stage_moves.scale_spend (spend > 0 days only; zero-spend days with purchases are not here).
- **Caveat:** adset_id / adset are '' for ad_daily rows whose ad has no meta_ads row. Meta-reported purchases / revenue here; the _upstack_ tables hold Upstack's credit.

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `family_key` | text | no | Tracker family key (creative_stage_moves.family_key). |
| `campaign_id` | text | no | Meta scaling campaign id. |
| `campaign` | text | no | Campaign name. |
| `adset_id` | text | no | Meta ad set id ('' when unknown). |
| `adset` | text | no | Ad set name. |
| `ads` | integer | no | Distinct Meta ads of the creative that spent in this ad set since the move. |
| `spend` | numeric | no | Spend since the move, dollars. |
| `purchases` | numeric | no | Purchases (Meta-reported here) since the move. |
| `revenue` | numeric | no | Purchase value since the move, dollars. |
| `first_day` | date | no | First day with spend there. |
| `last_day` | date | no | Last day with spend there. |
