### public.scaling_benchmarks

*table*

**Purpose.** What the Scaling campaigns spent and returned over the last 30 days of data, per product and Numbers basis: the benchmark the Test to scale page holds a testing creative against ('What should be scaled': is its testing ROAS or CPA at least as good as its product's scaling average?).

- **Grain:** primary key `(basis, product_slug)`; one row per (basis, product_slug) with scaling spend in the 30 days
- **Source:** refresh_scaling_benchmarks() from public.family_daily (stage 'scaling', and its _upstack_nc / _upstack_all twins) x ad_metrics first row x products
- **Refresh:** nightly (etl.stage_moves, same transaction as creative_stage_moves): DELETE + INSERT
- **Coverage:** 3 bases x up to 4 products (incl. 'none'); 30 days ending on the last day of family_daily
- **Caveat:** All scaling spend of the product counts, including creatives that moved in a mass relaunch and creatives that never tested.
- **Caveat:** product_slug 'none' = creatives of no active product (products.slug 'unassigned' or no tracker row). Spend is Meta's in every basis; purchases and revenue are Upstack's in the upstack_* bases (not additive across bases).

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `basis` | text | no | 'meta' (Meta's own reporting), 'upstack_nc' (Upstack new customers) or 'upstack_all' (Upstack all orders). |
| `product_slug` | text | no | products.slug of the creative's product, or 'none'. |
| `day_from` | date | no | First day of the 30-day window (day_to - 29). |
| `day_to` | date | no | Last day of family_daily: the window ends there. |
| `creatives` | integer | no | Creatives (tracker families) with scaling spend in the window. |
| `spend` | numeric | no | Scaling spend in the window, dollars. |
| `purchases` | numeric | no | Purchases in Scaling campaigns in the window (the basis's meaning: Meta purchases, Upstack new customers, or Upstack orders). |
| `revenue` | numeric | no | Purchase value in the window, dollars (the basis's meaning). |

### public.competitor_brands

*table*

**Purpose.** The competitor brands whose Meta ads are watched weekly (Heart & Soil, Primal Viking, My Marrow ...): exact search phrases for the public Ad Library, the advertiser-name regex that keeps only the brand's own page, and the freshness of the last scrape. Editable: insert / update rows to add or retire a brand (etl.competitor_ads only seeds missing brands).

- **Grain:** primary key `(brand)`; one row per brand
- **Source:** seeded by etl.competitor_ads (BRANDS constant), edited by hand; last_* columns written by each run
- **Refresh:** weekly (Sundays Pacific, etl.competitor_ads)
- **Coverage:** 18 brands seeded 2026-11-09
- **Caveat:** last_error is set when the brand failed in the latest run (blocked, age-gated, layout change); its rows are then carried forward and flagged stale in competitor_concepts.
- **Caveat:** Brands whose ads are age-gated for logged-out visitors (Hims, Keeps, Men's Edge, Reclaim Co) cannot be read and are not seeded.

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `brand` | text | no | Brand name (primary key), as shown in the views. |
| `product_type` | text | no | Free text: what the brand sells (beef organ capsules, joint supplement ...). |
| `search_phrases` | text[] | no | Exact phrases searched in the Ad Library (brand name, landing domain); results are filtered by name_match. |
| `name_match` | text | no | Case-insensitive regex the advertiser (page) name must match; creator ads named 'X with Brand' are dropped. |
| `active` | boolean | no | False retires the brand without deleting its history. |
| `last_attempt_at` | timestamptz | yes | Last time a run tried this brand. |
| `last_ok_at` | timestamptz | yes | Last time the brand loaded without error. |
| `last_error` | text | yes | Reason the last attempt failed; NULL after a good run. |
| `last_ad_count` | integer | yes | Active US ads read in the last good run (a top slice by impressions: capped at about 150 cards per phrase). |

### public.competitor_ads

*table*

**Purpose.** Weekly snapshot of the ACTIVE US Meta ads of each watched competitor, one row per Library ID, read from the public Meta Ad Library with headless Chromium. No spend, impressions or performance exist for these ads: days_running and variant_count are the only strength signals.

- **Grain:** primary key `(week_start, library_id)`; one row per (week_start, library_id)
- **References:** `(brand)` -> `public.competitor_brands(brand)`
- **Source:** etl.competitor_ads: public Ad Library cards (exact-phrase search, impressions-sorted, advertiser filtered)
- **Refresh:** weekly, Sundays Pacific; a failed brand has its previous week copied forward (carried_from set)
- **Coverage:** from 2026-11-09; about 100-150 ads per large brand (a top slice, not a census)
- **Caveat:** The Library lazy-loads: each phrase returns at most about 150 cards, so large brands (Heart & Soil about 1,000 ads) are a top slice by impressions.
- **Caveat:** start_date resets when a brand re-uploads an ad, so days_running understates the age of re-uploaded creatives.
- **Caveat:** format is 'video' when the card has a video, else 'image' (carousels are not distinguishable from images). hook is the first sentence of the copy (video speech and on-image text are not read).
- **Caveat:** Cards with 'N ads use this creative and text' count N in variant_count.

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `week_start` | date | no | Monday (Pacific) of the week of the run. |
| `library_id` | text | no | Meta Ad Library ID. |
| `brand` | text | no | competitor_brands.brand. |
| `advertiser` | text | yes | Page name shown on the card. |
| `start_date` | date | yes | 'Started running on' date (resets on re-upload). |
| `days_running` | integer | yes | seen_on minus start_date. |
| `variant_count` | integer | no | Ads sharing this creative and text (card says 'N ads use this creative and text'), else 1. |
| `format` | text | yes | 'video' or 'image'. |
| `primary_text` | text | yes | Ad copy as shown (up to 2,500 characters). |
| `headline` | text | yes | Card headline (first line after the landing domain that is not a button label). |
| `hook` | text | yes | First sentence of the copy, else the headline. |
| `landing_url` | text | yes | Landing domain and path (tracking query removed). |
| `concept_key` | text | no | Brand + hash of the first 160 characters of normalised copy: groups near-duplicate copy into one concept. |
| `seen_on` | date | no | Date the Library was read (Pacific); for carried rows the original date. |
| `carried_from` | date | yes | NULL for fresh rows; for carried-forward rows the week_start they were copied from. |

### public.competitor_concept_class

*table*

**Purpose.** Lonvera taxonomy classification of each competitor concept (concept_key), made once by headless Claude from the copy text and cached, so only new concepts use plan usage: an existing line (product / cluster / ICP / pain point from the autotag catalogs), a NEW angle with suggested cluster / ICP / pain point, or out of category.

- **Grain:** primary key `(concept_key)`; one row per concept_key
- **Source:** etl.competitor_ads classification step (claude -p, text only, batches of 8 concepts, catalogs etl/autotag/catalogs/*.json)
- **Refresh:** weekly; concepts already classified are never re-asked
- **Coverage:** from 2026-11-09
- **Caveat:** Text only: the model never sees the image or video, so low confidence is common for statics whose message is on the image.
- **Caveat:** line_code is NULL for NEW angles (cluster / icp / pain_point hold the suggestion) and for out-of-category ads (mapped_product 'none').

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `concept_key` | text | no | competitor_ads.concept_key (primary key). |
| `brand` | text | no | Brand. |
| `mapped_product` | text | yes | 'vita-bull' \| 'beef-liver' \| 'boneflex' \| 'none': the Lonvera product the angle competes with. |
| `line_code` | text | yes | Catalog line code (L.. Vital Bull, B.. Beef Liver, BF.. Boneflex) or NULL. |
| `cluster` | text | yes | Market cluster (catalog value, or suggested for NEW). |
| `icp` | text | yes | ICP (catalog value, or suggested for NEW). |
| `pain_point` | text | yes | Pain point (catalog value, or suggested for NEW). |
| `confidence` | text | yes | high \| medium \| low. |
| `is_new_angle` | boolean | no | True when no catalog line covers the angle. |
| `new_angle_label` | text | yes | 4-8 word label of a NEW angle. |
| `why` | text | yes | The model's reason, 15 words or fewer. |
| `model` | text | yes | Model id used. |
| `classified_at` | timestamptz | no | When it was classified. |

### public.competitor_concepts

*view*

**Purpose.** What competitors run on Meta right now, grouped by concept (near-duplicate copy), with the Lonvera line each maps to. The contract read by the weekly Creative priorities one-sheeter. Latest week: WHERE week_start = (SELECT max(week_start) FROM competitor_concepts).

- **Grain:** one row per (week_start, brand, concept_key)
- **Source:** public.competitor_ads x competitor_brands x competitor_concept_class
- **Refresh:** weekly (Sundays Pacific)
- **Coverage:** from 2026-11-09
- **Caveat:** No spend, impressions or ROAS: days_running (max across the concept's ads) and variant_count (ads sharing the creative) are proxies, and start dates reset on re-upload.
- **Caveat:** is_stale = the brand failed to load this week and the rows are the last good snapshot carried forward, or the data is older than 8 days.
- **Caveat:** Unclassified concepts show confidence 'low' and NULL mapped_product / line_code. For NEW angles cluster / icp / pain_point are the model's suggestion.

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `week_start` | date |  | Monday (Pacific) of the run week. |
| `brand` | text |  | Brand. |
| `concept_key` | text |  | Concept (near-duplicate copy of one brand). |
| `first_ad_start_date` | date |  | Earliest start date among the concept's ads. |
| `days_running` | integer |  | Max days running among the concept's ads (as of as_of). |
| `variant_count` | integer |  | Ads sharing the creative / copy, summed over the concept's cards. |
| `format` | text |  | 'video' or 'image' of the longest-running ad. |
| `hook` | text |  | First sentence of the longest-running ad's copy. |
| `primary_text_excerpt` | text |  | First 280 characters of that ad's copy. |
| `mapped_product` | text |  | 'vita-bull' \| 'beef-liver' \| 'boneflex' \| 'none' \| NULL. |
| `line_code` | text |  | Lonvera catalog line, NULL for NEW / out-of-category / unclassified. |
| `cluster` | text |  | Market cluster. |
| `icp` | text |  | ICP. |
| `pain_point` | text |  | Pain point. |
| `confidence` | text |  | high \| medium \| low (unclassified = low). |
| `is_new_angle` | boolean |  | No catalog line covers it. |
| `new_angle_label` | text |  | Label of the NEW angle. |
| `as_of` | date |  | Date the Library was read. |
| `is_stale` | boolean |  | Carried forward or older than 8 days. |

### public.unmatched_meta

*table*

**Purpose.** Meta families that map to no live tracker family, with the product guessed for them and their windowed and since-launch numbers. Closes the gap between ad_metrics and the account total.

- **Grain:** primary key `(meta_family_key)`; one row per Meta family (meta_family_key)
- **Source:** etl.build_metrics from meta_ads, ad_daily, meta_ad_lifetime, meta_campaigns, products.meta_regex
- **Refresh:** nightly: TRUNCATE and rebuild
- **Coverage:** 12K families (most never delivered since 2026-05-06; spend_launch can still be > 0)
- **Caveat:** product_id is a guess: from the family name (product_source 'name'), else the campaign of the family's biggest ad ('campaign'), else the ad set name ('adset'), else 'none' (the 'unassigned' product).
- **Caveat:** Windows end on the last complete day. spend_all / purchases_all / revenue_all are over the days held in ad_daily (2026-05-06 on).

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `meta_family_key` | text | no | lower(Meta ad family) (primary key). |
| `meta_family` | text | no | Display name (the variant with the highest lifetime spend). |
| `product_id` | integer | no | Guessed products.id. |
| `spend_7d` | numeric(14,2) | no | Spend over the last 7 days, dollars. |
| `purchases_7d` | numeric(12,2) | no | Purchases over the last 7 days. |
| `revenue_7d` | numeric(14,2) | no | Purchase value over the last 7 days, dollars. |
| `spend_30d` | numeric(14,2) | no | Spend over the last 30 days, dollars. |
| `purchases_30d` | numeric(12,2) | no | Purchases over the last 30 days. |
| `revenue_30d` | numeric(14,2) | no | Purchase value over the last 30 days, dollars. |
| `spend_all` | numeric(14,2) | no | Spend over every day held in ad_daily, dollars. |
| `purchases_all` | numeric(12,2) | no | Purchases over every day held. |
| `revenue_all` | numeric(14,2) | no | Purchase value over every day held, dollars. |
| `live` | boolean | no | true when the family spent on either of the last two days. |
| `spend_launch` | numeric(14,2) | no | All-time lifetime spend (Meta API), dollars. |
| `purchases_launch` | numeric(12,2) | no | All-time lifetime purchases. |
| `revenue_launch` | numeric(14,2) | no | All-time lifetime purchase value, dollars. |
| `impressions_launch` | numeric(14,0) | no | All-time impressions. |
| `clicks_launch` | numeric(12,0) | no | All-time link clicks. |
| `product_source` | text | no | name \| campaign \| adset \| none. |

*Spend of Meta families that sit on no tracker row, by guessed product*

```sql
SELECT p.slug, count(*) AS families, round(sum(u.spend_30d)) AS spend_30d FROM unmatched_meta u JOIN products p ON p.id = u.product_id GROUP BY 1 ORDER BY 3 DESC;
```

- **Source:** etl.build_metrics.rebuild_source('nc')

- **Coverage:** 3.2K families
- **Caveat:** purchases_* / revenue_* are Upstack's credit on this basis; spend is Meta's.

| `live` | boolean | no | true when the family spent on either of the last two days. |
| `spend_launch` | numeric(14,2) | no | All-time lifetime spend (Meta API), dollars. |
| `purchases_launch` | numeric(12,2) | no | All-time lifetime purchases. |
| `revenue_launch` | numeric(14,2) | no | All-time lifetime purchase value, dollars. |

## Pipeline bookkeeping

### public.alert_state

*table*

**Purpose.** De-duplication state of the failure and staleness alerts sent to Slack by etl.alerts: one row per check (a job that failed, a table that went stale, the web health check ...), so the job alerts once when a problem appears, repeats every 24 hours while it lasts, and sends one 'recovered' line when it clears.

- **Grain:** primary key `(check_id)`; one row per check id
- **Source:** etl.alerts (hourly Railway cron service `alerts`)
- **Refresh:** hourly
- **Coverage:** from the first run of the alerts service
- **Caveat:** resolved_at is NULL while the problem is open; a problem that comes back reopens the same row (first_seen restarts, alert_count restarts).
- **Caveat:** last_alerted is NULL when the problem was seen but no message could be sent (for example SLACK_ALERT_WEBHOOK_URL is not set yet): the alert goes out on the first run after the webhook works.

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `check_id` | text | no | Stable id of the check, for example job:run_daily, fresh:meta, web:health (primary key). |
| `first_seen` | timestamptz | no | When the current problem was first seen. |
| `last_seen` | timestamptz | no | Last run that still saw the problem (or the run that saw it clear, when resolved). |
| `last_alerted` | timestamptz | yes | When Slack was last told about it; NULL if never delivered. |
| `resolved_at` | timestamptz | yes | When the problem cleared; NULL while open. |
| `alert_count` | integer | no | Messages sent for the current problem. |
| `fingerprint` | text | yes | Short text that changes when the problem changes shape (for example another feed started failing); a change re-alerts at once. |
| `last_message` | text | yes | Text of the last alert (what broke, since when, how to fix). |

### public.etl_runs

*table*

**Purpose.** Log of every pipeline job run. analytics.etl_runs is the same table with the error cut to 500 characters.

- **Grain:** primary key `(id)`; one row per job run
- **Source:** etl.db.JobRun (own autocommit connection, so a failed job still leaves a 'failed' row)
- **Refresh:** one row per run
- **Coverage:** since 2026-11-04 UTC

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `id` | bigint | no | Run id. |
| `job` | text | no | Job name (run_daily, tracker_sync, meta_pull, lifetime_pull, status_refresh, build_metrics, upstack_daily, load_tags, load_hooks ...). |
| `started_at` | timestamptz | no | Start time. |
| `finished_at` | timestamptz | yes | Finish time (NULL while running). |
| `status` | text | no | running \| ok \| failed. |
| `data_through` | date | yes | Last data day covered, when the job has one. |
| `rows_written` | integer | yes | Rows written, when counted. |
| `stats` | jsonb | yes | Job-specific JSON. |
| `error` | text | yes | Scrubbed error text when failed. |

*Last 10 runs*

```sql
SELECT id, job, started_at, status, data_through FROM etl_runs ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 10;
```

### public.app_meta

*table*

**Purpose.** Key / value facts about the data: window starts, data-through dates, last refresh status, timezone note, tagging reliability.

- **Grain:** primary key `(key)`; one row per key
- **Source:** written by the ETL jobs (etl.db.set_meta)
- **Refresh:** updated by the jobs that own each key
- **Coverage:** 23 keys
- **Caveat:** Keys include daily_start (2026-05-06, never moves), data_through, lifetime_cutoff (legacy 2026-09-17), lifetime_pulled_at, metrics_built_at, status_refreshed_at, upstack_first_day / upstack_through / upstack_built_at / upstack_pulled_at, google_first_day / google_through / google_upstack_first_day / google_built_at, last_ok_at / refresh_status / last_error, tz_note, tracker_row_count / tracker_blank_family_count, and reliability:<product slug> (blind re-tag agreement as JSON).

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | text | no | Key (primary key). |
| `value` | text | no | Value as text (some are JSON). |
| `updated_at` | timestamptz | no | When the key was last written. |

*The facts the app reads about the data*

```sql
SELECT key, left(value, 80) AS value, updated_at FROM app_meta WHERE key NOT LIKE 'reliability:%' ORDER BY key;
```

### public.api_keys

*table*

**Purpose.** Scoped API keys for the Tag Explorer API: one row per key handed to a colleague. A key can read everything the app serves and can only assign items on the production board.

- **Grain:** primary key `(id)`; one row per key (id is the primary key; name and key_hash are each unique)
- **Source:** python -m etl.api_keys create / revoke (etl/api_keys.py); last_used_at is touched by the web app (app/api_keys.py)
- **Refresh:** on demand (a person creates or revokes a key; last_used_at moves at most once a minute while a key is in use)
- **Coverage:** from the day the migration was deployed
- **Caveat:** Only the sha256 hash of the key is stored. The key itself is shown once when it is made and cannot be recovered; make a new key and revoke the old one instead.
- **Caveat:** A revoked key is never deleted, so the history stays. Assignments made with a key carry created_by / updated_by 'key:<name>' in creative_priorities_assignments.

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `id` | bigint | no | Primary key (bigserial). |
| `name` | text | no | Short name of the key, for example the colleague it was given to (unique). Shown in the audit columns as key:<name>. |
| `key_hash` | text | no | sha256 hex digest of the full key (unique). The key is looked up by this hash. |
| `prefix` | text | no | First 8 characters of the key ('lvk_' plus 4), for display only. |
| `scope` | text | no | What the key may do. 'read_assign' (the only scope): every GET, plus POST /api/priorities/assignments and PATCH /api/priorities/assignments/<id>. |
| `created_at` | timestamptz | no | When the key was made. |
| `created_by` | text | yes | Operating-system user who ran the create command; NULL when unknown. |
| `last_used_at` | timestamptz | yes | When the key last made a request (updated at most once a minute); NULL while unused. |
| `revoked_at` | timestamptz | yes | When the key was revoked; NULL while active. A revoked key is refused with 401. |

### public.klaviyo_campaigns

*table*

**Purpose.** Dimension: one row per Klaviyo campaign (email and SMS) with its name, status, send time and subject. Names the parent ids of klaviyo_daily.

- **Grain:** primary key `(id)`; one row per campaign (id is the primary key; Klaviyo campaign id)
- **Source:** Klaviyo API GET /campaigns/ with campaign-messages, both channels, via python -m etl.klaviyo (etl/klaviyo.py)
- **Refresh:** nightly with the daily ETL (whole list re-upserted every run)
- **Coverage:** every campaign Klaviyo lists, regardless of the metric window
- **Caveat:** raw holds the Klaviyo attributes and the campaign's messages (id, label, subject); an A/B campaign has several messages and subject is the first one's.
- **Caveat:** A campaign that sent in the window but is no longer listed shows in klaviyo_daily as '(unknown campaign message ...)'.

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `id` | text | no | Klaviyo campaign id (primary key). |
| `name` | text | yes | Campaign name. |
| `channel` | text | no | 'email' or 'sms'. |
| `status` | text | yes | Klaviyo status text, for example Sent, Draft, Queued without Recipients. |
| `send_time` | timestamptz | yes | Scheduled send time (UTC); NULL when not scheduled. |
| `subject` | text | yes | Subject line of the first message; NULL for SMS and drafts without one. |
| `created_at` | timestamptz | yes | When the campaign was created in Klaviyo. |
| `updated_at` | timestamptz | yes | When the campaign was last edited in Klaviyo. |
| `raw` | jsonb | yes | Klaviyo attributes plus the campaign's messages, as JSON. |
| `loaded_at` | timestamptz | no | When this row was last written. |

### public.klaviyo_flows

*table*

**Purpose.** Dimension: one row per Klaviyo flow with its name, status and trigger type. Names the parent ids of klaviyo_daily.

- **Grain:** primary key `(id)`; one row per flow (id is the primary key; Klaviyo flow id)
- **Source:** Klaviyo API GET /flows/ via python -m etl.klaviyo (etl/klaviyo.py)
- **Refresh:** nightly with the daily ETL (whole list re-upserted every run)
- **Coverage:** every flow Klaviyo lists (draft, manual and live), regardless of the metric window
- **Caveat:** raw is the Klaviyo attribute object as returned.

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `id` | text | no | Klaviyo flow id (primary key). |
| `name` | text | yes | Flow name. |
| `status` | text | yes | draft, manual or live. |
| `trigger_type` | text | yes | What starts the flow, for example Metric, List, Segment, Date. |
| `created_at` | timestamptz | yes | When the flow was created. |
| `updated_at` | timestamptz | yes | When the flow was last edited. |
| `raw` | jsonb | yes | Klaviyo attributes as JSON. |
| `loaded_at` | timestamptz | no | When this row was last written. |

### public.klaviyo_flow_messages

*table*

**Purpose.** Dimension: one row per message (email or SMS step) inside a Klaviyo flow, with its name and subject. Names the message_id of klaviyo_daily flow rows.

- **Grain:** primary key `(id)`; one row per flow message (id is the primary key; Klaviyo flow message id)
- **Source:** Klaviyo API GET /flows/{id}/flow-actions/ then /flow-actions/{id}/flow-messages/ via python -m etl.klaviyo (etl/klaviyo.py)
- **Refresh:** nightly with the daily ETL, for live flows and flows that had activity in the pulled window
- **Coverage:** messages of every live flow and of flows with sends, opens, clicks or orders in any pulled window
- **Caveat:** Messages of draft or manual flows that were silent in every pulled window are not fetched, so they are missing here.
- **Caveat:** Messages deleted from a flow are not listed; their old metrics in klaviyo_daily keep the message_id with no name.

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `id` | text | no | Klaviyo flow message id (primary key). |
| `flow_id` | text | no | Parent flow (klaviyo_flows.id). No foreign key. |
| `name` | text | yes | Message name in the flow builder. |
| `channel` | text | yes | 'email' or 'sms' as Klaviyo reports it, lowercased. |
| `subject` | text | yes | Email subject line; NULL for SMS. |
| `raw` | jsonb | yes | Klaviyo attributes plus the flow action id, as JSON. |
| `loaded_at` | timestamptz | no | When this row was last written. |

### public.klaviyo_daily

*table*

**Purpose.** Klaviyo email and SMS performance by Pacific day, campaign or flow, message and channel: sends, delivered, opens, clicks, unsubscribes and Klaviyo-attributed orders and revenue.

- **Grain:** primary key `(day, source, parent_id, message_id, channel)`; one row per day (Pacific) x source ('campaign' | 'flow') x parent_id x message_id x channel (that is the primary key; message_id is '' where Klaviyo gives none)
- **Source:** Klaviyo metric-aggregates (Received / Bounced / Opened / Clicked / Marked as Spam / Unsubscribed events by $flow and $message, SMS equivalents, and Placed Order - Upstackified by $attributed_flow / $attributed_message) via python -m etl.klaviyo (etl/klaviyo.py)
- **Refresh:** nightly with the daily ETL; the trailing 14 complete Pacific days are replaced each run so late opens, clicks and orders land
- **Coverage:** complete Pacific days through yesterday; history from the first backfill (120 days at launch)
- **Caveat:** unique_opens and unique_clicks are per-day uniques: do not add them across days or messages; sums run 20-55% above Klaviyo's single-window uniques.
- **Caveat:** orders and revenue are Klaviyo-attributed conversions of the metric 'Placed Order - Upstackified' (S6xLbC, the CheckoutChamp / Upstack server feed), dated by the day the order happened, not the day the message was sent; Shopify's own Placed Order metric reads about 10% lower.
- **Caveat:** Clicks exclude clicks Klaviyo flags as bots. sends = delivered + bounced.
- **Caveat:** Klaviyo does not expose SMS unsubscribes per flow, and campaign SMS unsubscribes are matched by campaign name with no message (message_id ''); about 2K keyword-STOP unsubscribes have no campaign and are not in this table.
- **Caveat:** SMS rows have no opens, unique_opens or spam_complaints (0).
- **Caveat:** Sends, delivered and revenue reconcile within about 3% of Klaviyo's own campaign and flow value reports.

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `day` | date | no | Calendar day in America/Los_Angeles (event date). |
| `source` | text | no | 'campaign' or 'flow'. |
| `parent_id` | text | no | Klaviyo campaign id or flow id (klaviyo_campaigns.id / klaviyo_flows.id). No foreign key. |
| `message_id` | text | no | Klaviyo message id (campaign message or flow message); '' when the row has none (SMS unsubscribes by campaign name). |
| `channel` | text | no | 'email' or 'sms'. |
| `parent_name` | text | yes | Name of the campaign or flow when loaded (denormalised); '(unknown ...)' when Klaviyo no longer lists it. |
| `sends` | bigint | no | Messages Klaviyo tried to send that day (delivered plus bounced). |
| `delivered` | bigint | no | Messages delivered. |
| `bounced` | bigint | no | Emails bounced or SMS that failed to deliver. |
| `opens` | bigint | no | Email opens (includes Apple Mail Privacy Protection machine opens); 0 for SMS. |
| `unique_opens` | bigint | no | Distinct recipients who opened that day (per-day unique, not additive); 0 for SMS. |
| `clicks` | bigint | no | Clicks, bot clicks excluded. |
| `unique_clicks` | bigint | no | Distinct recipients who clicked that day (per-day unique, not additive). |
| `unsubscribes` | bigint | no | Unsubscribes counted that day (email: clicked-to-unsubscribe events; SMS: campaign-level only). |
| `spam_complaints` | bigint | no | Emails marked as spam; 0 for SMS. |
| `orders` | bigint | no | Orders Klaviyo attributes to the message, dated by order day. |
| `revenue` | numeric(14,2) | no | Revenue of those orders in dollars (Placed Order - Upstackified value), dated by order day. |
| `loaded_at` | timestamptz | no | When this row was last written. |

### public.klaviyo_message_content

*table*

**Purpose.** The copy of every Klaviyo message (campaign message or flow message) that had activity in a pulled window, or that sits in a live flow: subject, preview text, full email HTML, readable body text and the message links. Joins to klaviyo_daily on message_id.

- **Grain:** primary key `(message_id)`; one row per message (message_id is the primary key; Klaviyo campaign message id or flow message id)
- **Source:** Klaviyo API: GET /campaign-messages/{id}/template/ and /flow-messages/{id}/template/ (email HTML), the message definitions from GET /campaigns/ and /flow-actions/{id}/flow-messages/ (subject, preview, sender, SMS body), via python -m etl.klaviyo (etl/klaviyo.py)
- **Refresh:** nightly with the daily ETL, for messages with activity in the trailing 14 days and every message of a live flow
- **Coverage:** messages with activity in any pulled window (120 days at launch) plus live-flow messages; not every message Klaviyo has ever held
- **Caveat:** Only the latest copy is kept: when a message is edited the row is overwritten and fetched_at moves (content_hash changes); the history of edits is not kept, so old performance reads against the current copy.
- **Caveat:** Messages whose template Klaviyo no longer returns (deleted campaigns or templates) have a row with NULL html and body_text and raw.fetch_error set.
- **Caveat:** body_text is extracted from the HTML (head, scripts and styles dropped, link text kept, whitespace collapsed); image-only emails have little or no text, and image_count says how many real images the email carries. For SMS body_text is the message body and html is NULL.
- **Caveat:** image_count excludes tracking pixels and 1-pixel images.

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `message_id` | text | no | Klaviyo message id (primary key); equals klaviyo_daily.message_id. |
| `source` | text | no | 'campaign' or 'flow'. |
| `parent_id` | text | no | Campaign id or flow id of the message. No foreign key. |
| `channel` | text | no | 'email' or 'sms'. |
| `subject` | text | yes | Email subject line; NULL for SMS. |
| `preview_text` | text | yes | Email preview text; NULL for SMS or when none is set. |
| `from_name` | text | yes | Sender name of the email. |
| `from_email` | text | yes | Sender address of the email. |
| `template_id` | text | yes | Klaviyo template id the email renders from; NULL for SMS or when the template was not returned. |
| `editor_type` | text | yes | Klaviyo editor of the template: SIMPLE (plain text email), CODE, DRAG_AND_DROP, USER_DRAFTED; NULL for SMS. |
| `html` | text | yes | Full template HTML of the email; NULL for SMS or when not returned. |
| `body_text` | text | yes | Readable plain text of the email (or the SMS body); NULL when nothing could be fetched or the email is images only. |
| `image_count` | integer | yes | Real images in the email (tracking pixels excluded); 1 for an SMS with a picture. |
| `link_urls` | text[] | yes | Distinct http(s) links in the message, in order of appearance. |
| `content_hash` | text | yes | sha256 of the HTML (email) or body (SMS); changes when the message is edited. |
| `fetched_at` | timestamptz | no | When the content last changed in our copy (moves only when content_hash changes). |
| `raw` | jsonb | yes | The message definition as Klaviyo returns it (without the body / html), plus fetch_error when the template could not be fetched. |

### public.klaviyo_message_performance

*view*

**Purpose.** One row per Klaviyo message with its copy (subject, preview text, body text) and its totals and rates over everything in klaviyo_daily: the table to read which copy earns.

- **Grain:** one row per message_id (campaign or flow message with at least one klaviyo_daily row)
- **Source:** klaviyo_daily (grouped by message_id) joined to klaviyo_message_content
- **Refresh:** live (a view over klaviyo_daily; moves with the nightly Klaviyo load)
- **Coverage:** the days loaded in klaviyo_daily (120 days at launch, then trailing); first_send_day is the first loaded day with sends, not the message's real first send
- **Caveat:** unique_opens_daily_sum and unique_clicks_daily_sum are sums of per-day uniques, not window uniques: a person who opens on two days counts twice. open_rate_daily_uniques and click_rate_daily_uniques are built on them and read a little high; use them to compare messages, not as Klaviyo's reported rates.
- **Caveat:** Orders and revenue are Klaviyo-attributed conversions of 'Placed Order - Upstackified', dated by the order day, so a message can show revenue with no sends when it sent before the loaded window.
- **Caveat:** Rates are NULL when delivered is 0. SMS messages have no opens. Messages without fetched copy have NULL subject / body_text and has_copy false.
- **Caveat:** SMS campaign unsubscribes are matched to the campaign, not the message, so they are not in this view.

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `message_id` | text |  | Klaviyo message id. |
| `source` | text |  | 'campaign' or 'flow'. |
| `parent_id` | text |  | Campaign id or flow id. |
| `channel` | text |  | 'email' or 'sms'. |
| `parent_name` | text |  | Campaign or flow name. |
| `subject` | text |  | Email subject (current copy); NULL for SMS or when not fetched. |
| `preview_text` | text |  | Email preview text (current copy). |
| `body_text` | text |  | Readable body text (current copy). |
| `image_count` | integer |  | Real images in the email. |
| `editor_type` | text |  | Klaviyo template editor type. |
| `first_send_day` | date |  | First loaded day with sends. |
| `last_send_day` | date |  | Last loaded day with sends. |
| `sends` | bigint |  | Sum of sends. |
| `delivered` | bigint |  | Sum of delivered. |
| `bounced` | bigint |  | Sum of bounced or failed. |
| `opens` | bigint |  | Sum of opens (includes machine opens). |
| `unique_opens_daily_sum` | bigint |  | Sum of per-day unique opens (not a window unique). |
| `clicks` | bigint |  | Sum of clicks, bots excluded. |
| `unique_clicks_daily_sum` | bigint |  | Sum of per-day unique clicks (not a window unique). |
| `unsubscribes` | bigint |  | Sum of unsubscribes. |
| `spam_complaints` | bigint |  | Sum of spam complaints. |
| `orders` | bigint |  | Sum of attributed orders. |
| `revenue` | numeric(14,2) |  | Sum of attributed revenue in dollars. |
| `open_rate_daily_uniques` | numeric |  | unique_opens_daily_sum / delivered; NULL when delivered is 0. |
| `click_rate_daily_uniques` | numeric |  | unique_clicks_daily_sum / delivered; NULL when delivered is 0. |
| `orders_per_delivered` | numeric |  | orders / delivered; NULL when delivered is 0. |
| `revenue_per_delivered` | numeric |  | revenue / delivered in dollars; NULL when delivered is 0. |
| `has_copy` | boolean |  | True when body_text was fetched for the message. |

### public.landing_page_ad_checks

*table*

**Purpose.** Ads that etl/landing_pages.py has already looked at, so an ad with no readable link is not asked about every night.

- **Grain:** primary key `(ad_id)`; one row per ad (ad_id is the primary key)
- **Source:** etl/landing_pages.py
- **Refresh:** each run of python -m etl.landing_pages
- **Coverage:** ads with spend in the window of the runs
- **Caveat:** An ad with result no_link or error is looked at again 14 days after checked_at.

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `ad_id` | text | no | Meta ad id (primary key). |
| `checked_at` | timestamptz | no | When the ad was last looked at. |
| `result` | text | no | 'linked' (a landing page was found), 'no_link' (none could be read) or 'error' (Meta returned an error). |
| `detail` | text | yes | Short reason for no_link or error; NULL when linked. |

### public.brain_keys

*table*

**Purpose.** Agent keys for the hosted brain endpoint (/mcp). With sign-in on: one row per user who made a key on the Connect an agent page (a reset replaces it). With the app open (AUTH_DISABLED): one row per key anyone made, with no user. A brain key opens /mcp and nothing else.

- **Grain:** primary key `(id)`; one row per key (id is the primary key; key_hash is unique; username is unique and NULL for open-app keys)
- **Source:** the Connect an agent page (POST /api/brain/key in app/brain_mcp.py); last_used_at is touched by /mcp
- **Refresh:** on demand (a person makes a key; last_used_at moves at most once a minute while the key is in use)
- **Coverage:** from the day migration 220 was deployed; open-app keys from migration 221
- **Caveat:** Only the sha256 hash of the key is stored. The key is shown once and cannot be recovered; a signed-in user resets it to get a new one (the old one stops working at once). Open-app keys cannot be reset, only made again.
- **Caveat:** Separate from api_keys: a brain key never works on any /api/ route, and an api_keys key also works on /mcp.
- **Caveat:** Open-app keys (created_by = 'open-app') are capped at 50 per day for the whole app and keep only a salted hash of the creator's IP, never the IP.

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `id` | bigint | no | Primary key (bigserial). |
| `username` | text | yes | App login of the owner (a name from APP_USERS), unique: one key per user. NULL for open-app keys. |
| `key_hash` | text | no | sha256 hex digest of the full key (unique); the key is looked up by this hash. |
| `prefix` | text | no | First 8 characters of the key ('lvb_' plus 4), for display only. |
| `created_at` | timestamptz | no | When the key was made or last reset. |
| `last_used_at` | timestamptz | yes | When the key last made a /mcp request (at most once a minute); NULL while unused. |
| `created_by` | text | no | How the key was made: 'signed-in' (a logged-in user, username is set) or 'open-app' (made while AUTH_DISABLED was on, username is NULL). |
| `creator_hash` | text | yes | For open-app keys: HMAC-SHA256 of the creator's IP address under the app's secret key (first 32 hex characters); never the IP itself. NULL for signed-in keys. |

### public.brain_audit

*table*

**Purpose.** Audit log of the hosted brain endpoint: one row per tool call (who, which tool, how long, how many rows). Never holds the question, the SQL or the result.

- **Grain:** primary key `(id)`; one row per tool call (id is the primary key)
- **Source:** app/brain_mcp.py, written after every tools/call on /mcp
- **Refresh:** live (one row per call)
- **Coverage:** from the day migration 220 was deployed; no pruning
- **Caveat:** actor is the login name for a signed-in session, 'brain:<username>' for a brain key, 'brain:open-app:<key prefix>' for a key made while the app was open, 'key:<name>' for an API key.
- **Caveat:** row_count is NULL for tools that return no row list (describe, app_map).

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `id` | bigint | no | Primary key (bigserial). |
| `at` | timestamptz | no | When the call finished. |
| `actor` | text | no | Who called: a login name, 'brain:<username>' (brain key), 'brain:open-app:<prefix>' (open-app brain key) or 'key:<name>' (API key). |
| `tool` | text | no | Tool name: catalog, describe, search, metric, freshness, sql, past_questions or app_map. |
| `duration_ms` | integer | no | How long the tool ran, in milliseconds. |
| `row_count` | integer | yes | Rows (or hits) returned; NULL when the tool returns no list. |
| `ok` | boolean | no | True when the tool answered, false when it failed or the input was rejected. |

## Projection pacing feeds (daily actuals read by the Projections module; sheet-fed today, direct feeds in shadow)

### public.proj_feed_config

*table*

**Purpose.** Per-feed mode switch for the dual-run: which source the app reads for each feed.

- **Grain:** primary key `(feed)`; one row per feed
- **Source:** python -m etl.feeds mode <feed> <mode>
- **Refresh:** on change
- **Coverage:** 14 feeds, all 'shadow' on 2026-11-04
- **Caveat:** 'sheet' = the sheet raw tabs feed the proj_feed_* table (today's behaviour); 'shadow' = the sheet stays authoritative, the direct pull is written to proj_feedx_* and compared; 'direct' = the direct pull writes the main proj_feed_* table, the sheet read is the fallback. No row = 'sheet'.

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `feed` | text | no | Feed key (orders, amazon, platform, attribution, traffic, meta_reach, product_reach, nc_product, anyclick, bids, units_revamp, units_amazon, units_intl, stock_revamp). |
| `mode` | text | no | 'sheet' \| 'shadow' \| 'direct'. |
| `changed_at` | timestamptz | no | When the mode was set. |
| `changed_by` | text | yes | Who set it. |
| `note` | text | yes | Free note (<= 300 characters). |

### public.proj_feed_runs

*table*

**Purpose.** One row per pacing feed: what was last loaded and through which day (feeds the freshness chips).

- **Grain:** primary key `(feed)`; one row per feed
- **Source:** etl.projection_pacing
- **Refresh:** nightly
- **Coverage:** 12 feeds

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `feed` | text | no | Feed key (primary key). |
| `source` | text | no | 'sheet:<tab>' \| 'ad_daily' \| 'snapshot'. |
| `loaded_at` | timestamptz | no | When last loaded. |
| `rows_loaded` | integer | no | Rows of the last load. |
| `first_day` | date | yes | First day loaded. |
| `last_day` | date | yes | Last day loaded. |
| `note` | text | yes | Free note. |

### public.proj_feed_attempts

*table*

**Purpose.** One row per direct pull attempt of a feed (any mode).

- **Grain:** primary key `(id)`; one row per attempt
- **Source:** etl.feeds run
- **Refresh:** each feeds run (21:00 UTC)
- **Coverage:** since 2026-11-04

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `id` | bigint | no | Primary key. |
| `feed` | text | no | Feed key. |
| `mode` | text | no | Mode at the time. |
| `source` | text | no | 'api:upstack' \| 'api:warehance' \| 'api:meta' \| 'api:expandfi' \| 'sheet:<tab>'. |
| `started_at` | timestamptz | no | Start. |
| `finished_at` | timestamptz | no | Finish. |
| `status` | text | no | ok \| failed \| fallback \| skipped. |
| `rows` | integer | yes | Rows pulled. |
| `first_day` | date | yes | First day of the pull. |
| `last_day` | date | yes | Last day of the pull. |
| `note` | text | yes | Scrubbed note, <= 300 characters. |

| `units` | numeric(18,2) | no | Units. |
| `revenue` | numeric(18,2) | no | Revenue. |
| `nc_orders` | numeric(18,2) | no | New-customer orders. |
| `nc_rev` | numeric(18,2) | no | New-customer revenue. |
| `ad_spend` | numeric(18,2) | no | Amazon Ads spend (deduped). |
| `attributed_sales` | numeric(18,2) | no | Sales Amazon attributes to ads. |
| `clicks` | numeric(18,2) | no | Ad clicks. |
| `impressions` | numeric(18,2) | no | Ad impressions. |
| `loaded_at` | timestamptz | no | When loaded. |

### public.proj_feed_platform

*table*

**Purpose.** Ad-platform spend and platform-reported conversions per day for Meta and Google (Upstack ad-platform feed): the longest Meta + Google spend history in the database.

- **Grain:** primary key `(day, platform)`; one row per (day, platform)
- **Source:** the Lonvera Projections Google Sheet tab 'Platform - History' (read by etl.projection_pacing); the direct feed reads Upstack /api/query-channel-details at platform grain
- **Refresh:** nightly (etl service 13:00 UTC via etl.projection_daily): the trailing 14 days are replaced from the sheet tab; the `feeds` service (21:00 UTC) writes the shadow twin proj_feedx_* from the source API and compares
- **Coverage:** 2025-01-01 through yesterday; platform values 'Meta (Facebook/Instagram)' and 'Google Ads'
- **Caveat:** platform labels differ from analytics.platform_daily ('meta' / 'google'). Spend reconciles with ad_daily and google_daily to the cent on every day they share.
- **Caveat:** Purchases and values are platform-reported and overlap across platforms.

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `day` | date | no | Day. |
| `platform` | text | no | 'Meta (Facebook/Instagram)' \| 'Google Ads' (TikTok has no rows). |
| `spend` | numeric(18,2) | no | Spend, dollars. |
| `purchases` | numeric(18,2) | no | Platform-reported purchases. |
| `purchase_value` | numeric(18,2) | no | Platform-reported conversion value. |
| `nc_purchases` | numeric(18,2) | no | Platform-reported new-customer purchases (0 for Google). |
| `nc_purchase_value` | numeric(18,2) | no | Their value. |
| `link_clicks` | numeric(18,2) | no | Link clicks. |
| `clicks` | numeric(18,2) | no | All clicks. |
| `loaded_at` | timestamptz | no | When loaded. |

*Meta and Google spend per day since 2025 (the longest history)*

```sql
SELECT day, platform, round(spend, 2) AS spend FROM proj_feed_platform WHERE day >= current_date - 14 ORDER BY day DESC, platform;
```

### public.proj_feed_product_reach

*table*

**Purpose.** Unique outbound clicks per product, de-duplicated across the product's ad sets, per grain. The only per-product reach-like metric.

- **Grain:** primary key `(grain, period_key, product)`; one row per (grain, period_key, product)
- **Source:** the Lonvera Projections Google Sheet tab 'Meta Product - Reach' (read by etl.projection_pacing)
- **Refresh:** nightly (etl service 13:00 UTC via etl.projection_daily): the trailing 14 days are replaced from the sheet tab; the `feeds` service (21:00 UTC) writes the shadow twin proj_feedx_* from the source API and compares
- **Coverage:** from 2026-07-05 (Meta serves filtered unique clicks about 120 days back); earlier periods are absent, never re-pulled
- **Caveat:** Reach and frequency per product are NOT stored: only unique outbound clicks.

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `grain` | text | no | 'day' \| 'week' \| 'month'. |
| `period_key` | text | no | Period key. |
| `period_start` | date | no | First day. |
| `period_end` | date | no | Last day. |
| `product` | text | no | VitaBull \| Beef Liver \| Boneflex \| Other. |
| `unique_outbound_clicks` | numeric(18,2) | no | Unique outbound clicks de-duplicated across the product's ad sets. |
| `loaded_at` | timestamptz | no | When loaded. |

*Weekly unique outbound clicks per product*

```sql
SELECT period_key, product, unique_outbound_clicks FROM proj_feed_product_reach WHERE grain = 'week' ORDER BY period_start DESC, product LIMIT 12;
```

### public.proj_feed_nc_product

*table*

**Purpose.** ACTUAL Shopify first-order revenue and new customers per Pacific day by the order's largest product line (not Meta-attributed). The only product split of actual new-customer revenue.

- **Grain:** primary key `(day, product)`; one row per (day, product)
- **Source:** the Lonvera Projections Google Sheet tab 'NC Rev - By Product' (read by etl.projection_pacing); the direct feed reads ExpandFi merchant 1452 shopify_orders
- **Refresh:** nightly (etl service 13:00 UTC via etl.projection_daily): the trailing 14 days are replaced from the sheet tab; the `feeds` service (21:00 UTC) writes the shadow twin proj_feedx_* from the source API and compares
- **Coverage:** 2025-01-01 through today; product = VitaBull | Beef Liver | Boneflex | Other
- **Caveat:** Exposed as analytics.orders_by_product_daily. Revenue = order total_price split across product lines by net line price; nc_orders = orders whose largest product line is the product.

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `day` | date | no | Pacific day. |
| `product` | text | no | VitaBull \| Beef Liver \| Boneflex \| Other. |
| `nc_rev` | numeric(18,2) | no | First-order revenue, dollars. |
| `nc_orders` | numeric(18,2) | no | First orders. |
| `loaded_at` | timestamptz | no | When loaded. |

*Actual new-customer revenue by product, last 14 days*

```sql
SELECT product, round(sum(nc_rev)) AS nc_revenue, sum(nc_orders) AS new_customers FROM proj_feed_nc_product WHERE day >= current_date - 14 GROUP BY 1 ORDER BY 2 DESC;
```

| `is_estimate` | boolean | no | true = modeled. |
| `loaded_at` | timestamptz | no | When loaded. |

*Examples: same shape as `public.proj_units_daily`; query it the same way.*

- **Refresh:** nightly

| `product` | text | no | Product key. |
| `as_of` | date | no | Snapshot date. |
| `on_hand` | numeric(18,2) | no | Units on hand. |
| `allocated` | numeric(18,2) | no | Units allocated to open orders. |
| `source` | text | no | Where the count came from. |
| `loaded_at` | timestamptz | no | When loaded. |

### public.proj_feedx_intl_nonus_daily

*table*

**Purpose.** Daily units on Warehance orders shipped to a non-US address (any Warehance status: Shopify routes them to the overseas warehouse, Warehance auto-cancels them), by Pacific order date, product and country.

- **Grain:** primary key `(day, product, country)`; one row per (day, product, country)
- **Source:** etl.feeds intl_estimate (Warehance API); no sheet tab carries it
- **Refresh:** feeds service 21:00 UTC
- **Coverage:** about 488 rows
- **Caveat:** Raw bottle counts, not yet scaled by the ratio lever. Aggregates only.

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `day` | date | no | Pacific order day. |
| `product` | text | no | Product key. |
| `country` | text | no | ISO 3166 alpha-2, upper case. |
| `units_raw` | numeric(18,2) | no | Raw non-US bottles. |
| `loaded_at` | timestamptz | no | When loaded. |

### public.proj_feed_nc_hourly

*table*

**Purpose.** New-customer revenue and first orders by Pacific day and clock hour (Shopify first orders from the ExpandFi warehouse); the hourly share curve behind the 'NC revenue today' card on Projections > Pacing.

- **Grain:** primary key `(day, hour)`; one row per (day, hour) with at least one first order
- **Source:** etl.feeds nc_hourly (ExpandFi warehouse shopify_orders); no sheet tab carries it
- **Refresh:** feeds service 21:00 UTC, trailing 35 closed days
- **Coverage:** from the first pull (60-day backfill once)
- **Caveat:** Sparse: an hour with no first order has no row. Today is never stored. The 25-hour fall-back day merges both 1 a.m. hours into hour 1. Order totals include tax and are before refunds, like upstack_orders_daily.nc_rev; day sums equal proj_feed_nc_product. Aggregates only.

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `day` | date | no | Pacific day of the first order. |
| `hour` | smallint | no | Pacific wall-clock hour 0-23. |
| `nc_rev` | numeric(18,2) | no | Sum of order total_price of the first orders in that hour (tax included, before refunds); test orders and orders without priced lines excluded. |
| `nc_orders` | integer | no | Number of first orders in that hour. |
| `loaded_at` | timestamptz | no | When loaded. |

### public.proj_live_today_snapshots

*table*

**Purpose.** Today's Meta spend, Google spend, new-customer revenue and new customers so far, one snapshot per hourly pull from Upstack; fills the 'Meta spend today' and 'NC revenue today' cards on Projections > Pacing.

- **Grain:** primary key `(pulled_at)`; one row per pull (pulled_at, to the minute)
- **Source:** etl.live_today (Upstack /api/query and /api/query-channel-details for the Pacific day so far); no sheet tab carries it
- **Refresh:** Railway cron `live`, every hour on the hour
- **Coverage:** from the first pull; history kept 90 days
- **Caveat:** Upstack's spend for the current day trails the ad platforms by some minutes, so the figures are a floor of what has been spent and sold. Same measures as upstack_orders_daily (nc_rev = orders.new_customer_total_revenue, tax included, before refunds), so a closed day ties. Aggregates only.

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `pulled_at` | timestamptz | no | When Upstack was asked, to the minute (UTC). |
| `day` | date | no | Pacific day the numbers belong to. |
| `pacific_time` | time without time zone | no | Pacific wall-clock time of the pull, to the minute. |
| `meta_spend` | numeric(18,2) | no | Meta (Facebook / Instagram) spend so far that day, dollars, all ad accounts Upstack sees. |
| `google_spend` | numeric(18,2) | no | Google Ads spend so far that day, dollars. |
| `total_spend` | numeric(18,2) | no | Spend so far across every ad platform Upstack reports (Meta, Google, TikTok), dollars. |
| `nc_rev` | numeric(18,2) | no | New-customer revenue so far that day, dollars (orders.new_customer_total_revenue). |
| `nc_orders` | integer | no | New customers (first orders) so far that day. |

### public.proj_amazon_mtd_snapshots

*table*

**Purpose.** Completed Amazon month-to-date revenue, one snapshot per Pacific day and month: reported days plus the missing days projected at the trailing 7-day average of the reported days; read by Projections > Pacing instead of computing on every page load.

- **Grain:** primary key `(snapshot_day, month)`; one row per (snapshot_day, month)
- **Source:** proj_feed_amazon.revenue through etl.amazon_mtd
- **Refresh:** after the Amazon feed load in the feeds service (21:00 UTC) and again in the etl service (13:00 UTC); a same-day re-run replaces the row
- **Coverage:** from the first run; history kept so each day's projection can be compared with the final month
- **Caveat:** projected_mtd is an estimate, never an actual; a later feed load restates days (the page then computes live); Amazon revenue is total_amount_usd by purchase day, Amazon.com plus B2B. Aggregates only.

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `snapshot_day` | date | no | Pacific day of the run. |
| `month` | date | no | First day of the month that was completed. |
| `computed_at` | timestamptz | no | When the run computed it (UTC). |
| `data_through` | date | no | Last day that should have been complete at the run (yesterday by default). |
| `last_actual_day` | date | yes | Last day of the month with reported Amazon revenue; null before the first reported day. |
| `days_actual` | integer | no | Calendar days from the 1st through the last reported day. |
| `days_projected` | integer | no | Days after the last reported day, through data_through, that were projected (0 when the feed is current or stale). |
| `daily_rate` | numeric(18,2) | yes | Dollars per projected day: mean of the last 7 reported days with revenue above zero (fewer when fewer exist). |
| `actual_mtd` | numeric(18,2) | no | Reported Amazon revenue from the 1st through the last reported day, dollars. |
| `projected_mtd` | numeric(18,2) | no | actual_mtd plus the projected days plus any partial-day top-up, dollars. |
| `status` | text | no | complete (nothing to project), projected, stale (feed more than 5 days behind: nothing projected), no_rate (no revenue in the feed), no_days (month has no complete day). |
| `method` | text | no | Rule and version that made the row, e.g. trailing-7-available/v1. |
| `flags` | text[] | no | Plain flags: feed_stale, short_window, window_has_gaps, gap_days_inside_month, newest_day_partial, no_data. |
| `projected_days` | jsonb | no | The projected days as json: [{day, revenue}], so a reader can rebuild the completed month without recomputing. |
| `partial_topup` | numeric(18,2) | no | Dollars added because the newest reported day looked partial (below half the days before it); 0 otherwise. |
| `lag_days` | integer | yes | Days between data_through and the last reported day. |
| `rate_source` | text | yes | trailing_7_available, or previous_month_last_7 when the month has no reported day yet. |
| `note` | text | yes | The plain-language line the page shows. |

### public.proj_feedx_intl_estimate

*view*

**Purpose.** The overseas-3PL units estimate: raw non-US units x the product's ratio lever (else intl_ratio.default), flagged estimated.

- **Grain:** one row per (day, product)
- **Source:** proj_feedx_intl_nonus_daily x proj_feed_levers
- **Refresh:** live view
- **Coverage:** as the raw table
- **Caveat:** Half-up rounding at (day, product); the python job rounds half-to-even, so a tie can differ by one unit from proj_feedx_units_daily.

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `day` | date |  | Pacific day. |
| `product` | text |  | Product key. |
| `units_raw` | numeric |  | Raw non-US units. |
| `factor` | numeric(12,6) |  | Ratio applied. |
| `units` | numeric |  | Estimated overseas-3PL units. |
| `estimated` | boolean |  | Always true. |

### public.proj_meta_spend_monthly

*view*

**Purpose.** Monthly Meta spend from ad_daily, read by the projection drivers module.

- **Grain:** one row per month
- **Source:** ad_daily
- **Refresh:** live view
- **Coverage:** 2026-05 onward
- **Caveat:** A month is complete when days = the days in the month.

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `month` | date |  | First day of the month. |
| `spend` | numeric(18,2) |  | Meta spend, dollars. |
| `days` | integer |  | Days with any ad_daily row. |
| `last_day` | date |  | Last day with data. |

## Projection engine tables (finance, inventory, cohorts; read them through the analytics views)

### public.proj_scenarios

*table*

**Purpose.** Named lever sets (scenarios) of the Projections module. Projection engine (finance / planning); read it through the analytics projection_* / inventory_* / cohorts_* views, never the raw table.

- **Grain:** primary key `(id)`; one row per scenario
- **Source:** the Projections app
- **Refresh:** on edit
- **Coverage:** 7 scenarios incl. the default 'current'
- **Caveat:** params holds the module levers (financing, drivers, cohorts...): finance data.

| Column | Type | Null | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `id` | integer | no | Primary key. |
| `slug` | text | no | Unique slug. |
| `name` | text | no | Name. |
| `base_slug` | text | yes | Fallback scenario for series cells this one does not hold. |
| `is_default` | boolean | no | true for the plan of record. |
| `params` | jsonb | no | params[<module>] = that module's levers (jsonb). |
| `version` | integer | no | +1 on every change. |
| `created_at` | timestamptz | no | Created. |
| `updated_at` | timestamptz | no | Updated. |
| `frozen` | boolean | no | true = a frozen clone that no longer reads its base. |
| `archived_at` | timestamptz | yes | Set when deleted: kept 30 days. |

### public.proj_series

*table*

| `line_key` | text | no | Series key. |
| `month` | date | no | First of the month. |
| `value` | numeric(18,4) | no | Value. |
| `kind` | text | no | input \| override. |
| `note` | text | yes | Note. |
| `updated_at` | timestamptz | no | When changed. |

- **Source:** the Projections app (manual and auto-plan)
- **Refresh:** on edit
- **Coverage:** 74 rows
- **Caveat:** Money (unit_cost) and vendor terms: finance. Exposed as analytics.inventory_purchase_orders (the link column is left out).
